### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-05 03:18 UTC

## 一、第 2 套圖（圖 4–8）的風格分析

先講一個關鍵差異：第 1 套是**正面平視的橫向立面**（橫向卷軸背景的構圖），第 2 套全是**透視、左右對稱、中間一個大門洞**的構圖。所以第 2 套只能借**風格、材質和造型元素**，構圖一律不能跟著改，否則 AI 會把你的牆面改成透視走廊。

| 層 | 第 2 套的特徵 |
|---|---|
| 定位 | 3D 手繪風格的暗黑奇幻地獄要塞／熔岩地城，遊戲用美術 |
| 色彩 | 主色是偏暖的炭灰石（陰影帶紫褐色，不是純黑）。點綴色是熔岩橘黃（中心亮黃、外圍橘紅），天空是暗紅色 |
| 光 | **場景裡沒有天光，只有會發光的東西在照**：熔岩、火盆、柱上的小發光窗。石頭朝向熔岩的那一面有橘色輪廓光，其餘沉在暗處 |
| 材質 | 大塊石磚，倒角邊緣有缺口，邊緣用手繪高光；鍛鐵（鐵鍊、支架）；牛角／尖刺；地板裂縫透出熔岩細紋 |
| 母題 | ① 柱頂的牛角形火盆 ② 柱身的發光小龕窗 ③ 柱間垂掛的粗鐵鍊 ④ 彎角尖刺裝飾 ⑤ 拱門裡的熔岩瀑布 ⑥ 地面熔岩裂縫 ⑦ 柱腳的碎石堆 ⑧ 煙、火星、飛石、閃電 ⑨ 遠景的火山和要塞剪影 |

⑧ 煙、火星、飛石、閃電是會動的特效，**不要烙進背景圖**，之後在引擎裡用粒子做。烙進去就變成靜止的煙，也不能循環播放。

## 二、第 1 套的元素要換成什麼

| 原圖（圖 1） | 熔岩版 |
|---|---|
| 藍色磚牆、肋拱天花板 | 炭灰大塊石磚，陰影帶紫褐，底部受熔岩光往上照 |
| 上層藍色彩窗 | 保留窗花（tracery）造型，玻璃改成透出暗紅天空／餘燼的光 |
| 下層彩窗 | 窗花不動，後面改成熔岩橘光（類似圖 4 側門的效果） |
| 右邊的深色大拱門 | 熔岩瀑布（圖 4 的母題，屬於新增內容，要在 prompt 裡明確允許） |
| 陽台欄杆 | 暗色鍛鐵 |
| 左邊的壁燈 | 小火盆 |
| 牆腳 | 一條熔岩細裂縫 |
| **桃紅柱 ×6 + 窗上的小角** | 圖 4／圖 7／圖 8 那種石塊柱：牛角支架、火盆、發光龕窗；窗上的小角換成彎角尖刺 |

## 三、建議流程：分兩個 pass，一個 pass 只做一件事

不要一次同時換主題又換柱子。之前的案例反覆證明，兩件事放同一個 pass 會互相搶，結果兩邊都做不好。

```
Step 0  前處理
Step 1  整張主題翻譯（Pass 1）：只換材質、色和光，柱子先不處理
Step 2  柱子換造型（Pass 2）：沿用 B8 的紅圈定位法，把灰盒柱子貼到 Pass 1 的結果上當結構
Step 3  PS 合成和收尾：遮罩只取柱子貼回，調色交給 PS
```

**為什麼柱子放在後面做：** 柱子要吃到熔岩光才會融進場景。先做柱子再換主題，柱子會在 Pass 1 被重畫一次，造型會漂掉。

### Step 0 — 前處理

1. **底圖用沒有新柱子的乾淨原圖**。桃紅色的平面剪影會被 AI 當成桃紅色的柱子來畫。
2. 裁掉 viewport 的灰邊、上方黑條和左下角的黑塊，圖要滿版、不能有 letterbox。
3. **參考圖要裁切**（B9 的教訓：帶完整構圖的參考圖會把構圖一起帶進來）：
   - 風格參考：從圖 7 和圖 4 各裁一塊**只有石牆和柱子的特寫**，不要帶到中間的門洞。
   - 柱子設計參考：從圖 4 裁右邊那根柱（火盆＋龕窗＋石塊），從圖 8 裁一根帶牛角支架的柱。
4. Leonardo 設定：Nano Banana 2，Prompt Enhance 選 None，Style 選 None，輸出比例跟輸入一樣。

### Step 1 — Pass 1：整張主題翻譯（逐字 prompt）

```text
Two reference images. IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE: a flat, front-facing
orthographic elevation of a gothic cathedral interior wall for a
side-scrolling game — ribbed vaults, three tall upper windows with a
balcony railing, lower arched windows, relief panels and a large dark
archway on the right.
IMAGE 2 = STYLE & MATERIAL REFERENCE ONLY — take its stone material,
color palette and lava lighting; IGNORE its composition and
perspective: do NOT copy its central portal, distant volcano,
fortress silhouettes, smoke, flying rocks or lightning.

THEME TRANSLATION to a hellish lava fortress:
- All blue brick becomes heavy charcoal stone blocks with chipped
  bevelled edges and hand-painted edge highlights; shadows are warm
  purple-brown, never pure black.
- There is no sky light. The scene is lit ONLY by lava glow from
  below and behind: warm orange rim light on stone edges, darker
  toward the ceiling vaults.
- Upper windows: keep the exact tracery, the glass now glows deep
  ember red. Lower windows: keep the tracery, molten orange glow
  behind it.
- The large dark archway on the right becomes a glowing lavafall.
- Railing and metal parts become dark forged iron.
- A thin line of glowing lava cracks along the base of the wall.

GEOMETRY LOCK: flat orthographic front view — no perspective, no
vanishing point. Every arch, window, pilaster, railing and panel
keeps its EXACT position, size and silhouette from IMAGE 1. Do not
add pillars, chains or statues. Do not crop, zoom or extend.
MATERIAL & LIGHTING pass — not a redesign.
```

驗收：把結果用 50% 透明度疊回原圖，拱、窗、欄杆要能對齊。如果整張被改成透視，就加強 `orthographic` 那句，參考圖再裁得更緊。

### Step 2 — Pass 2：柱子換造型

**輸入圖：** 把 Pass 1 的結果放進 PS，在圖 2 的位置貼上**白色灰盒柱**當 BASE。這裡用白灰盒、不用桃紅剪影，因為灰盒帶有體積和支架高度的資訊，等於 B2「貼材質灰盒」的做法。桃紅那層留著，Step 3 拿來當遮罩。

**參考圖：** 柱子設計參考（Step 0 裁好的特寫）。

```text
IMAGE 1 is the BASE — a finished lava-fortress wall. It contains six
plain light-grey greybox pillars and small grey horn shapes above
three windows. These grey shapes are PLACEHOLDERS: they define the
exact position, width, height and bracket heights of each element.
IMAGE 2 is a DESIGN REFERENCE for the pillar style only.

TASK — repaint every grey placeholder as a hell-fortress pillar in
the style of IMAGE 2:
- Shafts of stacked heavy charcoal stone blocks with chipped edges.
- Where a greybox bracket exists, paint a dark forged-iron horned
  brazier bowl with fire on it; pillars without brackets stay
  plain stone with one small glowing slit niche.
- The small horn shapes above the windows become curved dark horns
  / spikes with an ember-lit tip.
Each pillar keeps its own variation as in the greybox — not six
identical copies.

INTEGRATION: light the pillars with the scene's existing lava glow —
orange rim on the edges facing the lava, deep shade elsewhere.
Brazier fires cast a soft warm glow on the nearby wall.

LOCKS: each pillar stays exactly inside its placeholder silhouette —
same position, width and height; flat orthographic front view.
Everything outside the placeholders stays pixel-identical. No chains,
statues or rubble unless they are inside a placeholder.
```

需要柱間垂鐵鍊的話，**等柱子定案後另外開一個小 pass**，在圖上用紅線畫出鐵鍊垂掛的路徑當定位圖（B8 的紅圈定位法）。

### Step 3 — PS 合成和收尾

1. **用桃紅那層當遮罩**，只取 Pass 2 的柱子貼回 Pass 1。NB2 會重繪整張圖，牆面會微漂，所以這步不是選配（B8 的教訓）。
2. 火盆的火和熔岩瀑布建議拆成獨立圖層，進引擎做動態或 bloom。
3. 調色一律在 PS 做（之前實測用 prompt 改曝光、改調色幾乎無效）：Curves 壓暗中間調 → 提高飽和 → 陰影推紫褐 → 熔岩區疊 Screen 加光暈。
4. 煙、火星、飛石用引擎粒子處理，不要烙進圖。

## 四、開跑前想跟你確認兩件事

1. **這張要不要投影回 3D 場景？** 圖 3 看起來是 3D 灰盒。如果要投影，像素對位就是硬需求，Step 3 的遮罩合成要嚴格做。如果只是 2D 背景圖，可以放寬一點。
2. **右邊大拱門要不要改成熔岩瀑布？** 這是新增的內容。不要的話，就把 Pass 1 裡那一行刪掉。

如果要，我可以把這份整理成案例 **B14**（`docs/asset-cases/B14-lava-fortress-theme.md`，裡面放風格 DNA、母題清單和兩個 pass 的逐字 prompt），等你實跑後再把結果和踩坑補進去。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-05 03:27 UTC

可以。這兩張的組合比之前那 5 張好用，但 Pass 2 是用 Pass 1 的結果當底，現在 Pass 1 還沒跑。所以要嘛先跑 Pass 1，要嘛直接用這兩張**一次做完**（主題翻譯＋柱子換造型），先試一輪也可以，有三點要注意。

## 為什麼新的參考圖（圖 10）比較好

圖 10 幾乎是**正面看牆**：柱子、火盆、拱門、熔岩門排成一排，跟你的橫向立面最接近。之前那 5 張都是透視走廊，AI 容易把你的構圖一起改掉。圖 10 這個風險小很多，柱子的造型也剛好就是你要的（石塊柱身、牛角火盆、發光龕窗）。

## 要先處理的三件事

1. **圖 10 把地板裁掉。** 圖 10 下半部是大片透視地板，你的原圖沒有地板。不裁的話，AI 很可能在牆腳長出一片透視地面。建議只保留上方約 55%（牆和柱子），裁完圖 10 不必是 16:9，比例照圖 9 就好。
2. **圖 9 裁掉 viewport 邊框。** 上方的深灰條、左右兩側的灰邊、左下角的黑塊都要裁掉，圖要滿版。輸出比例設成跟裁完的圖 9 一樣。
3. **白色灰盒柱要說清楚是佔位物。** 不說的話，AI 可能直接畫成白色大理石柱。

## 一次做完的 prompt（圖 9 放第一張，圖 10 放第二張）

```text
Two reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE: a flat, front-facing orthographic elevation of
a gothic cathedral interior wall for a side-scrolling game — ribbed
vaults, three tall upper windows with a balcony, lower arched windows,
relief panels, a large dark archway on the right. The six plain
light-grey pillars and the small grey horn shapes above three windows
are greybox PLACEHOLDERS: they define the exact position, width,
height and bracket heights of new elements — they are not white
marble.
IMAGE 2 = STYLE, MATERIAL AND PILLAR-DESIGN REFERENCE ONLY. Ignore its
composition and floor.

TASK 1 — THEME TRANSLATION to a hellish lava fortress, in the style of
IMAGE 2: all blue brick becomes heavy charcoal stone blocks with
chipped bevelled edges and hand-painted edge highlights; shadows are
warm purple-brown, never pure black. No sky light — the scene is lit
only by lava and fire glow: orange rim light on stone edges, darker
toward the ceiling vaults. Keep every window tracery exactly; the
glass now glows molten orange / ember red. Railing and metal parts
become dark forged iron.

TASK 2 — repaint every grey placeholder pillar as a hell-fortress
pillar like those in IMAGE 2: stacked heavy charcoal stone blocks;
where the greybox has a bracket, a dark forged-iron horned brazier
with fire; pillars without brackets get one small glowing slit
niche. Each pillar keeps its own variation from the greybox — not six
identical copies. The small horn shapes above the windows become
curved dark horns with ember-lit tips.

GEOMETRY LOCK: flat orthographic front view — no perspective, no
floor plane, no vanishing point. Every arch, window, railing, panel
and pillar keeps its EXACT position, size and silhouette from
IMAGE 1. Do not add chains, statues, rubble or lavafalls. Do not crop,
zoom or extend.
```

## 跑完看結果怎麼判斷

| 結果 | 下一步 |
|---|---|
| 牆和柱子都對 | 用桃紅那層當遮罩修邊，再進 PS 調色收尾 |
| 牆對、柱子漂了（太胖、位置偏、六根一樣） | 改回兩個 pass 做：留這張當 Pass 1 的底，柱子用 Pass 2 單獨重跑 |
| 柱子對、牆變成透視或長出地板 | 圖 10 再裁緊一點，加強 `orthographic` 那句 |
| 窗戶被改成熔岩瀑布 | 在 LOCK 補一句 `windows stay windows` |

想要右邊的大拱門變成熔岩瀑布的話，把 `lavafalls` 從禁止清單刪掉，再補一句 `the large dark archway on the right becomes a glowing lavafall`。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-05 03:28 UTC

參考圖（圖 11）這樣裁可以，用它沒問題。場景圖（圖 12）還要裁邊，另外兩張的順序要跟 prompt 的標籤對上。

## 1. 參考圖（圖 11）：可以用 ✅

地板裁掉了，剩下的是正面的牆和柱子，柱子造型、火盆、熔岩門都看得很清楚。
底部還留著一窄條熔岩裂縫，可以不用再裁。它剛好能提示「牆腳要有熔岩細縫」，prompt 裡的 `no floor plane` 會防止 AI 畫出整片地面。

## 2. 場景圖（圖 12）：還要裁邊 ⚠️

這張還是 viewport 截圖的原樣，下面幾個地方會被 AI 當成畫面內容補完：

- 上方的深灰條（約 y 0–62）
- 左邊的細黃線和灰邊。柱子層左側 x 約 0–50 是灰色的空區。
- 下方的深色條，以及左下角那塊黑色方塊（約 y 1035 以下）
- 右邊的細邊

大約裁到 **x 50–1915、y 62–1035**，以畫面裡實際的牆面邊緣為準。裁完是**約 1.92:1**，不是 16:9，所以 Leonardo 要用 Custom 尺寸，讓輸出比例跟裁完的圖一樣。

## 3. 順序：兩種改法擇一

你這次是**參考圖放第一張、場景圖放第二張**，跟我上一則 prompt 寫的 IMAGE 1 / IMAGE 2 剛好相反。

- **做法 A（推薦）：** 平台上把場景圖拖到第一張，prompt 不用改。
- **做法 B：** 維持現在的順序，把 prompt 裡所有的 IMAGE 1 和 IMAGE 2 對調。

做法 A 比較穩，因為 NB2 通常比較會把第一張圖當成「要改的底」。

處理完這三件事，就可以直接用上一則的 prompt 跑。跑出來照那張判斷表看是哪種結果，再決定下一步。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-05 05:12 UTC

這是正交正面圖本身的限制：正面看不到側面，所以柱子離牆多遠、柱腳柱頭的階梯往前凸多少、牛角往外伸多長，全都被壓成平面。圖 9 的白柱還是平光，等於把這些結構資訊全丟了，AI 只會畫成貼在牆上的浮雕。

2D 背景要表現深度，**只能靠光影和分層**，有三種做法，可以疊著用。

## 做法 1（必做）：用打了光的灰盒取代平光白柱

在 3ds Max 用**同一台正面正交相機**，對灰盒打一盞斜側光再 render：

- **主光從側前方打**，例如左上前方約 45°，或從下方模擬熔岩光，方向要跟你要的最終光向一致。這樣：
  - 柱子會**在後面的牆上落下陰影**，陰影偏移的距離就是柱子離牆的距離，是最重要的深度線索。
  - 柱腳和柱頭每一層階梯，上緣受光、下緣有陰影，凸出的層次就讀得出來。
  - 牛角朝前伸的部分有高光，根部有陰影。
- 加一張 **AO pass** 疊上去（Multiply），讓柱子和牆的交界、階梯的內角變暗。
- 把 render 出來的柱子（含落在牆上的影子）貼進場景圖，取代現在的白柱。

AI 會照著灰盒上的明暗來畫材質，這就是 B2「貼材質灰盒」的原理：**灰盒帶什麼光影，成品就帶什麼體積。**

## 做法 2（選配）：多出一張深度圖

在 3ds Max 的 Render Elements 加一個 **Z-Depth**，輸出近白遠黑的深度圖：

- 用 SD 或 Flux 的 ControlNet 時，直接把它當 depth 控制圖，深度最準。
- 用 NB2 時，可以當第三張參考圖，但 NB2 讀深度圖不一定穩，**做法 1 才是主力**。
- PS 收尾也用得到：拿它當遮罩，對遠處的牆壓暗、疊一點霧，柱子就會往前跳。

## 做法 3（強烈建議，橫向卷軸限定）：拆層做視差

如果這是橫向卷軸遊戲，**最有效的深度表現其實是讓畫面動起來**：

- 把柱子層和牆面層分開輸出成兩張圖（各自去背），進引擎後放在不同的 Z 位置，或給不同的捲動速度。
- 玩家一移動，柱子跟牆之間就有**視差**，深度自然出來，比任何靜態陰影都有說服力。
- 這也剛好解決合成問題：桃紅遮罩本來就是柱子層的 alpha。

## Prompt 要補的句子

不管用哪種做法，都在 prompt 的柱子段落加上這段，把空間關係寫清楚：

```text
DEPTH: the pillars stand freestanding about one metre IN FRONT of the
wall, not attached to it. Each pillar casts a soft shadow onto the
wall behind it, with a dark ambient-occlusion gap between pillar and
wall. The stepped bases and capitals project forward: their top
ledges catch warm rim light, their undersides fall into shadow. The
horns project toward the viewer, with a highlight on their tips and
a shadow at their roots. Follow the light and shadow of the greybox
pillars in IMAGE 1 exactly — they define the volume.
```

## 建議順序

1. 3ds Max 用同一台正面正交相機，render 打光灰盒、AO、Z-Depth 三張。
2. 把打光的灰盒柱子貼進場景圖，取代白柱，再裁邊。
3. 用上一則的 prompt 加上上面的 DEPTH 段落去跑。
4. PS 收尾時把柱子層和牆層分開輸出，進引擎做視差。

另外，圖 13 右側拱門裡有一個**井或水池**。正交正面看不到它的上緣開口，只會剩一條橫邊。如果它在遊戲裡很重要，可以考慮把它單獨做成前景物件，或改用帶一點俯角的相機畫。這屬於 B6 的「出圖相機和遊戲相機分開」，需要的話我們再細談。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-05 05:14 UTC

對，這張還不夠，但比圖 9 的白柱好一點。

## 這張缺什麼

這是 3ds Max viewport 的預設著色，只會根據面的朝向決定明暗。

| 有 ✅ | 沒有 ❌ |
|---|---|
| 柱子比牆深，大致分得出前後 | **柱子沒有落影到牆上**，看不出離牆多遠，最關鍵的線索缺了 |
| 柱頭、柱腳的分段看得到色帶 | 階梯沒有上亮下暗，看起來是畫上去的線，不是凸出來的 |
| | 沒有 AO，柱子和牆的交界沒有暗縫 |
| | 牛角是平的剪影，看不出往前伸 |

所以 AI 看到的是**深色長條貼在牆上**，畫出來大概也是浮雕柱，不是獨立站在牆前面的柱子。

## 怎麼改：同一台正面相機，加上會投影的光

### 快速版（約 5 分鐘）：直接開 viewport 陰影

1. 場景加一盞 **Target Direct（平行光）**，從**左上前方**打：水平偏左約 30–45°，往下約 30–45°。
2. 平行光的 Shadows 勾 **On**。
3. Viewport 左上角的著色選單打開：
   - **Lighting and Shadows → Illuminate with Scene Lights**
   - **Shadows** 打勾
   - **Ambient Occlusion** 打勾
4. 截圖或用 Grab Viewport 存成圖。

### 正式版：Render

用 Scanline 或 Arnold，材質一律灰色、同一盞平行光，再加一盞很弱的 Skylight 補暗部，避免陰影死黑。Render Elements 另外勾 AO 和 Z-Depth（上一則的做法 2）。

### 合格的灰盒應該長這樣

- 每根柱子右下方的牆上都有一條**偏移的影子**，偏移越多代表離牆越遠。
- 柱頭、柱腳每一層的**上緣亮、下緣暗**。
- 柱子和牆的交界有**暗縫**。
- 牛角**朝光的一面亮，根部有影子**。

## 兩個小提醒

1. **光向要跟最終成品一致。** 熔岩版的主光是火盆和下方的熔岩，如果想要熔岩光由下往上的效果，平行光就改成從**左下前方**往上打。灰盒的光向會被 AI 照著畫，現在定好，之後就不用跟固有光打架（SOP 2b-dir 的教訓）。
2. **上方拱頂那幾塊黑色三角形**是 viewport 背面或法線朝外造成的。AI 可能會把它們當成破洞或天空，render 前把法線修好，或在 prompt 寫明那裡是天花板拱頂。

如果打光之後還是讀不出深度，最後一招是把相機**往旁邊偏轉 5–10°**，讓柱子的側面露出一點。不過這樣就不是純正面了，要先確認遊戲畫面接不接受。

## 完整 prompt（已合併 DEPTH 段落，整段複製即可）

```text
Two reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE: a flat, front-facing orthographic elevation of
a gothic cathedral interior wall for a side-scrolling game — ribbed
vaults, three tall upper windows with a balcony, lower arched windows,
relief panels, a large dark archway on the right. The six light-grey
shaded pillars and the small grey horn shapes above three windows are
greybox PLACEHOLDERS: they define the exact position, width, height,
volume and bracket heights of new elements — they are not white
marble.
IMAGE 2 = STYLE, MATERIAL AND PILLAR-DESIGN REFERENCE ONLY. Ignore its
composition and floor.

TASK 1 — THEME TRANSLATION to a hellish lava fortress, in the style of
IMAGE 2: all blue brick becomes heavy charcoal stone blocks with
chipped bevelled edges and hand-painted edge highlights; shadows are
warm purple-brown, never pure black. No sky light — the scene is lit
only by lava and fire glow: orange rim light on stone edges, darker
toward the ceiling vaults. Keep every window tracery exactly; the
glass now glows molten orange / ember red. Railing and metal parts
become dark forged iron.

TASK 2 — repaint every grey placeholder pillar as a hell-fortress
pillar like those in IMAGE 2: stacked heavy charcoal stone blocks;
where the greybox has a bracket, a dark forged-iron horned brazier
with fire; pillars without brackets get one small glowing slit
niche. Each pillar keeps its own variation from the greybox — not six
identical copies. The small horn shapes above the windows become
curved dark horns with ember-lit tips.

DEPTH: the pillars stand freestanding about one metre IN FRONT of the
wall, not attached to it. Keep the shadow each pillar casts onto the
wall behind it, and the dark ambient-occlusion gap between pillar and
wall. The stepped bases and capitals project forward: their top
ledges catch warm rim light, their undersides fall into shadow. The
horns project toward the viewer, with a highlight on their tips and
a shadow at their roots. Follow the light and shadow of the greybox
pillars in IMAGE 1 exactly — they define the volume.

GEOMETRY LOCK: flat orthographic front view — no perspective, no
floor plane, no vanishing point. Every arch, window, railing, panel
and pillar keeps its EXACT position, size and silhouette from
IMAGE 1. Do not add chains, statues, rubble or lavafalls. Do not crop,
zoom or extend.
```

跑完把 4 張結果貼上來，我照之前那張判斷表幫你看是哪種狀況，再決定要直接收尾，還是柱子另外重跑。

## 新 prompt

```text
Three reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE: a flat, front-facing orthographic elevation of
a gothic cathedral interior wall for a side-scrolling game. The six
light-grey shaded pillars on the lower floor and the small grey horn
shapes above three lower windows are greybox PLACEHOLDERS that define
exact position, size and volume — they are not white marble.
IMAGE 2 = STYLE AND MATERIAL REFERENCE ONLY: painterly hand-painted 3D
game art, charcoal stone, lava glow. Ignore its composition.
IMAGE 3 = PLACEMENT MAP: the same scene with coloured boxes. Use it
ONLY to know WHAT goes WHERE. The coloured box lines must NOT appear
in the output.

BOX LEGEND (follow exactly):
- ORANGE boxes (3 total: lower part of pillar 1, middle of pillar 4,
  middle of pillar 6) = a dark forged-iron horned brazier bowl WITH
  FIRE. These are the ONLY three fires on the pillars.
- RED boxes (2 total: upper part of pillar 1 and pillar 2) = a
  decorative spike ornament: a stone collar with one central
  downward-pointing dark iron blade, flanked by two curved horns.
  NO fire, NO bowl, NO glow.
- GREEN boxes (3 total: above three lower windows) = small curved
  horns in matte dark charcoal stone / blackened bone, with only a
  faint edge highlight. NOT glowing, NOT orange, NOT red-hot, NO
  ember tips.
- Pillars 3 and 5 (no box) = plain stacked charcoal stone shafts.

THEME: all blue brick becomes heavy charcoal stone blocks with chipped
bevelled edges and hand-painted edge highlights; shadows warm
purple-brown, never pure black. Lit only by lava and fire glow: the
window glass glows molten orange behind the exact original tracery;
orange rim light on stone edges near the windows and braziers, darker
toward the ceiling vaults. Railing and metal parts become dark forged
iron.

DEPTH: the six lower pillars stand freestanding in front of the wall.
Keep the shadow each pillar casts onto the wall and the dark gap
between pillar and wall. Stepped bases and capitals project forward:
top ledges catch warm rim light, undersides in shadow.

DO NOT: no braziers or fire on the upper balcony pilasters or
anywhere outside the three orange boxes; no glowing horns; no chains,
statues, rubble or lavafalls; NOT pixel art, no pixelated edges —
smooth painterly rendering like IMAGE 2.

GEOMETRY LOCK: flat orthographic front view — no perspective, no
floor plane. Every arch, window, railing, panel and pillar keeps its
EXACT position, size and silhouette from IMAGE 1. Do not crop, zoom
or extend.
```

## 跑完怎麼看

- **火盆數量**：三個火盆都在橘框的位置，二樓沒有火 → ✅
- **尖角裝飾**：柱 1、柱 2 上方沒有火 → ✅
- **窗上的小角**：是暗色的，沒有發紅 → ✅
- **畫框線**：結果裡完全沒出現 → ✅；如果有殘留，PS 修掉或把 IMAGE 3 拿掉重跑

如果哪一項還是錯，把結果貼上來。這張主題已經對了的話，也可以只把錯的部位用遮罩局部重畫，不用整張重跑。

## 新 prompt

```text
Three reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE: a flat, front-facing orthographic elevation of
a gothic cathedral interior wall for a side-scrolling game. The six
light-grey shaded pillars on the lower floor and the small grey horn
shapes above three lower windows are greybox PLACEHOLDERS that define
exact position, size and volume — they are not white marble.
IMAGE 2 = STYLE AND MATERIAL REFERENCE ONLY: painterly hand-painted 3D
game art, charcoal stone, lava glow. Ignore its composition.
IMAGE 3 = PLACEMENT MAP: the same scene with coloured boxes. Use it
ONLY to know WHAT goes WHERE. The coloured box lines must NOT appear
in the output.

BOX LEGEND (follow exactly):
- ORANGE boxes (3: lower part of pillar 1, middle of pillar 4, middle
  of pillar 6) = a dark forged-iron horned brazier bowl WITH FIRE.
  These are the ONLY three open flames in the whole image.
- RED boxes (2: upper part of pillar 1 and pillar 2) = a decorative
  spike ornament: a stone collar with one central downward-pointing
  dark iron blade, flanked by two curved horns. NO fire, NO bowl,
  NO glow.
- GREEN boxes (3: above three lower windows) = small curved horns in
  matte dark charcoal stone / blackened bone, only a faint edge
  highlight. NOT glowing, NOT orange, NOT red-hot, NO ember tips.
- CYAN boxes (5: the carved pilaster at the far left, two upper
  balcony pilasters, upper part of pillar 3 and pillar 5) = a
  self-illuminated flame-coloured inlay: a narrow vertical slot or
  carved rune channel set INTO the stone, glowing molten orange-yellow
  from within, like the glowing slit niches in IMAGE 2. It is
  emissive light inside the stone — NO open flame, NO bowl, NO
  brazier. Keep each one exactly inside its box.

THEME: all blue brick becomes heavy charcoal stone blocks with chipped
bevelled edges and hand-painted edge highlights; shadows warm
purple-brown, never pure black. Lit only by lava and fire glow: the
window glass glows molten orange behind the exact original tracery;
orange rim light on stone edges near the windows, braziers and glowing
inlays, darker toward the ceiling vaults. Railing and metal parts
become dark forged iron.

DEPTH: the six lower pillars stand freestanding in front of the wall.
Keep the shadow each pillar casts onto the wall and the dark gap
between pillar and wall. Stepped bases and capitals project forward:
top ledges catch warm rim light, undersides in shadow.

DO NOT: no braziers or open flames anywhere outside the three orange
boxes; no glowing inlays outside the five cyan boxes; no glowing
horns; no chains, statues, rubble or lavafalls; NOT pixel art, no
pixelated edges — smooth painterly rendering like IMAGE 2.

GEOMETRY LOCK: flat orthographic front view — no perspective, no
floor plane. Every arch, window, railing, panel and pillar keeps its
EXACT position, size and silhouette from IMAGE 1. Do not crop, zoom
or extend.
```

## 為什麼要加第三張

青框錯的這兩處，就是**少了定位圖的典型症狀**。文字寫「two upper balcony pilasters」，AI 不知道是哪兩根，就乾脆四根對稱全畫；柱 3、柱 5 用文字描述太模糊，就被漏掉了。有了定位圖，AI 直接看框的位置，這種錯會少很多。

請確認上傳的是這三張，**都要用裁過的版本**：

|---|---|
| 二樓第 1、4 根短柱多出來的發光 | PS 用仿製印章，取旁邊石塊蓋掉，大約 2 分鐘 |
| 一樓柱 3、柱 5 缺的發光 | 從二樓短柱複製一條發光縫，貼到柱 3、柱 5 的青框位置，再疊一層 Outer Glow 或 Screen 光暈 |
| 灰邊和灰地板 | 直接裁掉 |

位置大致都對了，所以我猜你不滿意的可能是下面其中一項。每一項的解法不同，你說是哪個，我再針對它寫：

- **C. 整體太暗、太單調**，橘色只出現在窗戶和火盆。這要靠 PS 調色，加輪廓光和光暈。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-05 07:01 UTC

這次配件位置大致都對了。柱子會太白、太平，主要原因在**輸入圖**，不是 prompt。

## 為什麼柱子又白又平

1. **太白**：圖生圖會沿用輸入的明暗。灰盒柱子是淺灰色，AI 就照著畫成淺色石頭，跟深色的牆脫節。這跟之前光靠 prompt 調色幾乎無效是同一個原因：明暗要在**輸入圖**改，寫在 prompt 裡沒用。
2. **太平**：灰盒柱子是光滑的單一色塊，AI 只照著外形上材質，所以畫成表面平滑的方柱。參考圖的柱子是**一塊一塊堆起來的大石頭**，有凸出、有縫、左亮右暗，這些在灰盒裡都沒有。

```text
elements — they are not white marble.
IMAGE 2 = STYLE, MATERIAL AND PILLAR-DESIGN REFERENCE ONLY. Ignore its
composition and floor.

TASK 1 — THEME TRANSLATION to a hellish lava fortress, in the style of
IMAGE 2: all blue brick becomes heavy charcoal stone blocks with
chipped bevelled edges and hand-painted edge highlights; shadows are
warm purple-brown, never pure black. No sky light — the scene is lit
only by lava and fire glow: orange rim light on stone edges, darker
toward the ceiling vaults. Keep every window tracery exactly; the
glass now glows molten orange / ember red. Keep the carved panels and
emblems, re-materialised as dark carved stone. Railing and metal parts
become dark forged iron.

TASK 2 — repaint every grey placeholder pillar as a heavy
hell-fortress pillar exactly like those in IMAGE 2:
- Deep charcoal stone, as dark as the wall — never pale or beige.
- Built from LARGE, IRREGULAR stacked stone blocks of different sizes;
  some blocks protrude, some corners are broken off, with deep dark
  mortar gaps and a few cracks.
- One or two dark forged-iron bands with rivets wrapped around the
  shaft, and short iron spikes jutting out from the capital.
- A wider, heavier stepped base; small broken stone chunks at the
  foot; faint lava-orange glow licking up from below the base.
- Strong side lighting for volume: left faces catch warm orange rim
  light, right faces fall into deep purple-brown shadow; capital and
  base ledges project forward, top edges lit, undersides in shadow.
- Where the greybox has a bracket, paint a dark forged-iron horned
  brazier with fire. The small horn shapes above the windows become
  curved dark horns.
- Each pillar keeps its own variation — not six identical copies.

DEPTH: the pillars stand freestanding in front of the wall, not
attached to it. Each pillar casts a soft dark shadow onto the wall
behind it, with an ambient-occlusion gap between pillar and wall.

GEOMETRY LOCK: flat orthographic front view — no perspective, no
floor plane, no vanishing point. Every arch, window, railing, panel
and pillar keeps its EXACT position, height and silhouette from
IMAGE 1; pillars may only gain small protruding blocks, iron bands
and spikes within a few pixels of their outline. Do not add chains,
statues or lavafalls. Do not crop, zoom or extend.
```

跑完如果柱子變得太粗或太亂，把 TASK 2 的第 3、4 點（鐵箍環、碎石堆）刪掉一個再跑一次。這類細節一次加太多，AI 容易做過頭。

BOX LEGEND (follow exactly):
- ORANGE boxes (3: lower part of pillar 1, middle of pillar 4, middle
  of pillar 6) = a dark forged-iron horned brazier bowl WITH FIRE.

  self-illuminated flame-coloured inlay: a narrow vertical slot or
  carved rune channel set INTO the stone, glowing molten orange-yellow
  from within, like the glowing slit niches in IMAGE 2. It is
  emissive light inside the stone — NO open flame, NO bowl, NO
  brazier. Keep each one exactly inside its box.

bevelled edges and hand-painted edge highlights; shadows warm
purple-brown, never pure black. Lit only by lava and fire glow: the
window glass glows molten orange behind the exact original tracery;

horns; no chains, statues, rubble or lavafalls; NOT pixel art, no
pixelated edges — smooth painterly rendering like IMAGE 2.

GEOMETRY LOCK: flat orthographic front view — no perspective, no
floor plane. Every arch, window, railing, panel and pillar keeps its
EXACT position, size and silhouette from IMAGE 1. Do not crop, zoom
or extend.

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-05 10:20 UTC

兩件事都可行，但做法跟你想的有點不同。關鍵是：**你手上有 3ds Max 的 3D 灰盒**。這是最大的優勢，換角度和做三視圖都應該從 3D 出發，不要叫 AI 去「轉相機」。

## 一、用 PS 拼出最好的部位：✅ 建議這樣做

這是 SOP 的「合成收尾」：每一版各有好的部位時，不要一直重擲想一次到位，直接拼。

**步驟：**

1. **選底圖**：挑整體牆面、窗戶、光感最好的一張。我覺得 **v4 + 素材 7** 或 **v1 + 素材 7 的第一張**比較適合當底。
2. **對齊**：這 10 張的尺寸不同（有的 2000×1109 帶灰邊，有的 1376×768），先全部縮放到同一尺寸。所有圖層選起來，執行 PS 的 **Edit → Auto-Align Layers**，就能自動對準。
3. **貼部位**：用遮罩把其他版的好部位貼過來，例如 v4 的鐵箍柱子、v3 的尖角裝飾、v1 的二樓發光縫。
4. **統一色調**：貼過來的部位用 Match Color 或 Curves 對齊底圖的亮度和色溫。這一步最容易穿幫。
5. **接縫**：羽化 2–4px。

⚠️ 每一張都是 AI 重畫的，同一根柱子在不同版本的寬度可能差幾個像素。貼的時候整根柱子連同牆上的影子一起換，不要只換半根。

## 二、個別物件做三視圖：✅ 可行，但建議改用 3D 灰盒

**不推薦的做法：** 從正面圖裁出柱子，叫 AI 去想像側面和背面。AI 可以做（A5 石像鬼、火把就是這樣做的），但側面、背面是 AI 猜出來的，跟你的模型不一定吻合。

**推薦的做法：** 你已經有柱子、火盆、尖角的 3D 灰盒：

1. 在 3ds Max 用**正交相機**分別 render 灰盒的正面、側面、背面，三張用同一盞光。
2. 每張灰盒配上最終圖裡同一個物件的特寫（當材質參考）去跑 AI。等於把「灰盒上材質」的流程套到每個視角。
3. 結果是**形狀 100% 對得上你的模型**，AI 只負責畫材質。

A5 的教訓照樣適用：

- **先做保留光影版，再做去光版（de-lit）**，順序不能反，去掉的光補不回來。
- 去光要寫「**保留材質本色，只移除光影**」，否則會變成灰黏土。
- 左右對稱的物件，**左視圖直接鏡射右視圖**，不用重算。
- 保留光影版拿去貼不受光材質，去光版拿去餵 image-to-3D。兩者不要混用。

另外先確認一下：三視圖是要拿去**建 3D 模型**嗎？如果只是要做 2D 遊戲的素材，可能用不到三視圖，直接去背出單張物件就夠了。

## 三、換成直向走道：✅ 可行，但一定要從 3D 換角度

**不可行的做法：** 把現在這張正面圖丟給 AI，叫它「改成走道透視」。AI 沒辦法可靠地把平面圖轉成透視，柱子數量、窗戶位置、透視線都會亂掉。之前叫它保持正交都偶爾會跑出地板，反過來更難。

**可行的做法（B6 三相機的概念）：**

```
3ds Max 灰盒場景
 → 架一台走道相機（透視、往走道深處看，類似熔岩參考圖 4、8 的角度）
 → 對面那一側：把牆面模組鏡射複製一份，變成兩側都是牆的走道
 → 打光 render 灰盒（跟之前一樣，要有影子和明暗）
 → 丟給 AI，三張圖：
     IMAGE 1 = 走道灰盒 render（結構與透視）
     IMAGE 2 = 你定案的正面熔岩圖（元素字典：柱子、火盆、窗戶長什麼樣）
     IMAGE 3 = 熔岩參考圖（畫風與光）
```

這樣做的好處：

- **透視是 3D 算的，一定正確**。AI 只要把正面圖裡的元素按照灰盒的位置「貼」上去。
- **元素保持一致**：同一根柱子、同一種火盆，只是換了角度。
- 這套做法之後可以重複用，任何新角度都是架一台相機、render、跑 AI。

⚠️ 走道會比正面圖多出兩樣東西，要先在 3D 裡做好：**地板**（正面圖沒有）和**走道盡頭**（深處要放什麼，例如門、拱門或熔岩瀑布）。灰盒裡沒有的東西，AI 就會自己發明。

## 建議順序

1. **先定案正面圖**（PS 合成），它會是之後所有東西的「元素字典」。
2. 從定案圖裁出每種元素的特寫，做成一張**元素參考板**：柱子、火盆、尖角裝飾、窗上小角、發光縫、窗戶。
3. **走道**：3ds Max 架相機、render 灰盒，配上元素參考板跑 AI。
4. **三視圖**：只對要建模的物件做，用 3D 灰盒 render 各視角當結構。

需要的話，我可以把這次整個流程整理成案例 **B14** 寫進 repo，包括四版提示詞的對照、哪一版效果好和原因，以及踩過的坑：太白、太平、框線、像素風。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-06 03:07 UTC

不像的主要原因在**提示詞自相矛盾**，不是 AI 沒看參考圖。

## 為什麼完全不像

1. **柱子的外形被鎖死了。** GEOMETRY LOCK 要求每根柱子「keeps its EXACT silhouette from IMAGE 1」，也就是維持灰盒那根細長、上下有階梯的柱子。但 IMAGE 3 的柱子是**粗壯、輪廓凹凸不平、頂部有大彎角**，外形本身就不同。兩邊衝突時，AI 會照鎖的外形畫，所以畫出來還是灰盒柱，只是換了材質。
2. **比例差很多。** 灰盒柱寬高比約 **1:7**（約 60×430px），IMAGE 3 的柱子約 **1:5**，粗了三成以上。要做出那種厚重感，柱子本來就得變粗。
3. **參考圖是斜角透視。** 要 AI 從斜角特寫自己推出正面長什麼樣，同時還要配合灰盒位置畫六根，一次做太多事了。

所以繼續改文字，結果還是會一樣。

## 建議改法：先做出一根正面柱子，再貼進場景

這是一個 pass 只做一件事，分成三步。

### 第 1 步：把 IMAGE 3 轉成正面柱子素材（單獨跑）

上傳一張：IMAGE 1 = 柱子特寫（圖 3）。比例設成直式，例如 **768×1376**。

```text
IMAGE 1 is a design reference of a hell-fortress stone pillar, seen at
an angle. Redraw this SAME pillar as a clean game asset:

VIEW: a flat, front-facing orthographic elevation — camera level and
perpendicular, no perspective, no foreshortening; the brazier bowl rim
is a straight horizontal line.
DESIGN: keep its exact design — chunky, irregular charcoal stone
blocks of different sizes with chipped corners and dark mortar gaps;
large curved dark horns / tusks jutting out from the capital; a dark
forged-iron horned brazier bowl with fire on the middle of the shaft;
a wide, heavy stepped base with a faint lava-orange glow at the foot.
LIGHT: warm orange rim light on the left faces, deep purple-brown
shadow on the right faces — same painterly hand-painted 3D style.
FRAMING: the whole pillar, centred, from the top of the horns to the
bottom of the base, filling the height of the image.
BACKGROUND: a single flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) background,
no gradient, no floor, no shadow on the background.
REMOVE: the chain, the wall, the lava and everything behind the
pillar.
```

如果還要一根**沒有火盆的版本**（給柱 3、柱 5 用），把 DESIGN 裡火盆那句換成：
`no brazier — instead one small glowing slit niche in the middle of the shaft`

背景用綠色，是因為柱子本身有橘、紅、紫褐色，用綠底去背不會吃到柱子的顏色。

### 第 2 步：PS 貼進場景

1. 綠底去背（Select → Color Range 選綠色 → 刪除）。
2. 用你最滿意的那張結果當底，例如 v4 + 素材 7。
3. 把柱子縮放到**從地面到二樓陽台底下**的高度，對齊六根柱子的中心。
4. 柱子會比原本粗，所以原柱外露的部分直接被蓋掉；如果蓋不住，用仿製印章補牆面。
5. 依照灰盒配置替換火盆和尖角：柱 1、2 頂部用大彎角，柱 1 下段、柱 4、柱 6 用火盆，柱 3、5 用發光縫版。
6. 要讓六根不一樣，可以水平翻轉、稍微調亮暗，或局部換石塊。

做到第 3 步時，把六根柱子的位置（貼好的 PSD 截圖或底圖）給我，我可以像之前一樣幫你算出每根柱子的遮罩，並在牆上加落影。

### 第 3 步（選配）：融合 pass

貼完如果柱子跟牆的光不太一致，用貼好的圖當 IMAGE 1 再跑一次，只調光、不重畫：

```text
IMAGE 1 is a finished lava-fortress wall with six stone pillars pasted
in. RELIGHTING pass only, NOT a repaint: blend the six pillars into
the scene — match their colour, contrast and warm lava rim light to
the wall, add a soft dark shadow from each pillar onto the wall behind
it and an ambient-occlusion gap where pillar meets wall. Every pillar
keeps its exact shape, design, size and position; everything else
stays pixel-identical.
```

## 另一條路：直接改 3D 灰盒

如果之後還要做**走道、三視圖**，最一勞永逸的做法是在 3ds Max 把灰盒柱子改成 IMAGE 3 的造型：變粗、頂部加大彎角、柱腳加寬。灰盒形狀對了，之前的 v1 提示詞就能直接畫出像的柱子，走道和三視圖也會自動一致。

**我的建議：** 先走第 1、2 步，把這張正面圖做完。之後要做走道時再改灰盒。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-06 03:11 UTC

━━━━━━ 20261106 ━━━━━━

這張柱子可以照 **concept→3D 雙軌流程**拆。下面的步驟和提示詞都照 repo 裡的 SOP（`concept-to-3d-v2.md`、A5 石像鬼與火把案例）整理。

## 先判讀這張圖

| 項目 | 判讀 |
|---|---|
| 要拆的物件 | 方形石柱，包含：頂部的大彎角柱頭、柱身中段的牛角火盆、寬厚的階梯柱腳 |
| 要丟掉的 | 鐵鍊、後面的牆和拱、熔岩、所有背景 |
| 相機 | 斜角透視（從左前方看），要壓成正面平視 |
| 原圖光向 | 左側迎光（暖橘），右側背光（紫褐暗部），之後每一視都固定這個世界光 |
| ⚠️ 解析度 | 原圖只有 **263×724**，太小。直接拆的話 AI 會自己補細節，造型容易漂 |

**定義「正面」：** 火盆掛的那一面朝向鏡頭，火盆在正中間。三視圖都以這個為準。

## 流程

```
Step 0  放大原圖（Universal Upscaler 2×，creativity 最低）
Step 1  A 保留光影軌：正面 → 確認 → 側面 → 3/4
Step 2  B 去光軌：拿 A 的每一張轉成平光版，給 image-to-3D
```

**順序不能反：** 先保留光影，再去光。去掉的光補不回來。

- **A 保留光影版**：就是你場景要用的柱子。正面那張直接去背，貼進牆面那張圖。
- **B 去光版**：只有要用 AI 建 3D 模型時才需要。

## Step 0 — 先放大

Leonardo **Universal Upscaler 2×、creativity 最低**，把 263×724 放大到約 526×1448。只放大，不要求加細節。

## Step 1 — A 保留光影軌

平台設定：**Nano Banana 2**、Prompt Enhance None、Style None、比例 **9:16**（例如 768×1376）。

背景用**綠色 #00B140**，不用模板的深色背景。這根柱子本身就是深色，用深色背景去背會吃到柱子；綠色是柱子上沒有的顏色。

### ① 正面（先跑這張，確認對了再往下）

上傳：IMAGE 1 = 放大後的原圖

```text
From the attached concept art, extract ONLY the stone pillar — its
horned capital at the top, the horned brazier bowl with fire mounted
on its shaft, and its stepped base — exactly as they appear in the
image. Re-orient it to a true head-on FRONT view: the face that the
brazier is mounted on faces the viewer directly, with the brazier
centred on the shaft. IGNORE the concept's camera angle and
perspective — do NOT copy its 3/4 view or low angle.

REMOVE completely: the hanging chain, the wall and arch behind the
pillar, the lava, the floor and the whole background.

Render in TRUE ORTHOGRAPHIC PROJECTION, like a model-sheet elevation:
NO perspective, NO foreshortening, NO vanishing point; the camera axis
is horizontal at the pillar's mid-height; the brazier rim reads as a
straight horizontal line.

CRITICAL — preserve the source's baked lighting, colour and tone
EXACTLY as painted: warm orange light on the left side, deep
purple-brown shadow on the right side, the same dark charcoal stone
colour, the ambient occlusion in every crevice, the fire glow. Do NOT
relight, do NOT flatten to studio light, do NOT brighten.

FRAMING: the complete pillar from the tips of the horns to the bottom
of the base, centred, generous margin on all sides, nothing cropped.
BACKGROUND: one flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) colour, no gradient,
no floor, no shadow on the background.
```

**確認方法：** 跟原圖並排看柱頭彎角的數量和方向、火盆形狀、石塊大小、柱腳層數。不對就重跑這張，**先別往下做**。

### ② 側面（正面確認後）

上傳：IMAGE 1 = 放大後的原圖、IMAGE 2 = 確認過的正面圖

```text
IMAGE 1 is the concept art; IMAGE 2 is the confirmed FRONT plate of
the same stone pillar. Render the SAME pillar — identical proportions,
stone blocks, horns, brazier and base as IMAGE 2 — from its RIGHT SIDE,
rotated exactly 90 degrees. The brazier now sticks out to the left in
profile; the horns show their side curve.

Render in TRUE ORTHOGRAPHIC PROJECTION: NO perspective, NO
foreshortening, camera horizontal at mid-height, perpendicular to the
side face.

FIXED WORLD LIGHT: the same light as the front plate, which now comes
from the front of the pillar (the left of this image): the front-facing
edge is lit warm orange, the back half falls into deep purple-brown
shadow. Keep the same dark low-key exposure as the front plate, do NOT
brighten.

No chain, no wall, no lava. FRAMING: complete pillar, centred, nothing
cropped. BACKGROUND: one flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) colour.
```

**左側不用另外生**：把右側圖在 PS 水平翻轉就是左側（A5 驗證過，固定世界光下可以直接鏡射）。

**背面可以省略：** 柱子背面在你的場景裡是靠牆的，這跟 A5 壁掛火把的情況一樣，正面加側面就夠建模了。如果柱子是四面都看得到的獨立柱，再用同一段 prompt 把 `RIGHT SIDE, rotated 90` 改成 `BACK, rotated 180`，光線改成 `backlit, only a thin warm rim on the outline`。

### ③ 3/4 立體圖（給 image-to-3D 當主圖）

上傳：IMAGE 1 = 正面、IMAGE 2 = 側面

```text
IMAGE 1 is the FRONT plate and IMAGE 2 is the SIDE plate of the same
stone pillar. Render the SAME pillar — identical geometry, proportions
and details — as a single 3/4 view: rotated about 35 degrees to show
the front and the right side, seen slightly from above so the top of
the capital and the brazier rim are visible. Near-orthographic, no
wide-angle distortion. Same fixed world light and dark low-key
exposure as the plates. No chain, no wall, no lava. Complete pillar,
centred, nothing cropped, on one flat solid chroma-green (#00B140)
background.
```

## Step 2 — B 去光軌（只在要用 AI 建 3D 時做）

每一張 A 圖各跑一次，上傳那張 A 圖，提示詞相同，只有開頭的視角要換：

```text
IMAGE 1 is a plate of a stone pillar in [FRONT / RIGHT SIDE / 3/4]
view. Keep its EXACT shape, proportions, stone blocks, horns, brazier
and base — this is a lighting change only, do NOT redraw the geometry.

KEEP the real material albedo — the true charcoal stone colour, the
dark iron of the brazier and horns, all surface detail. Do NOT turn it
into grey clay. DE-LIT it: remove all baked lighting — no orange rim
light, no shadow side, no fire glow cast on the stone. Light it only
with perfectly even, uniform, flat diffuse studio lighting. NO cast
shadows, NO highlights.

Centred on a flat neutral grey (#808080) seamless background, nothing
cropped. High-resolution clean de-lit albedo reference plate for
image-to-3D.
```

⚠️ 一定要寫「**保留材質本色**」，A5 就是寫了「去除反照率」，結果變成灰黏土。

火盆裡的**火焰**：去光版建議連火一起拿掉，模型只建碗，火之後在引擎用特效做。要拿掉的話，在 DE-LIT 那句後面加一句 `remove the fire from the brazier bowl, leave it empty`。

## 交付前檢查

- [ ] 每一視都上傳了原圖或前一視的圖，沒有只靠文字描述
- [ ] 正面有疊回原圖比對彎角、火盆、石塊和柱腳
- [ ] 每一視的光向一致：左（前）亮右（後）暗
- [ ] 左側用鏡射，不重生
- [ ] 去光版有寫「保留本色」，背景是 `#808080`

⚠️ AI 只能做到「接近正交」，不會是數學上真正的正交。這組三視圖拿來當**建模參考**就夠了，最終的正交圖要建好模型後用 3D 相機 render。

**先跑 ① 正面，結果貼給我**，我幫你比對原圖確認造型對不對，再往下跑側面。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-06 03:21 UTC

對，**正面要先用 PS 合成一張定稿，再往下做側面**。側面和 3/4 都要拿正面當一致性參考。如果正面還有 5 種版本，後面每一視會各自偏向不同版本，三視圖就對不起來。

## 先看這 5 張

| # | 優點 | 問題 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 比例細長、乾淨，柱腳有浮雕紋章 | 柱頭偏簡單，彎角比較細 |
| 2 | **最粗壯厚重**，最接近原圖的份量 | 柱腳兩側多了裝飾板，比較雜 |
| 3 | **比例最均衡**，柱頭有中央尖刺加兩側彎角，柱腳階梯清楚 | 彎角比原圖小一點 |
| 4 | **彎角最大、最有氣勢** | 柱頭頂端被切成平的，少了收頂 |
| 5 | 有中央尖刺 | 柱腳左邊多一階，**左右不對稱**，不適合當正面定稿 |

整體來說都**抓到原圖的設計**：大石塊柱身、牛角火盆、階梯柱腳、柱頭彎角都在，沒有長出鐵鍊和背景，綠底也很乾淨。這一步算成功。

## 建議的合成方式

**以 #3 當底**（比例最穩、左右對稱），再視需要換兩個部位：

1. **想要彎角更大** → 從 #4 取兩側大彎角，蓋到 #3 柱頭上，保留 #3 的中央尖刺和收頂。
2. **想要柱子更厚重** → 柱身用 #2 的寬度，把 #3 整根水平拉寬約 10–15%。拉寬整根比拼接柱身簡單，也不會有接縫。

### 合成要注意

- **只取同一個部位整塊替換**，例如整組柱頭或整個火盆，不要半個彎角配半個彎角。
- 換過來的部位用 Curves 對齊亮度，**左亮右暗的光向要跟底圖一致**。
- 定稿必須**左右對稱**（火盆、柱腳）。後面側面會直接拿正面當結構依據，正面不對稱，側面就會亂掉。
- 合成完**不要再丟給 AI 重跑一次「統一」**，那樣會重畫幾何，等於白合成。小瑕疵用筆刷在 PS 修。

## 兩個小提醒

1. **要用原始輸出檔，不要用截圖。** 這張是 Leonardo 介面的截圖，有圓角和黑框，解析度也低了。請從 Leonardo 下載每張的原圖再合成。
2. **這 5 張不要丟，正好可以當場景裡的柱子變體。** 正面牆那張需要 6 根不一樣的柱子，例如 #1 細柱放窗戶之間，#2 粗柱放大拱門兩側。

## 接下來

PS 合成好定稿正面後，貼給我。我先拿它跟原圖比對造型，確認後你就可以用上一則的**② 側面**提示詞，上傳「原圖 + 這張定稿」去跑。

如果之後真的要建 3D 模型，建議把柱子拆成**柱頭、柱身、火盆、柱腳**四個模組來做。這樣 6 根柱子可以用同一套模組組合：有的有火盆、有的沒有，火盆高度也不同，剛好對應你灰盒裡的配置。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-06 03:23 UTC

可以，而且這次會比上次好很多。上次失敗是因為柱子參考是**斜角透視**，現在是**正面定稿**，AI 不用自己轉角度。

不過有一點要先改：上次的提示詞把柱子**外形鎖成灰盒那根細柱**，新柱子比灰盒粗、頂部彎角也很寬，照舊寫還是會被細柱外形壓回去。下面兩個做法都處理了這個問題。

## 做法 A：照你說的，三張素材重新出圖

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 藍色場景（灰盒柱）、IMAGE 2 = 熔岩風格參考、IMAGE 3 = 合成好的正面柱子

```text
Three reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE: a flat, front-facing orthographic elevation of
a gothic cathedral interior wall for a side-scrolling game — ribbed
vaults, three tall upper windows with a balcony, lower arched windows,
relief panels, a large dark archway on the right. The six plain
light-grey pillars and the small grey horn shapes above three windows
are greybox PLACEHOLDERS: they mark only the POSITION (centre line),
HEIGHT and the height of each bracket — not the final shape.
IMAGE 2 = STYLE AND MATERIAL REFERENCE ONLY: painterly hand-painted 3D
game art, charcoal stone, lava glow. Ignore its composition and floor.
IMAGE 3 = THE FINAL PILLAR DESIGN, already in flat front view on a
green background. Ignore the green background.

TASK 1 — THEME TRANSLATION to a hellish lava fortress, in the style of
IMAGE 2: all blue brick becomes heavy charcoal stone blocks with
chipped bevelled edges and hand-painted edge highlights; shadows are
warm purple-brown, never pure black. No sky light — the scene is lit
only by lava and fire glow: orange rim light on stone edges, darker
toward the ceiling vaults. Keep every window tracery exactly; the
glass now glows molten orange / ember red. Railing and metal parts
become dark forged iron.

TASK 2 — replace every grey placeholder pillar with the pillar from
IMAGE 3: copy its design exactly — its stone blocks, its horned
capital, its brazier, its stepped base, its width-to-height
proportion. Each pillar stands on the same centre line and spans the
same height (floor to balcony) as its placeholder; it may be WIDER
than the placeholder, and its horns may extend beyond it.
Follow the greybox for the details of each pillar:
- where the greybox has a bowl-shaped bracket, keep the brazier with
  fire, at that bracket's height;
- where the greybox has no bracket, paint the same pillar WITHOUT the
  brazier — plain stone shaft with one small glowing slit niche.
The small horn shapes above the windows become curved dark horns with
ember-lit tips.

GEOMETRY LOCK: flat orthographic front view — no perspective, no
floor plane, no vanishing point. Every arch, window, railing and
panel keeps its EXACT position, size and silhouette from IMAGE 1.
Do not add chains, statues, rubble or lavafalls. Do not crop, zoom
or extend.
```

## 做法 B（推薦）：先把柱子貼進場景，再出圖

這是 B2 的「貼材質灰盒」思路：**柱子已經在輸入圖裡，AI 就不用猜**。這比用文字叫 AI「照 IMAGE 3 畫」可靠很多。

**PS 準備：**

1. 柱子去背（綠底用 Color Range 刪掉）。
2. 貼到藍色場景的 6 根灰盒柱位置，**中心線對齊、高度從地面到二樓陽台底**，蓋掉白柱。
3. 依灰盒配置調整每一根：
   - 柱 4、柱 6：保留火盆，移到灰盒支架的高度
   - 柱 1：火盆移到下段
   - 柱 3、柱 5：把火盆擦掉補石塊（或改用沒火盆的那版）
   - 柱 1、柱 2：柱頭維持尖角裝飾
4. 想要每根不一樣，可以混用你那 5 張變體。

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 貼好柱子的場景、IMAGE 2 = 熔岩風格參考

```text
Two reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE: a flat, front-facing orthographic elevation of
a gothic cathedral interior wall for a side-scrolling game. The six
dark stone pillars with horns and braziers are FINAL painted assets —
keep each one EXACTLY as it is: same shape, design, size and position.
IMAGE 2 = STYLE AND MATERIAL REFERENCE ONLY: painterly hand-painted 3D
game art, charcoal stone, lava glow. Ignore its composition and floor.

TASK — THEME TRANSLATION of everything EXCEPT the six pillars to a
hellish lava fortress, matching the pillars' style: all blue brick
becomes heavy charcoal stone blocks with chipped bevelled edges and
hand-painted edge highlights; shadows warm purple-brown, never pure
black. No sky light — lit only by lava and fire glow: orange rim light
on stone edges, darker toward the ceiling vaults. Keep every window
tracery exactly; the glass glows molten orange / ember red. Railing
and metal parts become dark forged iron. The small grey horn shapes
above the windows become curved dark horns.

BLEND THE PILLARS IN: each pillar casts a soft dark shadow onto the
wall behind it, with an ambient-occlusion gap where pillar meets
wall; the braziers cast warm firelight onto the nearby wall. Do not
repaint the pillars themselves.

GEOMETRY LOCK: flat orthographic front view — no perspective, no
floor plane, no vanishing point. Every arch, window, railing, panel
and pillar keeps its EXACT position, size and silhouette from
IMAGE 1. Do not add chains, statues, rubble or lavafalls. Do not crop,
zoom or extend.
```

## 兩個做法比較

| | A：三張素材 | B：先貼進場景 |
|---|---|---|
| 準備工作 | 少，直接丟 | 要在 PS 貼 6 根柱子 |
| 柱子像不像 | 不一定，AI 可能又畫成自己的版本 | **幾乎一定像**，柱子就是你的定稿 |
| 每根柱子的配置 | 靠文字，可能放錯 | 你在 PS 擺好，**完全照你的** |
| 重跑時一致性 | 每次的柱子都不同 | 每次的柱子都一樣，只有牆在變 |

**建議：** 先用 A 試一次（不用準備）。如果柱子還是不像，或位置又放錯，就改走 B。

⚠️ 不論哪個做法，請用**原始輸出檔**，不要用 Leonardo 介面的截圖。這次傳上來的三張都是縮圖，AI 看到的細節會差很多。

## 做法

圖 2 比圖 1 粗短很多（圖 1 的柱身寬高比約 1:13，圖 2 約 1:6.5），所以我把它拆成這幾塊來排：

| 模組 | 處理 |
|---|---|
| 頂部檐口 | 放在最上面 |
| 尖刺 + 雙彎角柱頭 | 對齊灰盒尖刺和彎角的高度 |
| 柱身石塊 | 原樣**重複堆疊**，把柱子拉長到灰盒的高度，不壓扁也不拉伸 |
| 牛角火盆 | 移到灰盒火盆的位置（下段約 3/4 處） |
| 柱腳 | 柱腳上緣加原本的底座，放在最下面 |

寬度、整體高度、尖刺、火盆和柱腳的位置都跟灰盒對齊了，比例對照請看上面的 ② 對照圖。

檔案：`/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/pillar_restack.png`（460×1705，綠底）

## 要注意的地方

1. **柱身石塊看得出重複。** 同一段石塊重複了好幾次，而且那段原本就在火盆上方，整段都偏橘亮，上半截也是一樣的橘色。
2. **柱頭和柱身的接縫**有點硬。

這兩個問題可以跑一次「只統一、不改形」的 AI pass 解決：

上傳：IMAGE 1 = `pillar_restack.png`

```text
IMAGE 1 is a stone pillar game asset assembled from parts. Keep its
EXACT silhouette, width, height and the exact position, shape and
size of the top cornice, the spike with two curved horns, the horned
brazier and the stepped base — do NOT move, resize or redraw them.

Only repaint the stacked stone blocks of the shaft so they look
natural: vary the block sizes, cracks and chips so no two blocks
repeat; smooth the seams between parts. LIGHTING: dark charcoal stone,
warm orange light on the left faces, deep purple-brown shadow on the
right; the orange fire glow only on the blocks just above the brazier,
the upper shaft is darker. Same painterly hand-painted 3D style.

Keep the flat front orthographic view, nothing cropped, on the same
flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) background.
```

比例設 **9:32**（或 Custom 576×2048），要跟這張圖一樣細長。跑完如果柱頭或火盆跑掉，就用 PS 把我這張的柱頭和火盆疊回去，只保留 AI 重畫的柱身。

如果你在 PS 自己修也可以：用仿製印章把重複的石塊換幾塊，上半截加一層 Multiply 壓暗，這樣大約 5 分鐘。

```text
Two reference images.
IMAGE 1 = PROPORTION GREYBOX: a plain light-grey placeholder pillar.
It defines the EXACT silhouette — width, height, slenderness, and the
vertical position of every part. Follow it strictly.
IMAGE 2 = DESIGN REFERENCE ONLY: take its materials and ornament
designs, NOT its proportions.

TASK — repaint the greybox pillar of IMAGE 1 with the design of
IMAGE 2, part by part, keeping IMAGE 1's layout:
- TOP CAP: the flat square cap at the very top becomes a stepped,
  chipped charcoal stone capital like IMAGE 2's capital mouldings.
- SPIKE RING (just below the cap): the small blade and two side horns
  become IMAGE 2's ornament — one central dark iron spike blade
  flanked by two curved dark horns. No fire here.
- UPPER SHAFT: large stacked charcoal stone blocks with chipped
  corners and dark mortar gaps, like IMAGE 2's shaft.
- BRAZIER (lower-middle, where the greybox has the dark bowl with two
  horns): IMAGE 2's horned iron brazier bowl with fire, at exactly the
  greybox height.
- LOWER SHAFT and BASE: the moulded ring under the brazier, the
  stone-block lower shaft, and a stepped base carved with IMAGE 2's
  emblem panel.

PROPORTION LOCK: keep IMAGE 1's slender proportions — the shaft stays
as thin as the greybox, do NOT widen it to IMAGE 2's thickness. Every
part stays at the same height as in IMAGE 1. Only the horns and the
brazier may stick out slightly past the shaft, as in IMAGE 1.

STYLE: same painterly hand-painted 3D game art as IMAGE 2 — warm
orange light on the left faces, deep purple-brown shadow on the right
faces, fire glow on the stone near the brazier.
VIEW: flat front orthographic view, no perspective.
FRAMING: the complete pillar from top cap to base, centred, nothing
cropped. BACKGROUND: one flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) colour.
```

| 尖刺環跟柱頭黏在一起 | 在 SPIKE RING 加 `a separate band below the cap, with plain shaft between them` |
| 都對了 | 這就是定稿的正面圖，可以直接進下一步：② 側面 |

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-06 06:48 UTC

這次只修圖 1，所以用**局部修整**的寫法：結構、比例、位置都鎖住，只補火和修掉破碎的地方。圖 2 當參考，但要特別排除它的一個瑕疵。

## 圖 1 要修的地方

| 問題 | 修法 |
|---|---|
| 火盆是空的，只有一顆小紅寶石 | 加上火焰 |
| 火盆跟柱身沒接上，看起來是浮著的 | 火盆後方要扣在柱身上，加上鐵箍或支架 |
| 火盆下方那圈線腳破碎，邊緣透出綠色縫隙 | 修成完整、連續的石材線腳 |
| 部分接縫和邊緣不夠乾淨 | 收乾淨，但不改造型 |

⚠️ 圖 2 的火焰有瑕疵：中間是一塊**方形的發光塊**，不是真的火。所以提示詞只拿它當「有火的樣子」參考，並明確禁止方形光塊。

## 上傳與設定

1. IMAGE 1 = 圖 1（要修的柱子）
2. IMAGE 2 = 圖 2（參考）

Nano Banana 2、Prompt Enhance None、Style None，輸出比例**跟圖 1 一樣**。

## 提示詞

```text
Two reference images.
IMAGE 1 is the BASE — a hand-painted stone pillar game asset on a
green background. Its layout, proportions, width, height and the
position of every part are FINAL. This is a REPAIR and FINISHING pass,
NOT a redesign.
IMAGE 2 is a reference for a lit brazier and a finished pillar only.

FIX in IMAGE 1:
1. FIRE: put a natural, lively fire in the horned brazier bowl —
   organic tongues of flame, bright yellow core fading to orange and
   red tips, a few floating embers, rising a little above the rim.
   Do NOT paint a square or rectangular glowing block. Remove the small
   red gem on the bowl front.
2. BRAZIER MOUNT: the bowl must be solidly attached to the shaft —
   add a dark forged-iron collar band with rivets around the shaft
   behind the bowl, so it is clearly held, not floating.
3. MOULDING under the brazier: make it one complete, continuous
   stepped stone moulding with clean edges. Fill every gap — no green
   showing through anywhere inside the pillar.
4. FINISH: clean up all block edges and mortar lines on the whole
   pillar; keep the chipped, hand-painted stone look.
5. FIRE LIGHT: warm orange glow from the fire on the stone just above
   and around the bowl, fading with distance.

LOCKS: keep IMAGE 1's exact silhouette, slender proportions, top cap,
the carved diamond panels on the upper shaft, the stone blocks, the
emblem panel and the stepped base, all in the same positions. Do NOT
add the spike ornament from IMAGE 2. Keep the same painterly style,
warm light on the left faces, purple-brown shadow on the right faces.
Flat front view, complete pillar, nothing cropped, same flat solid
chroma-green (#00B140) background.
```

## 跑完怎麼看

- **火**：是自然的火舌，不是方塊
- **火盆**：有鐵箍或支架接在柱身上
- **綠縫**：柱子內部完全沒有綠色透出
- **結構**：跟圖 1 疊在一起，外形和各部位高度都沒動

如果想要圖 2 頂部那圈尖刺彎角，把 LOCKS 裡 `Do NOT add the spike ornament from IMAGE 2` 這句改成 `add IMAGE 2's spike ornament under the top cap`。

### 🧑 使用者 · 2026-11-06 06:52 UTC

鐵箍的地方 是層板結構 是否能更合理

---

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-06 06:53 UTC

可以。比起加鐵箍，更合理的做法是**讓火盆直接坐在那圈層板上**：把層板改成從柱身挑出的石托（corbel），火盆放在上面。這樣受力關係一眼就看得懂，也跟哥德建築的托座語彙一致。

## 結構邏輯

```
   柱身（上段）
  ┌─────────┐
  │  火盆碗  │ ← 碗底坐在石托頂面，碗背貼著柱身
 ═╧═════════╧═ ← 層板頂面：平的承托面
  ╲_________╱  ← 層板往下逐層收窄（倒階梯石托）
   柱身（下段）
```

- **層板頂面要平**，寬度比火盆碗底稍寬，才能穩穩承托。
- **層板往下逐層收窄**，像倒過來的階梯，把重量導回柱身。
- 火盆的**兩根牛角是從碗身長出來的裝飾**，不負責支撐。
- 鐵件縮小成兩個角落的小鐵扣，把碗固定在層板上就好，不要整圈鐵箍。

## 把提示詞的 FIX 2、3 換成這段

其他段落不變，只換掉原本的 2 和 3：

```text
2. BRAZIER SEAT: the brazier bowl sits directly ON TOP of the stepped
   stone moulding below it — this moulding is a projecting stone
   corbel that supports the bowl. Its top is a flat, level shelf,
   slightly wider than the bottom of the bowl; the bowl rests on it
   with a clear contact line and a soft contact shadow, its back
   touching the shaft. No iron collar band around the shaft — at most
   two small dark iron clamps holding the bowl's rim to the shelf.
3. CORBEL: make the moulding one complete, solid stone corbel that
   steps INWARD as it goes down (wider at the top, narrowing toward
   the lower shaft), like a classic supporting bracket. Clean,
   continuous ledges with chipped stone edges; fill every gap — no
   green showing through anywhere inside the pillar. The two horns
   grow from the sides of the bowl as decoration, not as supports.
```

另外，LOCKS 段要加一句，避免 AI 把石托改回原本的樣式：

```text
The corbel under the bowl may be reshaped as described above; every
other part stays exactly as in IMAGE 1.
```

## 跑完檢查

- 火盆碗底是**坐在**層板頂面上，有接觸陰影，沒有懸空
- 層板是**上寬下窄**，看起來在撐東西
- 柱身沒有多出一圈鐵箍

如果石托畫得太大、太搶眼，就在 CORBEL 那段加 `keep it compact — no wider than the bowl`。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-06 07:12 UTC

兩根柱子都完成了，分工也很清楚：

| | 柱子 A（尖刺彎角＋下段火盆） | 柱子 B（菱形雕紋＋石托火盆） |
|---|---|---|
| 對應灰盒 | 柱 1、柱 2（頂部有尖刺環） | 柱 4、柱 6（中段火盆） |
| 狀況 | ⚠️ 火盆裡**還是方形的發光塊** | ✅ 火焰自然，石托承重的關係很合理 |

## 收尾前還有兩個小地方

1. **柱子 A 的方形火塊**：這張看起來跟之前的參考圖是同一張，火還沒修。最快的做法是在 PS 把**柱子 B 的火焰**（含光暈）複製過來，蓋在 A 的火盆上，對齊碗口後縮放到同樣寬度。兩根柱子的火長得一樣，在場景裡反而更統一。
2. **兩個火盆前面的小紅寶石**：如果不想要，用仿製印章取旁邊的碗身蓋掉。想保留當裝飾也可以，但兩根要一致。

## 接下來可以走兩條路

**A. 放回場景（2D 背景用）**
1. 綠底去背：Select → Color Range 選綠色 → 刪除，邊緣用 Defringe 1px 去掉綠邊。
2. 貼回正面牆那張定稿圖，按灰盒位置放：A 放柱 1、柱 2，B 放柱 4、柱 6。柱 3、柱 5 可以用 B 去掉火盆的版本，或改成發光縫。
3. 每根在牆上加落影和暗縫，讓柱子站在牆前面。貼好後把圖給我，我可以像之前一樣幫你自動算影子。

**B. 繼續做三視圖（要建 3D 才需要）**
用之前的「② 側面」提示詞：IMAGE 1 放原始概念圖，IMAGE 2 放這次定稿的柱子，A、B 兩根各跑一次。

你要先走哪一條？如果想把這次的流程記錄下來，我也可以整理成 B14 案例寫進 repo：熔岩主題翻譯、灰盒比例加元素圖、石托修整、PS 合成定稿。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-06 07:13 UTC

兩根柱子都成型了，PS 合成的效果很好。

## 檢查結果

| | 柱 A（菱形雕花，火盆在上段） | 柱 B（尖刺環，火盆在下段） |
|---|---|---|
| 火焰 | ✅ 自然的火舌，沒有方塊 | ✅ 自然的火舌 |
| 火盆承托 | ✅ 坐在石托上，有支撐感 | ✅ 下方有線腳承托 |
| 綠縫 | ✅ 沒有 | ✅ 沒有 |
| 比例 | ✅ 跟白模一樣細長 | ✅ 跟白模一樣細長 |
| 小瑕疵 | 火盆正面還留著小紅寶石 | 同樣有小紅寶石 |

紅寶石如果是你刻意保留的裝飾就沒問題。不要的話，用 PS 仿製印章蓋掉，大約 1 分鐘。

兩根的柱腳、柱身石塊和紋章都一樣，放在場景裡風格會很統一。

## 下一步，兩條路

**1. 放回正面牆場景（建議先做）**

這兩根就能對應灰盒的配置：

- **柱 B**（尖刺環 + 下段火盆）→ 柱 1、柱 2 這類頂部有尖刺的位置
- **柱 A**（上段火盆）→ 柱 4、柱 6 這類中段有火盆的位置
- 沒有火盆的柱 3、柱 5 → 把柱 A 的火盆蓋掉，換成一條發光縫

做法：綠底去背，縮放到灰盒柱的高度，對齊位置貼到場景底圖上。貼好後把 PSD 截圖給我，我可以幫你在牆上加柱子的落影和暗縫（之前做過的流程）。

**2. 做三視圖（要建 3D 模型才需要）**

用之前的「② 側面」提示詞，上傳「這根柱子 + 原本的柱子概念圖」就能跑。兩根柱子的柱身和柱腳一樣，**只要做一根的側面**，另一根換頂部和火盆位置就好。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-06 07:14 UTC

可以，但**不建議把兩根柱子當參考圖丟給 AI，讓它「照著畫」**。之前已經試過，AI 不會照抄參考柱子，而且比例會被灰盒鎖住（就是那次「柱子完全不像」）。

比較穩的做法是：**先把柱子直接貼進場景，再讓 AI 處理牆面**。柱子已經是成品了，就不要再給 AI 重畫的機會。

## 第一步：在 PS 把柱子貼進灰盒場景

先對照灰盒，決定每根位置用哪根柱子：

| 位置 | 灰盒配件 | 使用的柱子 |
|---|---|---|
| 柱 1 | 頂部尖刺 + 下段火盆 | **柱 B**（原樣） |
| 柱 2 | 頂部尖刺，沒有火盆 | 柱 B，**去掉火盆** |
| 柱 3 | 素柱 | 柱 A，**去掉火盆** |
| 柱 4 | 中段火盆 | **柱 A**（原樣） |
| 柱 5 | 素柱 | 柱 A，**去掉火盆** |
| 柱 6 | 中段火盆 | **柱 A**（原樣） |

火盆高度剛好吻合：柱 A 的火盆在柱身約 40% 高，柱 B 的在約 62% 高，都跟灰盒一致。

**做法：**
1. 綠底去背，**再多做兩個去掉火盆的版本**：B 的火盆位置、A 的石托位置，用仿製印章補成石塊。
2. 把柱子縮放到灰盒柱的高度，從二樓陽台底下到地面，對齊中心，蓋掉白柱。
3. 柱子比白柱寬一點也沒關係，蓋過去就好。
4. 用裁過邊框的版本（`scene27_crop.png` 的範圍）當底圖。

## 第二步：跑 v1，但柱子鎖住

上傳：IMAGE 1 = 貼好柱子的場景，IMAGE 2 = 熔岩風格參考。

v1 原本 TASK 2 是「重畫灰盒柱」，現在改成「**柱子已完成，只調光、不重畫**」：

```text
Two reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE: a flat, front-facing orthographic elevation of
a gothic cathedral interior wall for a side-scrolling game — ribbed
vaults, three tall upper windows with a balcony, lower arched windows,
relief panels, a large dark archway on the right. The six dark stone
pillars with horns and fire braziers on the lower floor are FINISHED
ASSETS already painted in the final style.
IMAGE 2 = STYLE AND MATERIAL REFERENCE ONLY. Ignore its composition
and floor.

TASK 1 — THEME TRANSLATION to a hellish lava fortress, in the style of
IMAGE 2: all blue brick becomes heavy charcoal stone blocks with
chipped bevelled edges and hand-painted edge highlights; shadows are
warm purple-brown, never pure black. No sky light — the scene is lit
only by lava and fire glow: orange rim light on stone edges, darker
toward the ceiling vaults. Keep every window tracery exactly; the
glass now glows molten orange / ember red. Railing and metal parts
become dark forged iron. The small grey horn shapes above the windows
become curved dark horns with ember-lit tips.

TASK 2 — the six pillars are FINAL: keep their exact design, shape,
stone blocks, horns, braziers and fire. Do NOT redesign or repaint
them. Only blend them into the scene: match their colour and contrast
to the new wall, let the brazier fires cast a soft warm glow on the
wall around them, and give each pillar a soft dark shadow on the wall
behind it with an ambient-occlusion gap where pillar meets wall.

GEOMETRY LOCK: flat orthographic front view — no perspective, no
floor plane, no vanishing point. Every arch, window, railing, panel
and pillar keeps its EXACT position, size and silhouette from
IMAGE 1. Do not add chains, statues, rubble or lavafalls. Do not
crop, zoom or extend.
```

## 第三步：柱子貼回原圖

NB2 會重畫整張圖，柱子細節多少會漂一點。所以跑完以後，**把第一步的柱子圖層再貼回最上層一次**：

- 牆面用 AI 的結果（有熔岩光、有柱子影子）
- 柱子用你的原圖（100% 不變）
- 接縫羽化 2px，用 Curves 微調柱子亮度，讓它跟牆面一致

這樣柱子跟你定案的完全一樣，牆面也帶有柱子的落影和火光。

## 更省事的替代做法

如果你對之前某張 v1 結果的牆面已經滿意，例如 v4 + 素材 7 那張，可以**跳過第二步**：

1. 用那張結果當底圖
2. 柱子直接貼上去，蓋掉 AI 畫的舊柱子
3. 跑一次之前給過的「融合 pass」提示詞（只調光、加落影）
4. 柱子再貼回去

這樣不用重擲整張牆，只需要處理柱子的光影融合。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-06 07:16 UTC

對，就是這樣：**6 根柱子全部貼好 → 讓 AI 調光影 → 柱子再貼回去**。

## 流程

```
① PS：6 根柱子貼到牆面底圖上（照對照表配置）
② AI：只調光影（加落影、火光照牆、統一色調）
③ PS：原柱子圖層再蓋回最上層，收邊
```

## 底圖選哪張

| 情況 | 底圖 | 第 ② 步用的提示詞 |
|---|---|---|
| 已經有滿意的熔岩牆面（例如 v4 + 素材 7） | 那張 AI 結果 | **融合 pass**（下面這段） |
| 還沒有滿意的牆面 | 藍色灰盒場景 | 上一則的 v1 柱子鎖版本（牆面也要翻譯） |

我建議用**已經有的熔岩牆面**，這樣只需要調光，風險最小。

## 第 ② 步提示詞（融合 pass）

上傳一張：貼好柱子的圖。

```text
IMAGE 1 is a finished lava-fortress wall for a side-scrolling game,
flat front orthographic view, with six stone pillars pasted in. This
is a RELIGHTING and BLENDING pass only, NOT a repaint.

Blend the six pillars into the scene:
- Match their colour, contrast and warm lava rim light to the wall.
- Each brazier fire casts a soft warm orange glow on the wall and
  stone around it, fading with distance.
- Each pillar casts a soft dark shadow onto the wall behind it, with
  a dark ambient-occlusion gap where pillar meets wall, so the
  pillars read as standing in front of the wall.
- Clean up any hard pasted edges around the pillars.

LOCKS: every pillar keeps its exact design, shape, size and position —
same stone blocks, horns, braziers and fire. The wall, windows,
balcony, arches and panels stay exactly as they are. Do not add,
remove or move anything. Do not crop, zoom or extend.
```

## 第 ③ 步重點

- 柱子圖層放回最上層，用**柱子的去背輪廓**當遮罩
- AI 畫的**落影、火光照在牆上的光**留在下層，不要被蓋掉
- 接縫羽化 2px，柱子亮度用 Curves 對齊周圍

貼好柱子後，可以先把 ① 的圖給我看，我幫你確認配置和比例有沒有對。

## 為什麼用 PS 的做法，不重跑 AI

之前的實測已經證明，**用提示詞調光幾乎沒效果**：圖生圖會沿用輸入圖的明暗。而且重跑整張，柱子和牆面又會被重畫。補光是最適合用確定性工具做的事。

## 我做了什麼

在三個火盆（柱 1、柱 4、柱 6）的位置各加一圈**暖橘色的光**：

- **靠近火的地方最亮**：柱身、火盆上下的石塊、旁邊的牆面都被照亮
- **往外逐漸變暗**，大約延伸到半根柱子的距離
- 光是乘在原本的材質上，所以**石塊紋理還在**，不是一片糊掉的橘色

| 檔案 | 用途 |
|---|---|
| `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/scene98_fireglow.png` | 加好火光的整張圖 |
| `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/fireglow_zoom.png` | 下層放大，方便檢查 |

## 想自己在 PS 調整的話

1. 新增一個空白圖層，混合模式設 **Screen**（要更亮就用 Linear Dodge (Add)）。
2. 用大號**柔邊筆刷**，顏色約 `#FF8A30`，不透明度 20–30%，在每個火盆上點一下。火盆周圍加一圈小的、比較亮的光。
3. **要讓柱子本身更亮**：再加一層，Clipping Mask 到柱子圖層，柱身朝火的那一側再刷亮一點。
4. 用圖層不透明度控制強弱。

## 還可以再補的兩點

- **底部那條橘褐色色帶**是 AI 畫出來的地板殘留，要裁掉或塗黑。
- **柱子的落影**：AI 這次沒在牆上畫出柱子的影子。如果需要，我可以用柱子的位置幫你在牆上加落影和暗縫，就像之前做過的那樣。

看懂了：柱 4 和柱 6 的火盆就在柱 3、柱 5 的右邊，但柱 3、柱 5 朝火的那一側（右側）沒有被照亮，所以看起來不合理。

## 提示詞

上傳：IMAGE 1 = 這張結果（單張）
設定：Nano Banana 2、Prompt Enhance None、Style None，輸出比例跟這張一樣

```text
IMAGE 1 is a finished lava-fortress wall for a side-scrolling game,
flat front orthographic view. This is a LOCAL RELIGHTING pass only,
NOT a repaint.

On the lower floor there are six freestanding stone pillars. Counting
from the LEFT, pillar 3 stands just left of the brazier fire on
pillar 4, and pillar 5 stands just left of the brazier fire on
pillar 6. Right now pillars 3 and 5 ignore that fire — fix it:

- Pillar 3 and pillar 5: add a warm orange-yellow RIM LIGHT along
  their RIGHT edges, the side facing the nearby fire. It is strongest
  at the height of the fire, fading smoothly up and down the shaft.
  Brighten the right-facing faces of their stone blocks, capitals and
  base mouldings slightly in warm orange, with a soft bounce glow on
  the stone between each pillar and the fire.
- Their LEFT sides stay in the current shadow.
- Pillars 4 and 6 (with the fires): add a soft warm glow from their
  own fire onto the stone just above and below the brazier.

LOCKS: every pillar keeps its exact shape, design, stone blocks,
carvings, size and position. Do not change the fires, the walls,
windows, balcony, arches, panels or colours anywhere else. Do not
add, remove or move anything. Do not crop, zoom or extend.
```

## 注意

1. **這張要先裁邊再上傳。** 左邊有一條灰邊，底部有一條咖啡色帶，不裁的話 AI 可能把它們當成地板畫進去。
2. **原本計畫「最後把原柱子貼回去」，這一步要改。** 如果現在才貼回原柱子，AI 補上的邊緣光會被蓋掉。建議**直接用這張的柱子**，不貼回原柱；或者貼回原柱後，再跑一次這段提示詞。
3. 如果 AI 改到其他地方，可以改用 **PS 手補**，大約 2 分鐘：
   - 新增一個圖層，混合模式設 **Color Dodge** 或 **Linear Dodge (Add)**
   - 用柔邊筆刷、橘色（約 #FF8A30），低流量，沿著柱 3、柱 5 的**右緣**刷一條
   - 火盆高度刷最亮，往上往下逐漸變淡
   - 圖層不透明度調到 40–60%

如果你要讓這兩根柱子跟火盆的光完全一致，PS 手補反而最穩。之前 SOP 的教訓也是：**單張只差光向時，dodge 收尾比重擲整張可靠。**

## 檢查結果

| 位置 | 結果 |
|---|---|
| 柱 4、柱 6（有火盆） | ✅ 火盆上方的柱身被火照亮，暖光往上漸弱，很自然 |
| 柱 3、柱 5 的右緣 | ⚠️ 只有一點點暖色，邊緣光不明顯 |
| 細壁柱（約在柱 4 窗戶右邊，x≈1230） | ❌ AI 把邊緣光打到了這根細壁柱的左緣，**打錯對象** |
| 其他地方 | ✅ 牆面、窗戶、柱子造型都沒有被改 |

AI 分不清「第 3、5 根獨立柱」和「牆上的細壁柱」，所以光打錯了位置。用文字再描述一次，效果也有限。

## 建議：剩下的用 PS 補

這張整體已經很好了，不要再整張重跑。

**1. 補柱 3、柱 5 的右緣光**
- 新增圖層，混合模式設 **Linear Dodge (Add)**
- 柔邊筆刷，橘色約 **#FF8A30**，流量 10–15%
- 沿柱 3、柱 5 的**右緣**刷一條細光：**在火盆高度最亮**，往上往下漸淡
- 柱頭、柱腳的線腳右側凸角也點一下
- 圖層不透明度調到 40–60%

**2. 壓掉打錯的光**
- 細壁柱（x≈1230）左緣多出來的那條亮光，用一個 **Multiply** 圖層，吸旁邊牆面的暗色輕刷壓下去
- 或直接從上一版結果裡，把這一小塊遮罩貼回來

**3. 裁邊**
左邊的灰邊和底部的咖啡色帶要裁掉。

## 如果想再用 AI 試一次

可以改用**紅圈定位法**：在 PS 用紅筆把柱 3、柱 5 的右緣各畫一條豎線，當第二張圖上傳，提示詞這樣寫：

```text
IMAGE 1 is the BASE — a finished lava-fortress wall. IMAGE 2 is the
same image with two red vertical lines; use it ONLY as a placement
map — the red lines must NOT appear in the output.

LOCAL RELIGHTING only: along each red line, add a warm orange-yellow
rim light on the RIGHT edge of that stone pillar, the side facing the
nearby brazier fire. Strongest at the height of the fire, fading
smoothly up and down. Nothing else changes — every other pillar, the
thin wall pilasters, walls, windows and fires stay pixel-identical.
Do not crop, zoom or extend.
```

不過這種**只差局部光向**的情況，PS 手補大約 2 分鐘就能完成，而且不會動到其他地方。我比較建議用 PS 收尾。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 03:19 UTC

━━━━━━ 20261109 ━━━━━━

這次有效了，可以直接拿去 PS 合成。

## 檢查結果

| 位置 | 結果 |
|---|---|
| 柱 3 右緣 | ✅ 有暖色邊緣光，跟柱 4 火盆之間的牆面也被照亮了 |
| 柱 5 右緣 | ✅ 有暖光，比柱 3 弱一點，但看得出光是從火盆那一側來的 |
| 柱 4、柱 6 | ✅ 火盆上下的柱身有火光 |
| 其他地方 | ⚠️ 整張是重畫的，窗戶、石磚細節會跟圖 1 有些微差異，所以合成時**只取需要的區域** |

## PS 合成重點

1. **對齊**：兩張放在同一個檔案，執行 **Edit → Auto-Align Layers**。兩張都帶 viewport 灰邊，可能差幾個像素，先對齊再做遮罩。
2. **只取兩塊區域**：從圖 2 取「柱 3 到柱 4 火盆」和「柱 5 到柱 6 火盆」這兩塊，連同中間被照亮的牆面，其他地方都用圖 1。
3. **遮罩要用大柔邊**：光是漸層的，遮罩邊緣用大號柔邊筆刷（約 100–200px），不要用硬邊，否則會看到一塊一塊的亮區。
4. **對色**：貼過來的區域如果整體偏亮或偏紅，用 Curves 或 Color Balance 微調，讓它跟圖 1 的牆面接得上。
5. **最後裁邊**：上方和左右的灰邊、左下角的黑塊都要裁掉。

合成完這張正面牆就可以定稿了。要的話，我可以把整個流程整理成案例 **B14** 寫進 repo，內容包括灰盒加落影、定位圖、柱子拆解與三視圖、柱子合成回場景、局部補光，以及每一步的逐字提示詞和踩過的坑。

最左邊那根雕花壁柱還是**偏粉米色、表面光滑**，跟其他炭灰石柱不搭。下面的提示詞只改這一根，其他地方都鎖住。

|---|---|
| 粉米色、偏亮 | 深炭灰石，跟其他柱子一樣暗 |
| 表面光滑 | 大小不一的石塊，有崩角和深色石縫 |
| 平光、看不出光向 | 柱 1 的火盆就在它右邊，所以**右緣要有暖橘邊緣光**，左側落在陰影裡 |
| 雕花紋飾 | 保留，改成深色石雕 |

## 上傳與設定

上傳：IMAGE 1 = 這張合成圖（**建議先裁掉灰邊和左下黑塊**）
設定：Nano Banana 2、Prompt Enhance None、Style None，輸出比例跟這張一樣

## 提示詞

```text
IMAGE 1 is a finished lava-fortress wall for a side-scrolling game,
flat front orthographic view. It is final except for ONE element.
This is a LOCAL RESTYLE pass, NOT a repaint of the image.

TARGET: the tall carved pilaster at the FAR LEFT edge of the lower
floor — the pale, pinkish-beige column left of the first brazier.
Right now it is too light and too smooth and does not match the rest.

Restyle ONLY this pilaster to match the six dark stone pillars:
- Deep charcoal stone, as dark as the other pillars — no pale beige
  or pink.
- Built from stacked stone blocks of slightly different sizes, with
  chipped corners, dark mortar gaps and a few cracks, in the same
  painterly hand-painted style.
- Keep its carved panel ornaments, now as dark carved stone with
  hand-painted edge highlights.
- Lighting: the brazier fire stands just to its RIGHT, so its right
  edge catches a warm orange rim light, strongest at the height of
  the fire and fading up and down; its left side falls into deep
  purple-brown shadow.

LOCKS: keep the pilaster's exact position, width, height and outer
silhouette. Everything else — all six pillars, the braziers and
fires, walls, windows, balcony, arches and panels — stays
pixel-identical. Do not add, remove or move anything. Do not crop,
zoom or extend.
```

## 跑完以後

- 跟之前一樣**用 PS 只取這根壁柱**貼回合成圖，用柔邊遮罩收邊，其他地方不動。

- 想讓它跟其他柱子呼應，可以在提示詞的雕花那點後面加一句：
  `add one narrow glowing molten-orange slit niche in its lower half`

```text
Two reference images.

Rebuild ONLY this pilaster in the exact stone-block style of IMAGE 2:
- REMOVE all of its old carved ornaments, tracery and decorative
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 03:48 UTC

有參考價值。它的**外形結構**值得保留，**表面的雕花**才是要換掉的部分。上一版只寫「保留外形」，AI 分不出哪些算結構、哪些算裝飾，就把雕花一起留下來了。

## 這根柱子的結構

| 部位 | 結構（保留） | 表面（換掉） |
|---|---|---|
| 整體 | **轉角柱**：正面寬、右側有一條窄側面，看得出是兩個面 | — |
| 上段 | 頂部線腳 | 尖拱形的哥德壁龕雕花 |
| 中段 | **一道往外凸的水平腰線**（約 1/3 高度），把柱子分成上下兩段 | — |
| 下段 | 兩個長方形的凹面板框 | 面板裡的花紋 |
| 底部 | 階梯狀柱腳 | 雕花 |

這些分段跟你那 6 根柱子的語彙一致：柱頭、腰線、柱腳都有。**保留分段、只換表面**，它就會像同一套建築。

## 改法：把結構逐項寫進提示詞

上傳的兩張圖跟上次一樣：IMAGE 1 = 合成圖，IMAGE 2 = `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/stone_block_ref.png`。只換掉中間「Rebuild ONLY this pilaster」那段：

```text
Rebuild ONLY this pilaster. KEEP its architectural structure, REPLACE
its surface:

KEEP (structure):
- It is a corner pilaster: a wide front face plus a narrow side face
  on its right, so it reads as a solid block with two visible faces.
- A moulded cap at the top.
- One projecting horizontal waist moulding about one third of the way
  up, dividing it into a tall upper section and a shorter lower
  section.
- A stepped base at the bottom.

REPLACE (surface):
- REMOVE every carving: the pointed gothic niche tracery, the panel
  frames and all relief patterns. No ornaments on any face.
- Fill both faces of both sections with large, clearly separated
  stacked stone blocks exactly like IMAGE 2 — distinct rectangles with
  chipped bevelled edges, lit top edges, shadowed bottom edges and
  deep dark mortar gaps. Bold, easy-to-read block outlines.
- The waist moulding, cap and base become chunky chipped stone ledges
  like the capital and base in IMAGE 2.
- Same dark charcoal colour as IMAGE 2. The front face catches a warm
  orange rim light on its right edge from the nearby brazier fire; the
  narrow side face is in deeper purple-brown shadow.
```

LOCKS 段不變。

## 如果還想留一點哥德味

可以只保留**上段的尖拱輪廓**，把它簡化成一個淺淺的凹龕，裡面不放雕花，或放一條發光縫，跟其他柱子的發光縫呼應。要這樣做的話，在 REPLACE 第一點後面加：

```text
Exception: keep ONE simple pointed-arch recess in the upper section,
plain stone inside with a narrow glowing molten-orange slit — no
tracery.
```

我的建議是**先跑純石塊版**。石塊版跟 6 根柱子最統一，凹龕之後隨時能在 PS 加上去。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 03:50 UTC

不一定要 3 張，**2 張就夠了**。第 3 張（原始壁柱）其實已經在圖 1 合成圖的最左邊。不過放第 3 張也可以，它的好處是解析度比較清楚，AI 比較容易看懂結構。缺點是 AI 也會清楚看到它的**雕花和粉藍色**，容易又抄回來。

**我的建議：** 先用 2 張跑。如果結構跑掉了（腰線不見、轉角側面不見），再改用 3 張的版本。

## 方案 A：2 張（推薦先跑）

1. IMAGE 1 = 合成圖
2. IMAGE 2 = 石塊特寫

提示詞用上一則的那版，也就是「KEEP 結構、REPLACE 表面」那段，加上 LOCKS 段。

## 方案 B：3 張

1. IMAGE 1 = 合成圖
2. IMAGE 2 = 石塊特寫
3. IMAGE 3 = 原始壁柱

完整提示詞：

```text
Three reference images.
IMAGE 1 is a finished lava-fortress wall for a side-scrolling game,
flat front orthographic view. It is final except for ONE element.
This is a LOCAL RESTYLE pass, NOT a repaint of the image.
IMAGE 2 = MATERIAL REFERENCE ONLY: a close-up of one of the dark stone
pillars already in IMAGE 1 — copy its stone blocks, colour and style.
IMAGE 3 = STRUCTURE REFERENCE ONLY: a close-up of the pilaster to be
rebuilt, in its OLD style. Take ONLY its shape and divisions from it —
IGNORE its carvings, its pale pink and blue colours and its smooth
surface.

TARGET: the tall pilaster at the FAR LEFT edge of the lower floor of
IMAGE 1, left of the first brazier (the same pilaster as IMAGE 3).

KEEP (structure, from IMAGE 3):
- It is a corner pilaster: a wide front face plus a narrow side face
  on its right, so it reads as a solid block with two visible faces.
- A moulded cap at the top.
- One projecting horizontal waist moulding about one third of the way
  up, dividing it into a tall upper section and a shorter lower
  section.
- A stepped base at the bottom.

REPLACE (surface, from IMAGE 2):
- REMOVE every carving: the pointed gothic niche tracery, the panel
  frames and all relief patterns. No ornaments on any face.
- Fill both faces of both sections with large, clearly separated
  stacked stone blocks exactly like IMAGE 2 — distinct rectangles with
  chipped bevelled edges, lit top edges, shadowed bottom edges and
  deep dark mortar gaps. Bold, easy-to-read block outlines.
- The waist moulding, cap and base become chunky chipped stone ledges
  like the capital and base in IMAGE 2.
- Same dark charcoal colour as IMAGE 2. The front face catches a warm
  orange rim light on its right edge from the nearby brazier fire; the
  narrow side face is in deeper purple-brown shadow.

LOCKS: keep the pilaster's exact position, width, height and outer
silhouette in IMAGE 1. Everything else — all six pillars, the braziers
and fires, walls, windows, balcony, arches and panels — stays
pixel-identical. Do not add, remove or move anything. Do not crop,
zoom or extend.
```

## 判斷用哪個方案

| 2 張跑出來的結果 | 下一步 |
|---|---|
| 石塊清楚，腰線和轉角側面都在 | ✅ 完成，PS 只取這根壁柱貼回去 |
| 石塊清楚，但結構變簡單了（腰線或側面不見） | 改用方案 B（3 張） |
| 又出現雕花 | 3 張也救不了，改走 PS 粗稿：在壁柱上畫出石縫線再跑 |

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 05:03 UTC

方向是對的：雕花拿掉了，壁柱也換成深色石塊。但效果勉強，主要是**解析度不夠**，提示詞已經不是瓶頸了。

## 為什麼勉強

這根壁柱在整張圖裡只有約 **70×430 像素**。AI 每次都要重畫整張 2000 寬的圖，分給這根壁柱的細節非常少，所以石塊畫得小而模糊，腰線和轉角側面也做不出來。這就是 B2 記錄過的「**像素預算**」問題：小物件放在大圖裡，提示詞寫得再好也畫不精細。

## 解法：裁切 → 生成 → 貼回（Crop-Gen-Paste）

只把壁柱那一塊裁下來放大，讓 AI 用整張畫布的解析度只畫這一根柱子。我已經幫你裁好、放大 3 倍（上面送出的檔案），裡面包含壁柱、旁邊一點牆面和柱 1 的火盆，所以 AI 看得到火光從哪裡來。

### 上傳

1. IMAGE 1 = `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/left_pilaster_crop.png`（局部裁切）
2. IMAGE 2 = 石塊特寫（`stone_block_ref.png`）
3. IMAGE 3 = 原始壁柱（結構參考）

設定：Nano Banana 2、Prompt Enhance None、Style None，Dimensions **Custom，比例跟裁切圖一樣**（612×1302，約 1:2.1，例如 960×2040）。

### 提示詞

```text
Three reference images.
IMAGE 1 = BASE: a close-up crop of a lava-fortress wall, flat front
orthographic view. On the LEFT is a tall pale carved pilaster; on the
right are a brick wall niche and a dark stone pillar with a brazier
fire. Only the pilaster changes.
IMAGE 2 = MATERIAL REFERENCE ONLY: copy its stone blocks, colour and
painterly style.
IMAGE 3 = STRUCTURE REFERENCE ONLY: the same pilaster in its old
style. Take ONLY its shape and divisions — ignore its carvings and its
pink and blue colours.

Rebuild the LEFT pilaster:
- STRUCTURE: a corner pilaster with a wide front face and a narrow
  side face on its right; a moulded cap at the top; one projecting
  horizontal waist moulding about one third of the way up; a stepped
  base at the bottom.
- SURFACE: remove every carving, niche tracery and panel. Both faces
  are built only from large stacked charcoal stone blocks like
  IMAGE 2 — about 6 to 8 blocks in the upper section and 3 to 4 in the
  lower section, each a distinct rectangle with chipped bevelled
  edges, a lit top edge, a shadowed bottom edge and deep dark mortar
  gaps. Bold, clearly readable block outlines.
- The cap, waist moulding and base are chunky chipped stone ledges
  like IMAGE 2.
- LIGHT: same dark charcoal value as the pillar on the right. The
  brazier fire is to its right: warm orange rim light on the right
  edge of the front face, strongest at the fire's height; the narrow
  side face in deep purple-brown shadow.

LOCKS: keep the pilaster's exact position, width, height and outer
silhouette. The brick wall, the niche, the pillar and the brazier
fire on the right stay exactly as they are. Do not crop, zoom or
extend. The left edge of the image is the canvas edge — paint nothing
beyond the pilaster.
```

### 貼回合成圖

1. 結果**縮回 1/3**（612×1302 → 204×434），也就是原本裁切的大小。
2. 貼回合成圖的原位置：**左上角對齊 x=46、y=598**。
3. 遮罩**只留壁柱本體**，右邊的牆和柱 1 用原合成圖，接縫羽化 2px。
4. 用 Curves 對齊亮度。

這樣壁柱就能畫出比整張重跑時清楚得多的石塊，其他地方完全不會動。之後如果有其他小物件不夠精細，都可以用同一招。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 05:29 UTC

四根柱子都已經是正面正交、綠底，**可以直接當三視圖的「正面定稿」**，不用再跑正面。

## 先決定做幾套

這四根其實是 **2 種柱身 × 有火盆／沒火盆**，所以不用跑 4 套三視圖：

| 跑三視圖 | 不用跑，從跑好的圖修出來 |
|---|---|
| **柱 B 有火盆**（圖 1：尖刺環 + 下段火盆） | 柱 B 無火盆（圖 2）→ 在 PS 把火盆蓋掉 |
| **柱 A 有火盆**（圖 3：菱形雕花 + 石托火盆） | 柱 A 無火盆（圖 4）→ 在 PS 把火盆蓋掉 |

只跑有火盆的兩套，**無火盆版從同一組圖修出來，四根的三視圖保證一致**。另外跑四套的話，每套都會各自漂移。

## 共同設定

- Nano Banana 2、Prompt Enhance None、Style None
- 比例跟正面圖一樣（**768×1376**）
- 背景一律綠色 #00B140
- **柱子斷面當作正方形**：側面寬度 = 正面寬度。這句一定要寫，不然 AI 會把側面畫成薄片。

## ① 右側面（每套跑一次）

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 該柱的正面圖（圖 1 或圖 3）

```text
IMAGE 1 is the confirmed FRONT orthographic plate of a stone pillar
game asset. Render the SAME pillar — identical height, stone blocks,
mouldings, ornaments and brazier — from its RIGHT SIDE, rotated
exactly 90 degrees.

SHAPE: the shaft has a SQUARE cross-section, so the side view is the
SAME width as the front view, with the same stone-block courses at the
same heights. The cap, mouldings and stepped base keep the same
heights and the same step profile as in IMAGE 1.
ORNAMENTS IN PROFILE: the brazier bowl now sticks out to the LEFT of
the image (toward the pillar's front), seen from the side; its horns
show their side curve. [柱 B 加：The spike ring shows its central blade
projecting to the left in profile and one curved horn facing the
viewer.] [柱 A 加：The diamond carvings are only on the front face — the
side face is plain stone blocks. The stone corbel under the bowl
projects to the left.]

Render in TRUE ORTHOGRAPHIC PROJECTION: no perspective, no
foreshortening, camera horizontal at mid-height, perpendicular to the
side face.
FIXED WORLD LIGHT: the same light as IMAGE 1, now coming from the
pillar's front (the left of this image): the front-facing edge catches
warm orange light, the rest of the side face is in deeper purple-brown
shadow; the brazier fire glows on the stone near it. Keep the same
dark exposure as IMAGE 1, do NOT brighten.

Same painterly hand-painted style. Complete pillar, centred, nothing
cropped. Background: one flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) colour.
```

把 `[柱 B 加…]` 或 `[柱 A 加…]` 換成對應那句，另一句刪掉。

**左側面不用跑**：右側面在 PS 水平翻轉就是左側面。

**驗收：** 側面圖跟正面圖並排，**柱頭、腰線、火盆、柱腳的高度要能用水平線對齊**。對不齊就重跑，先不要往下做。

## ② 背面（選配）

柱子站在牆前面，橫向卷軸遊戲看不到背面。**只有要建完整 3D 模型才需要。**

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 正面、IMAGE 2 = 側面

```text
IMAGE 1 is the FRONT plate and IMAGE 2 is the SIDE plate of the same
stone pillar. Render the SAME pillar from the BACK, rotated 180
degrees: same width, height, cap, mouldings and base as IMAGE 1. The
back face is plain stacked stone blocks with no carvings; only the
tips of the horns and the rim of the brazier peek out at the edges.
True orthographic, no perspective. Backlit: mostly deep purple-brown
shadow with only a thin warm rim on the outline. Same style. Complete
pillar, centred, on one flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) background.
```

## ③ 3/4 立體圖（給 image-to-3D 當主圖）

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 正面、IMAGE 2 = 側面

```text
IMAGE 1 is the FRONT plate and IMAGE 2 is the SIDE plate of the same
stone pillar. Render the SAME pillar — identical geometry, proportions
and details — as a single 3/4 view: rotated about 35 degrees to show
the front and the right side, seen slightly from above so the top of
the cap and the brazier rim are visible. Square shaft. Near-
orthographic, no wide-angle distortion. Same fixed world light and
dark exposure as the plates. Complete pillar, centred, nothing
cropped, on one flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) background.
```

## ④ 去光版（只有要餵 image-to-3D 才做）

每張圖各跑一次，上傳那張圖：

```text
IMAGE 1 is a plate of a stone pillar in [FRONT / RIGHT SIDE / BACK /
3/4] view. Keep its EXACT shape, proportions, stone blocks, mouldings,
ornaments and brazier — lighting change only, do NOT redraw the
geometry.

KEEP the real material albedo — the true charcoal stone colour, the
dark iron of the brazier and horns, all surface detail. Do NOT turn it
into grey clay. DE-LIT it: remove all baked lighting — no orange rim
light, no shadow side, no fire glow on the stone. Remove the fire from
the brazier bowl and leave it empty. Light it only with perfectly
even, flat diffuse studio lighting. NO cast shadows, NO highlights.

Centred on a flat neutral grey (#808080) seamless background, nothing
cropped. High-resolution clean de-lit albedo reference plate for
image-to-3D.
```

## 建議順序

1. 柱 B：側面 → 對齊驗收 →（選配）背面 → 3/4
2. 柱 A：同上
3. PS：把每張有火盆的圖蓋掉火盆，做出無火盆版
4. 要建模才做去光版

**先跑柱 B 的側面，結果貼給我**。我幫你對齊正面檢查高度，確認後再往下做。

## 四張圖各自負責什麼

| 圖 | 負責 | 不要拿 |
|---|---|---|
| 走道灰盒 | **構圖、透視、柱子位置和輪廓**（權威） | — |
| 概念圖 | 氣氛、熔岩光、地面裂縫、盡頭的火山熔岩瀑布 | 它的柱子、鐵鍊、破屋頂、構圖 |
| 柱子 ×2 | 柱子設計：有火盆版、無火盆版 | 它們是正面圖，透視角度要照灰盒 |

## 我先處理了灰盒（上面送出的檔案）

1. **桃紅剪影換成深炭灰。** 桃紅色會被 AI 當成要畫的顏色，就像之前白柱被畫成白石頭。我把剪影換成深灰色，靠走道中央那一側稍微亮一點，提示 AI 光的方向。
2. **盡頭拱門的透明區**原本轉出來會變黑，我把它補成**白色**，提示詞會指定這裡是要補畫遠景的區域。

1. IMAGE 1 = `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/corridor_greybox_shaded.png`
2. IMAGE 2 = 概念圖
3. IMAGE 3 = 有火盆的柱子
4. IMAGE 4 = 無火盆的柱子

Nano Banana 2、Prompt Enhance None、Style None、**16:9**。

## 提示詞

```text
Four reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE AND COMPOSITION AUTHORITY: a 3D greybox render
of a long gothic cathedral corridor in one-point perspective — ribbed
vaulted ceiling, upper balconies with railings, tall arched windows,
a stone-tiled floor, and two rows of freestanding pillars. The dark
grey pillar shapes are PLACEHOLDERS: they define the exact position,
size, perspective and silhouette of each pillar. The pure white
pointed archway at the far end is an EMPTY OPENING to be filled.
IMAGE 2 = MOOD AND LIGHTING REFERENCE ONLY: take its hellish lava
atmosphere, colour grade, lava-cracked floor and the volcano with a
lavafall in the distance. IGNORE its pillars, chains, broken roof and
composition.
IMAGE 3 = PILLAR DESIGN WITH BRAZIER. IMAGE 4 = THE SAME PILLAR
WITHOUT BRAZIER. Both are flat front views — take ONLY their design.

TASK 1 — THEME TRANSLATION, in the mood of IMAGE 2: all blue stone
becomes dark charcoal stone with chipped edges and hand-painted edge
highlights; shadows warm purple-brown, never pure black. The window
glass glows molten orange. Railings become dark forged iron. The
floor tiles become cracked dark stone with thin glowing lava cracks,
brighter toward the far end. Lit only by fire and lava: a strong warm
glow from the far end of the corridor, plus the braziers.

TASK 2 — PILLARS: repaint every placeholder pillar in the design of
IMAGES 3 and 4 — stacked charcoal stone blocks, a spike ring with a
central blade and two curved horns near the top, stepped base — seen
in the SAME perspective as its placeholder. Alternate them: on each
side, the nearest pillar has a burning horned brazier like IMAGE 3,
the next has none like IMAGE 4, and so on down the corridor. The
corridor-facing side of each pillar catches warm firelight; the outer
side falls into shadow.

TASK 3 — FAR OPENING: fill the white archway with a distant hellish
vista like IMAGE 2 — a red smoky sky, a volcano and a glowing
lavafall — small, hazy and far away, the brightest point of the
image.

GEOMETRY LOCK: keep IMAGE 1's exact camera, one-point perspective,
vanishing point, ceiling vaults, balconies, windows, floor grid and
every pillar's position and size. Do not crop, zoom or extend. Do
not add chains, rubble, broken walls or a broken roof. Not pixel art
— smooth painterly 3D game-art rendering.
```

## 跑完以後

- **柱子不像**：不要整張重跑。把近處那兩、三根大柱子各自裁下來（裁切 → 生成 → 貼回），因為近景柱子最需要細節。
- **火盆配置想換**：在 TASK 2 改「Alternate them」那句。如果規則複雜，就照之前的做法，用彩色框做一張定位圖當第 5 張。
- **構圖被改**，例如屋頂破掉、多了鐵鍊：那是概念圖的內容滲進來了。把概念圖裁到只剩**盡頭火山那一塊**再上傳，參考圖越小，越不會帶進它的構圖。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 06:51 UTC

可以，而且**比我改的那張更好**，建議直接改用這張。

## 為什麼比較好

| | 剪影版（上一張） | 這張（3D 灰盒 render） |
|---|---|---|
| 柱子體積 | 平面剪影，只能靠我加的漸層提示 | ✅ 有真正的受光面和背光面，斷面和階梯都看得出來 |
| 火盆位置 | 看不出來，只能用文字規定「交錯」 | ✅ **模型裡已經有火盆碗**：近處的第 2 根有、第 1 根沒有，AI 照著畫就好 |
| 尖刺彎角 | 剪影上的小凸起 | ✅ 有立體的彎角 |

這就是之前說的「灰盒帶什麼光影，成品就帶什麼體積」。這張的柱子資訊量多很多。

## 兩個要注意的地方

1. **柱子太白。** 近處的柱子幾乎是白色，之前「柱子太白」的問題就是從這裡來的。兩種處理方式擇一：
   - **3ds Max**：柱子材質的顏色改成深灰（RGB 約 60 左右）再 render 一次，最穩。
   - **PS**：用快速選取工具選出柱子，Curves 往下壓暗。
   - 都不做的話，就靠提示詞裡的 `never pale` 硬壓，效果比較不穩。
2. **柱子上有彩色的燈光染色。** 有些柱子偏橘、有些偏綠，這是 3D 場景燈光造成的。提示詞要說明這只是灰盒的顏色。

## 改過的提示詞

上傳順序：IMAGE 1 = 這張，IMAGE 2 = 概念圖，IMAGE 3 = 有火盆柱，IMAGE 4 = 無火盆柱。設定不變。

```text
Four reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE AND COMPOSITION AUTHORITY: a lit 3D greybox
render of a long gothic cathedral corridor in one-point perspective —
ribbed vaulted ceiling, upper balconies with railings, tall arched
windows, a stone-tiled floor, and two rows of freestanding pillars.
The pale grey pillars are greybox PLACEHOLDERS: their shape, volume,
perspective, spike horns and brazier bowls define the final pillars
exactly. Their pale, orange or greenish tints are only greybox
lighting — ignore those colours. The pure white pointed archway at the
far end is an EMPTY OPENING to be filled.
IMAGE 2 = MOOD AND LIGHTING REFERENCE ONLY: take its hellish lava
atmosphere, colour grade, lava-cracked floor and the volcano with a
lavafall in the distance. IGNORE its pillars, chains, broken roof and
composition.
IMAGE 3 = PILLAR DESIGN WITH BRAZIER. IMAGE 4 = THE SAME PILLAR
WITHOUT BRAZIER. Both are flat front views — take ONLY their design.

TASK 1 — THEME TRANSLATION, in the mood of IMAGE 2: all blue stone
becomes dark charcoal stone with chipped edges and hand-painted edge
highlights; shadows warm purple-brown, never pure black. The window
glass glows molten orange. Railings become dark forged iron. The
floor tiles become cracked dark stone with thin glowing lava cracks,
brighter toward the far end. Lit only by fire and lava: a strong warm
glow from the far end of the corridor, plus the braziers.

TASK 2 — PILLARS: repaint every greybox pillar in the design of
IMAGES 3 and 4 — stacked charcoal stone blocks with dark mortar gaps,
the spike ring with a central blade and curved horns near the top, a
stepped base — following each greybox pillar's exact shape and
perspective. They are deep charcoal stone, as dark as the walls —
NEVER pale, white or light grey. Where a greybox pillar has a bowl,
paint a horned iron brazier with a burning fire like IMAGE 3; where it
has no bowl, paint it like IMAGE 4 with no fire. The corridor-facing
side of each pillar catches warm firelight; the outer side falls into
shadow.

TASK 3 — FAR OPENING: fill the white archway with a distant hellish
vista like IMAGE 2 — a red smoky sky, a volcano and a glowing
lavafall — small, hazy and far away, the brightest point of the
image.

GEOMETRY LOCK: keep IMAGE 1's exact camera, one-point perspective,
vanishing point, ceiling vaults, balconies, windows, floor grid and
every pillar's position, size and shape. Do not crop, zoom or extend.
Do not add chains, rubble, broken walls or a broken roof. Not pixel
art — smooth painterly 3D game-art rendering.
```

跟上一版的差別：火盆改成**照模型裡有沒有碗**決定，不再用「交錯」規則；另外加了「忽略灰盒的燈光染色」和「柱子不能是淺色」。

**建議先壓暗柱子再跑**，效果會穩很多。
