先說整體：構圖、透視、天花板、盡頭的火山熔岩瀑布都對了，柱子設計（尖刺環、彎角、石塊）也有照參考圖畫出來。跟灰盒對照，我看到以下幾處偏差：

| # | 問題 | 灰盒 | 結果 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **火盆多了** | 最近的兩根柱子（畫面最左、最右）**沒有碗** | 每根柱子都畫了火盆，連最近那兩根也有 |
| 2 | **地面裂縫太多** | — | 熔岩裂縫鋪滿整個地面，像一張發光的網格，搶走焦點 |
| 3 | **整體太紅、太均勻** | — | 牆、柱、地面全都泛紅發光，沒有暗部。概念圖是「暗中有亮點」，這張是「全部都亮」 |
| 4 | 火盆正面有紅寶石 | — | 參考柱子上的紅寶石被抄過來了 |

你說「不太對」，是指上面哪一項？還是別的地方（例如柱子造型、空間感）？我先把這四項的改法給你。

## 改法

**1. 火盆位置 → 在 TASK 2 明確點名**

把 TASK 2 裡 `Where a greybox pillar has a bowl...` 那句換成：

```text
Braziers ONLY where IMAGE 1's greybox pillar has a bowl. The two
NEAREST pillars (the large ones at the far left and far right edges)
have NO bowl — paint them like IMAGE 4, no fire. Do not add a brazier
to any pillar that has no bowl in IMAGE 1.
```

如果還是錯，就用之前正面牆那招：做一張定位圖，用橘框標出有火盆的柱子，當第 5 張上傳。

**2. 地面裂縫 → 改成少量**

把 TASK 1 的地面那句換成：

```text
The floor tiles become cracked dark stone; only a FEW thin glowing
lava cracks, mostly in the far half of the corridor near the opening.
Most tiles in the foreground are intact dark stone with no glow.
```

**3. 太紅太均勻 → 加明暗對比句**

加在 TASK 1 最後：

```text
Keep it LOW-KEY: most of the walls, ceiling and pillars stay in deep
shadow; light comes only from the far opening, the braziers and the
windows, falling off quickly. Strong dark-to-light contrast, not an
evenly red-lit scene.
```

調色這件事 PS 比 AI 可靠（之前實測過 prompt 改曝光幾乎無效）。所以也可以直接保留這張，在 PS 用 Curves 壓暗中間調，再把火盆、盡頭和窗戶的亮度遮罩回來。

**4. 紅寶石** → 在 DO NOT 加 `no red gem on the brazier bowls`，或 PS 用仿製印章蓋掉。

## 建議

這張的構圖和柱子都已經對了，**不要整張重跑**：

- 第 1 項（最近兩根柱子的火盆）：用 PS 仿製印章蓋掉，或者把最近的兩根柱子用「裁切 → 生成 → 貼回」各自重畫。
- 第 2、3 項：用 PS 調色處理，把地面的裂縫壓暗。

只有你覺得柱子造型或空間感不對，才需要改提示詞整張重跑。告訴我是哪一項，我再幫你調整。

```text
Five reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE AND COMPOSITION AUTHORITY: a lit 3D greybox
render of a long gothic cathedral corridor in one-point perspective —
ribbed vaulted ceiling, upper balconies, tall arched windows, a
stone-tiled floor, and two rows of freestanding pillars. The grey
pillars are PLACEHOLDERS: their shape, volume, perspective, spike
horns and brazier bowls define the final pillars exactly. Their
pale, orange or greenish tints are only greybox lighting — ignore
those colours. The pointed archway at the far end is an EMPTY
OPENING to be filled.
IMAGE 2 = MOOD AND LIGHTING REFERENCE ONLY: hellish lava atmosphere,
colour grade, and the volcano with a lavafall in the distance. IGNORE
its pillars, chains, broken roof and composition.
IMAGE 3 and IMAGE 4 = PILLAR MATERIAL AND DETAIL REFERENCE ONLY: take
their stone blocks, spike ring, horns and brazier bowl design. They
are flat front views — do NOT copy where the brazier sits on them.
IMAGE 5 = BRAZIER PLACEMENT MAP: the same scene as IMAGE 1 with six
orange boxes. The orange lines must NOT appear in the output.

BRAZIERS — follow IMAGE 5 exactly:
- There are exactly SIX braziers, one inside each orange box: three
  on the left row and three on the right row.
- Each brazier hangs on the side of its pillar that FACES THE CENTRE
  OF THE CORRIDOR — on the left row it sticks out to the right, on
  the right row it sticks out to the left. Seen in perspective, NOT
  facing the camera.
- Every other pillar has NO brazier and NO fire — including the two
  large nearest pillars at the far left and far right edges. Pillars
  alternate: no brazier, brazier, no brazier, brazier...

PILLARS: repaint every greybox pillar in the material of IMAGES 3 and
4 — stacked charcoal stone blocks with dark mortar gaps, the spike
ring with a central blade and curved horns near the top, a stepped
base — following each greybox pillar's exact shape and perspective.
Deep charcoal stone, as dark as the walls — never pale. The
corridor-facing side catches warm firelight, the outer side is in
shadow. No red gems on the braziers.

THEME: all blue stone becomes dark charcoal stone with chipped edges
and hand-painted edge highlights; shadows warm purple-brown. Window
glass glows molten orange. Railings become dark forged iron. The floor
becomes cracked dark stone with only a FEW thin glowing lava cracks,
mostly near the far opening. Keep it LOW-KEY: most surfaces stay in
deep shadow; light comes from the far opening, the six braziers and
the windows, falling off quickly.

FAR OPENING: a distant hellish vista like IMAGE 2 — red smoky sky, a
volcano and a glowing lavafall — small and hazy, the brightest point.

GEOMETRY LOCK: keep IMAGE 1's exact camera, perspective, vanishing
point, ceiling, balconies, windows, floor grid and every pillar's
position, size and shape. Do not crop, zoom or extend. No chains,
rubble or broken roof. Smooth painterly 3D game-art rendering, not
pixel art.
```

對，兩處配件都跟參考圖不一樣：

| 部位 | 參考圖 | 結果 |

| **柱頭尖刺環** | 一圈石托，中間一片**往上的刀刃**，兩側各一支**往上彎的彎角**，左右對稱 | 只剩**一支往外伸的大角**，刀刃不見了 |
| **火盆** | 方口鐵碗，兩側各一支**高高往上彎的月牙角**，角包住火焰 | 碗太小，兩側的角也很小 |

原因是配件在整張圖裡太小，AI 只能大概畫一下。之前給的柱子參考圖是**整根柱子**，配件細節在裡面也只佔一小塊。

## 解法：配件特寫參考圖，加上局部放大重畫

我準備了 4 張圖（上面送出的檔案）：

- **配件參考**：從你的柱子圖裁出的**尖刺環特寫**和**火盆特寫**
- **要修的底圖**：結果近景的**左側**和**右側**裁切，各包含兩根柱子，放大 2 倍

左右各跑一次，每次上傳 3 張：

1. IMAGE 1 = `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/corridor_crop_left.png`（第二次換成 `corridor_crop_right.png`）
2. IMAGE 2 = `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/ref_spike_ring.png`
3. IMAGE 3 = `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/ref_brazier.png`

設定：Nano Banana 2、Enhance None、Style None，Dimensions 跟裁切圖同比例（1120×1640，約 2:3）。

```text
Three reference images.
IMAGE 1 is the BASE — a close-up crop of a finished lava-fortress
corridor, seen in perspective. It is final except for the ornaments on
the stone pillars. This is an ORNAMENT REPLACEMENT pass, NOT a repaint.
IMAGE 2 = EXACT DESIGN of the spike ring. IMAGE 3 = EXACT DESIGN of the
brazier. Copy their shapes precisely, adapted to the perspective of
each pillar in IMAGE 1.

1. SPIKE RING — on every pillar, replace the single curved horn near
   the top with the full spike ring of IMAGE 2: a projecting stone
   collar ledge; in the centre of the pillar face a tall pointed dark
   stone blade standing UPRIGHT, with chevron-carved layers at its
   base; and on BOTH sides of the collar a curved horn rising UPWARD
   like a crescent, mirror-symmetric. Seen in perspective: the blade
   on the face toward the corridor, the two horns at the two corners.
2. BRAZIER — on the pillar that has a brazier, replace it with the
   brazier of IMAGE 3: a wide, square-rimmed dark iron bowl with an
   iron band, and on BOTH sides a tall crescent horn curving UP and
   inward, rising higher than the flames and framing them. Make it
   larger, like IMAGE 3, keeping the fire. No red gem.
3. Pillars without a brazier stay without one.

LIGHT: the ornaments take the scene's lighting — warm firelight on
edges facing the fire and the corridor, purple-brown shadow elsewhere.

LOCKS: the pillars' position, size, shape, stone blocks and
perspective, and the walls, windows, railings, floor and balcony stay
exactly as in IMAGE 1. Do not crop, zoom or extend.
```

### 🧑 使用者 · 2026-11-09 07:44 UTC

我想要用柱子的三視圖加強  這樣在5張圖生成會比較好嗎

---

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 07:44 UTC

會有一點幫助，但不是最有效的做法。這次會錯，主要是**灰盒本身**的問題。

## 為什麼錯：AI 照灰盒畫

對照灰盒，近處柱子的頂部本來就是**每根只有一支往外伸的大角**，沒有中間的刀刃，火盆碗兩側的角也很小。AI 在這張圖裡把灰盒當成「幾何權威」，所以照灰盒的形狀畫，柱子參考圖只拿來決定材質。**灰盒跟參考圖衝突時，灰盒會贏。**

這跟正面牆那次「柱子完全不像」是同一個問題：結構被鎖住，參考圖改不動造型。

## 三視圖能做到什麼

| | 效果 |
|---|---|
| ✅ 幫助 | AI 看得到配件的**側面輪廓**：刀刃從側面看是一片薄板、彎角的弧度、火盆從側面看的樣子。透視角度下畫起來會比較像 |
| ❌ 限制 | 灰盒的形狀還是會蓋過它；圖越多，每張分到的注意力越少；配件在整根柱子的三視圖裡仍然很小 |

## 建議順序

**1. 根本解法：在 3ds Max 改灰盒的配件形狀（最有效）**

- 柱頭：做成**石托 + 中間直立刀刃 + 兩側往上彎的彎角**
- 火盆：碗加大，兩側加上**高過火焰的月牙角**
- 形狀粗略就好，有正確的剪影和方向，AI 就會照著畫

重新 render 一次，用剛才那段 5 張圖的提示詞跑，配件就會對。之後換角度、做其他走道也都會一致。

**2. 不想改模型：局部重畫（上一則的做法）**

用配件特寫當參考，分左右兩側局部重畫近景柱子。這段提示詞明確寫了「把灰盒那支大角**換掉**」，不受灰盒限制。

**3. 想用三視圖的話：拼成一張「設定稿」，取代 IMAGE 3、IMAGE 4**

把正面、側面、3/4 拼成**一張圖**，再把尖刺環和火盆的特寫放大貼在旁邊，總數維持 5 張不增加。提示詞裡 IMAGE 3、IMAGE 4 那兩段改成：

```text
IMAGE 3 = PILLAR MODEL SHEET: the same pillar in front, side and 3/4
views, plus close-ups of its spike ring and brazier. Use it as the
EXACT design for every pillar's ornaments, seen in each pillar's
perspective: the spike ring is a stone collar with a tall upright
central blade and two upward-curving crescent horns, mirror-symmetric;
the brazier is a wide square-rimmed iron bowl with two tall crescent
horns rising above the flames. These ornament shapes OVERRIDE the
rough horn shapes in the IMAGE 1 greybox.
```

圖片編號要跟著改：定位圖從 IMAGE 5 變成 IMAGE 4，提示詞裡的 `IMAGE 5` 全部改成 `IMAGE 4`，開頭改成 `Four reference images.`。

最後那句 **`OVERRIDE the rough horn shapes in the greybox`** 是關鍵，它明確告訴 AI 配件可以不照灰盒畫。

**我的建議：** 能改模型就走第 1 條，一次解決，之後做其他角度也一致。不想動模型就走第 2 條，只修近景、最穩。第 3 條值得試，但結果不保證。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 07:46 UTC

**有幫助，但只對火盆有用，而且要裁過再用。** 不能整張附上。

## 這張的價值

它的角度跟走道柱子一樣，都是**斜角透視**，剛好示範了火盆從側前方看起來的樣子：碗口變成橢圓，兩支月牙角一前一後，前面那支比較大。前面給的火盆特寫是正面圖，AI 要自己推算透視；這張直接給答案。

## 整張附上的風險

| 內容 | 問題 |
|---|---|
| **柱頭** | 這裡是**往外掃的大獠牙加小尖刺**，跟你定稿的「中間直立刀刃 + 兩側月牙角」不一樣。剛才結果裡那支往外伸的大角，就很像這張的柱頭。整張附上，等於在教 AI 畫錯的版本 |
| 鐵鍊、熔岩、破牆 | 之前特別排除的東西，附上去可能又會長回來 |
| 解析度 | 只有 263×724，太小 |

所以我只把**火盆那一塊裁下來**（上面送出的檔案）。柱頭照舊用你定稿的尖刺環特寫。

## 用在局部重畫（推薦）

上一則的左右局部重畫，改成上傳 4 張：

1. IMAGE 1 = `corridor_crop_left.png`（或 right）
2. IMAGE 2 = `ref_spike_ring.png`（柱頭，正面）
3. IMAGE 3 = `ref_brazier.png`（火盆，正面）
4. IMAGE 4 = `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/ref_brazier_persp.png`（火盆，透視）

提示詞的改法：

- 開頭改成 `Four reference images.`
- `IMAGE 3 = EXACT DESIGN of the brazier.` 那句後面加：

```text
IMAGE 4 = the SAME brazier seen at an angle — use it to see how the
bowl and its two crescent horns look in perspective (oval rim, the
near horn larger than the far horn). Take ONLY the brazier from it —
ignore its chain, lava and stone.
```

其他段落不變。

## 如果要用在整張 5 張圖的生成

就把它取代 IMAGE 4（無火盆柱），總數維持 5 張，IMAGE 4 的說明換成上面那段。不過還是一樣，**灰盒裡那支大角的形狀會蓋過參考圖**，柱頭不一定修得好。柱頭要對，還是得在 3ds Max 改灰盒的造型，或用局部重畫處理。

```text
Three reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE AND COMPOSITION AUTHORITY: a 3D greybox render
of a long gothic cathedral corridor in one-point perspective — vaulted
ceiling, upper balconies, arched windows, a tiled floor and two rows
of dark square stone pillars. The pillars' shape, thickness, horns and
brazier bowls are defined by IMAGE 1. The white archway at the far end

and the volcano with a lavafall. IGNORE its pillars, chains and broken

blocks, spike ring and brazier design. It is a flat front view — do

PILLARS: paint every pillar as a solid square charcoal stone pillar
like IMAGE 3, keeping both visible faces from IMAGE 1 (front face and
the side face toward the corridor), with stone blocks wrapping around
the corner. Near the top, a spike ring like IMAGE 3: a stone collar
with a tall upright central blade and two upward-curving crescent
horns. Dark, never pale.

BRAZIERS: ONLY where IMAGE 1 has a bowl — exactly six, three per side,
on every second pillar, hanging on the side facing the corridor
centre. Each is a wide iron bowl with two tall crescent horns rising
above a burning fire, like IMAGE 3. The two nearest large pillars and
all pillars without a bowl in IMAGE 1 have NO brazier and NO fire.

THEME: blue stone becomes dark charcoal stone with chipped edges;
windows glow molten orange; railings become dark iron; the floor is
dark cracked stone with only a few thin lava cracks near the far end.
Low-key: mostly deep shadow, lit by the far opening, the braziers and
the windows.

FAR OPENING: a distant red smoky sky with a volcano and a glowing
lavafall like IMAGE 2 — the brightest point.

LOCK: keep IMAGE 1's exact camera, perspective, architecture and
every pillar's position and size. Do not crop or extend. No chains,
rubble or broken roof. Painterly 3D game art, not pixel art.
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 07:53 UTC

我把灰盒和結果的柱子下段並排比對過了（第一張對照圖）。

## 哪裡不一樣

| 灰盒 | 結果 |
|---|---|
| 腰線以下是**切角（八角）柱身**，側面看得到 45° 斜切面 | 變成普通方柱，切角不見了 |
| 柱腳是**往外擴的切角底座**，一層一層往外放大 | 變成一個方石塊，沒有往外擴 |
| 欄杆**收在柱子側面**，不會穿過柱子 | 最左邊大柱子下段被欄杆擋到、跟欄杆混在一起 |

原因跟之前一樣：整張圖一起畫，柱子下段在畫面裡太小，AI 就簡化成方塊。用文字改整張圖也很難修好。

## 解法：只重畫近景柱子的下段

我已經準備好裁切圖，**結果跟灰盒都裁同一個範圍**，並放大 2 倍。左右各跑一次，每次上傳 2 張：

1. IMAGE 1 = 結果裁切：`/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/lower_left_result.png`（右側換 `lower_right_result.png`）
2. IMAGE 2 = 灰盒裁切：`/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/lower_left_greybox.png`（右側換 `lower_right_greybox.png`）

設定：Nano Banana 2、Enhance None、Style None，Dimensions 跟裁切圖同比例（1120×920）。

```text
Two reference images of the SAME crop of a gothic lava-fortress
corridor, seen in perspective.
IMAGE 1 = the BASE: the finished painted version. Its style, colours,
lighting, fire, windows, railings and floor are final.
IMAGE 2 = the STRUCTURE AUTHORITY: the 3D greybox of the same crop.
The pillars' lower sections in IMAGE 1 are wrong — rebuild them to
match IMAGE 2 exactly.

Fix the lower part of each stone pillar (from the moulded ring down to
the floor) so its geometry matches IMAGE 2:
- Below the moulded ring the shaft has CHAMFERED corners — the corners
  are cut at 45 degrees, so in perspective you see the front face, a
  narrow angled chamfer face, and the side face, exactly as shaded in
  IMAGE 2.
- The base FLARES OUTWARD in stepped, chamfered tiers down to the
  floor, wider than the shaft, exactly like IMAGE 2.
- The railings stop against the SIDE of each pillar — they never pass
  in front of a pillar or overlap it.
Paint the rebuilt parts with the same charcoal stone blocks, chipped
edges, warm firelight and purple-brown shadows as the rest of IMAGE 1.

LOCKS: everything above the moulded ring, the braziers and fire, the
windows, walls, railings and floor stay exactly as in IMAGE 1. Same
perspective and framing. Do not crop, zoom or extend.
```

## 貼回

1. 結果縮回一半（1120×920 → 560×460）
2. 貼回原圖：左側對齊 **x=0、y=440**，右側對齊 **x=1440、y=440**（原圖是 2000×1109）
3. 遮罩只取柱子下段，羽化 2px

**中遠景的柱子**比較小，切角看不太出來，通常可以不修。

**之後要做其他角度的話**，這類比例和結構的細節，最根本的解法還是在灰盒那一步就用局部特寫輸出。你也可以考慮讓 3ds Max 灰盒的切角**更明顯**，例如加深斜切面的明暗，AI 會更容易照著畫。

```text
Four reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE AND STRUCTURE AUTHORITY: a 3D greybox render of
a long gothic cathedral corridor in one-point perspective — vaulted
ceiling, upper balconies, arched windows, a tiled floor and two rows
of dark stone pillars. Every pillar's exact geometry — its faces,
chamfers, mouldings, base tiers, horns and brazier bowls — is defined
by IMAGE 1. The white archway at the far end is an EMPTY OPENING to be
filled.
IMAGE 2 = MOOD REFERENCE ONLY: hellish lava atmosphere, colour grade
and the volcano with a lavafall. IGNORE its pillars, chains and broken
roof.
IMAGE 3 = PILLAR MATERIAL REFERENCE ONLY: stacked charcoal stone
blocks, spike ring and brazier design. It is a flat front view — take
its materials and ornaments, NOT its shape or where its brazier sits.
IMAGE 4 = BRAZIER PLACEMENT MAP: the same scene as IMAGE 1 with six
orange boxes. The orange lines must NOT appear in the output.

PILLARS — follow IMAGE 1's geometry exactly, top to bottom:
- UPPER SHAFT: a solid square pillar; both visible faces (front face
  and the side face toward the corridor), with stone blocks wrapping
  around the corner.
- SPIKE RING near the top, like IMAGE 3: a stone collar with a tall
  upright central blade and two upward-curving crescent horns.
- MOULDED RING: a projecting stone ledge part-way down, as in IMAGE 1.
- LOWER SHAFT below that ring: CHAMFERED corners cut at 45 degrees —
  in perspective you see the front face, a narrow angled chamfer face
  and the side face, exactly as shaded in IMAGE 1. NOT a plain square
  block.
- BASE: flares OUTWARD in stepped, chamfered tiers down to the floor,
  wider than the shaft, exactly as in IMAGE 1.
- The railings stop against the SIDE of each pillar — they never pass
  in front of a pillar or overlap it.
- Material: charcoal stone blocks with chipped edges and dark mortar
  gaps, like IMAGE 3. Dark, never pale.

BRAZIERS — follow IMAGE 4 exactly: exactly SIX, one inside each orange
box, three per side, on every second pillar, hanging on the side
facing the corridor centre — seen in perspective, NOT facing the
camera. Each is a wide iron bowl with two tall crescent horns rising
above a burning fire, like IMAGE 3. No red gem. The two nearest large
pillars and every pillar without a box have NO brazier and NO fire.

THEME: blue stone becomes dark charcoal stone with chipped edges;
windows glow molten orange; railings become dark iron; the floor is
dark cracked stone with only a few thin lava cracks near the far end.
Low-key: mostly deep shadow, lit by the far opening, the braziers and
the windows.

FAR OPENING: a distant red smoky sky with a volcano and a glowing
lavafall like IMAGE 2 — the brightest point.

LOCK: keep IMAGE 1's exact camera, perspective, architecture and
every pillar's position, size and silhouette. Do not crop or extend.
No chains, rubble or broken roof. Painterly 3D game art, not pixel
art.
```

**跟上一版的差別：**

- IMAGE 1 改成「**結構權威**」，柱子的切角、線腳、柱腳層數都以灰盒為準。
- IMAGE 3 只取材質和裝飾，**不取形狀**。上一版柱子變成方塊，就是照了這張正面圖的方柱。
- PILLARS 段**從上到下逐段寫出結構**，包括下段的切角、往外擴的柱腳、欄杆收在柱子側面。
- 加上 IMAGE 4 定位火盆。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 07:59 UTC

同意。柱子一直畫錯，根本原因是 AI 手上只有一張**正面圖**，側面、切角、柱腳都要自己猜。給它一份**跟灰盒一致的三視圖設定稿**，每個角度都有答案，就不用再猜了。

不過三視圖有兩種做法，結果差很多：

| 做法 | 結果 |
|---|---|
| ❌ 從正面圖叫 AI 想像側面 | 側面還是 AI 猜的，切角、柱腳不一定跟灰盒一樣，還是會亂 |
| ✅ **3ds Max 灰盒出三個角度，再讓 AI 上材質** | 形狀 100% 跟灰盒一致，AI 只負責畫材質。之前正面牆就是這樣做成功的 |

**建議走第二種。**

## 第 1 步：3ds Max 輸出灰盒柱子（不需要打光技巧）

只要兩種柱子：**有火盆**一根、**無火盆**一根。

1. 選一根柱子，按 **Alt+Q**（Isolate Selection，只顯示這根）
2. 視窗背景設成**綠色**（Viewport → Configure Viewport Background → Solid Color，RGB 0,177,64）
3. 分別切到這三個角度截圖（用 Grab Viewport 或直接截圖）：
   - **Front**（火盆朝向你）
   - **Right**（側面）
   - **Perspective 3/4**：轉到大約 35° 斜角、稍微俯視
4. 著色模式用現在走道那種（有明暗的預設著色），讓切角和層次看得出來

兩種柱子各 3 張，共 6 張。

## 第 2 步：AI 逐張上材質

每張跑一次，上傳 2 張：

1. IMAGE 1 = 灰盒柱子截圖（某一個角度）
2. IMAGE 2 = 你定稿的柱子正面圖（有火盆或無火盆那張）

設定：Nano Banana 2、Enhance None、Style None，比例跟截圖一樣。

```text
Two reference images.
IMAGE 1 = GEOMETRY AUTHORITY: a 3D greybox of a stone pillar, seen
from one angle on a green background. Its exact silhouette, faces,
chamfers, mouldings, base tiers, horns and brazier bowl are FINAL —
follow them exactly, including the camera angle.
IMAGE 2 = MATERIAL AND ORNAMENT REFERENCE: the finished pillar design
in front view. Take its materials and ornament details, NOT its shape.

Repaint the greybox pillar of IMAGE 1 in the design of IMAGE 2:
- Charcoal stone blocks with chipped edges and dark mortar gaps,
  wrapping around every visible face and continuing across corners
  and chamfers.
- The horn shapes near the top become IMAGE 2's spike ring: a stone
  collar with a tall upright central blade and two upward-curving
  crescent horns.
- [有火盆版] The bowl becomes IMAGE 2's brazier: a wide iron bowl with
  two tall crescent horns rising above a burning fire. No red gem.
- Same painterly hand-painted 3D style; warm orange light on one side,
  purple-brown shadow on the other.

LOCK: keep IMAGE 1's exact silhouette, proportions, camera angle and
every structural part — do not simplify the chamfered lower shaft or
the stepped base. Complete pillar, nothing cropped, same flat solid
chroma-green (#00B140) background.
```

無火盆版就把 `[有火盆版]` 那一行刪掉。

## 第 3 步：PS 拼成設定稿

每種柱子拼成**一張橫向的設定稿**：正面｜側面｜3/4，再把柱頭尖刺環和火盆的放大特寫貼在旁邊。最後會有 **2 張設定稿**（有火盆、無火盆）。

## 第 4 步：回到走道

走道提示詞照用上一版 4 張圖的版本，只把 IMAGE 3 換成**有火盆的設定稿**，並把 IMAGE 3 那段改成：

```text
IMAGE 3 = PILLAR MODEL SHEET: the exact final pillar in front, side and
3/4 views, plus close-ups of its spike ring and brazier. Every pillar
in the corridor must look exactly like this model sheet, seen from the
angle of its own position in IMAGE 1 — same chamfered lower shaft,
stepped base, spike ring and brazier. Pillars without a box in IMAGE 4
are the same pillar without the brazier.
```

這樣做，之後要產任何角度的走道（橫向、直向、斜角）都能拿同一份設定稿當依據，柱子會一直保持一致。

**先做第 1 步**，把有火盆柱子的 3 張灰盒截圖給我看，我幫你確認角度和清晰度夠不夠，再往下跑。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 08:00 UTC

這張已經是**正面正交**的定稿，所以可以直接當三視圖的「正面」，不用再拆正面，從側面開始做就好。

## 先定義這根柱子

| 部位 | 正面看到的 | 側面要推算的 |
|---|---|---|
| 柱身 | 方柱，大石塊 | **斷面是正方形**：側面寬度 = 正面寬度 |
| 柱頭尖刺環 | 中間直立刀刃 + 兩側月牙角 | 刀刃往**前**凸，兩支角在左右兩側 |
| 火盆 | 掛在正面中段 | 碗從柱子正面往**前**伸出 |
| 柱腳 | 階梯狀往外擴 | 四面都一樣的階梯 |

正面 = **火盆掛的那一面**。

⚠️ 這根柱子的下段是**方柱加紋章**，跟走道灰盒那種**切角柱身**不一樣。如果三視圖要拿去當走道的依據，先決定以誰為準：照這張設計，就把灰盒改成方柱；照灰盒，下段要改成切角。

## 共同設定

- Nano Banana 2、Prompt Enhance None、Style None
- 比例跟這張一樣（768×1376）
- 背景用綠色 **#00B140**，方便去背。這張原本的深咖啡底跟柱子顏色太接近，去背會吃到柱子。

## ① 右側面

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 這張正面圖

```text
IMAGE 1 is the confirmed FRONT orthographic plate of a stone pillar
game asset — the brazier is mounted on its front face. Render the SAME
pillar — identical height, proportions, stone blocks, mouldings,
spike ring, brazier and base — from its RIGHT SIDE, rotated exactly
90 degrees, so the front of the pillar now faces the LEFT of the image.

SHAPE: the shaft has a SQUARE cross-section, so the side view is the
SAME width as the front view, with stone-block courses at the same
heights. The top cap, the spike-ring collar, the moulding under the
brazier and the stepped base keep the same heights and step profile
as in IMAGE 1.
ORNAMENTS IN PROFILE:
- Spike ring: the upright central blade is on the front face, so here
  it projects to the LEFT edge in profile, as a thin pointed wedge.
  The right-hand crescent horn now faces the viewer, rising upward
  from the collar; the left-hand horn is hidden behind the pillar.
- Brazier: the bowl sticks out to the LEFT, seen from the side; its
  two crescent horns overlap in profile, curving up above the fire.
- The carved emblem is only on the front face — the side face is
  plain stone blocks.

Render in TRUE ORTHOGRAPHIC PROJECTION: no perspective, no
foreshortening, camera horizontal at mid-height, perpendicular to the
side face.
FIXED WORLD LIGHT: same as IMAGE 1, now coming from the pillar's front
(the left of this image): front-facing edges catch warm orange light,
the rest of the side face is in deeper purple-brown shadow; the fire
glows on nearby stone. Keep the same dark exposure, do NOT brighten.

Same painterly hand-painted style. Complete pillar, centred, nothing
cropped. Background: one flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) colour.
```

**驗收：** 跟正面並排，用水平線比對**柱頭、尖刺環、火盆、柱腳**，高度要對齊，柱身寬度要一樣。對不上就重跑，先不要往下做。

**左側面不用跑**：右側面水平翻轉就是左側面。

## ② 3/4 立體圖

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 正面，IMAGE 2 = 確認過的側面

```text
IMAGE 1 is the FRONT plate and IMAGE 2 is the SIDE plate of the same
stone pillar. Render the SAME pillar — identical geometry, proportions
and details — as a single 3/4 view: rotated about 35 degrees to show
the front face and the right side face, seen slightly from above so
the top of the cap and the brazier rim are visible. Square shaft,
stone blocks wrapping around the corner. The blade and brazier on the
front face, the crescent horns at the corners. Near-orthographic, no
wide-angle distortion. Same fixed world light and dark exposure as
the plates. Complete pillar, centred, nothing cropped, on one flat
solid chroma-green (#00B140) background.
```

## ③ 背面（選配）

柱子在走道裡看不到背面，**只有要建完整 3D 模型才需要**。

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 正面，IMAGE 2 = 側面

```text
IMAGE 1 is the FRONT plate and IMAGE 2 is the SIDE plate of the same
stone pillar. Render the SAME pillar from the BACK, rotated 180
degrees: same width, height, cap, mouldings and stepped base. The back
face is plain stacked stone blocks with no emblem; only the tips of
the two crescent horns and the rim of the brazier peek out at the
edges. True orthographic. Backlit: mostly deep purple-brown shadow
with a thin warm rim on the outline. Complete pillar, centred, on one
flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) background.
```

## ④ 去光版（只有要餵 image-to-3D 才做）

每一張各跑一次：

```text
IMAGE 1 is a plate of a stone pillar in [FRONT / RIGHT SIDE / 3/4 /
BACK] view. Keep its EXACT shape, proportions, stone blocks,
mouldings, spike ring and brazier — lighting change only, do NOT
redraw the geometry.

KEEP the real material albedo — the true charcoal stone colour, the
dark iron of the brazier and horns, all surface detail. Do NOT turn it
into grey clay. DE-LIT it: remove all baked lighting — no orange rim
light, no shadow side, no fire glow on the stone. Remove the fire and
leave the bowl empty. Perfectly even, flat diffuse studio lighting,
NO cast shadows, NO highlights.

Centred on a flat neutral grey (#808080) seamless background, nothing
cropped. High-resolution clean de-lit albedo reference plate for
image-to-3D.
```

## 做完以後

把**正面｜側面｜3/4** 拼成一張設定稿，就能當走道提示詞的 IMAGE 3（上一則第 4 步那段）。

**先跑 ① 右側面，結果貼給我**，我幫你跟正面對齊檢查高度和寬度。

方向是對的，但現在比較像「轉了角度的平面圖」，立體感不夠，問題在這幾處：

| 部位 | 現在 | 應該 |
|---|---|---|
| 柱頭頂蓋 | 只比柱身寬一點 | **明顯往外挑出**，看得到頂面，底下有陰影 |

| 刀刃、月牙角 | 貼在柱面上 | **離開柱面往外伸**，根部有陰影 |
| 火盆 | 碗淺、貼著柱子 | 碗往前伸出，**看得到碗的深度**，在柱子上落下影子 |
| 柱腳階梯 | 每層差不多寬 | **一層比一層寬**，每層頂面都看得到 |
| 正面 vs 側面 | 明暗差不多 | 兩個面的明暗要差很多，轉角清楚 |

## 修正提示詞（以這張為底，局部加強）

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 這張 3/4 結果，IMAGE 2 = 正面圖，IMAGE 3 = 側面圖（如果有跑出來）
**設定：** Nano Banana 2、Enhance None、Style None、比例跟這張一樣

```text
IMAGE 1 is the BASE — a 3/4 view of a stone pillar game asset on a
green background. Its design, proportions and camera angle are final.
IMAGE 2 is the FRONT plate and IMAGE 3 is the SIDE plate of the same
pillar. This is a DEPTH and VOLUME pass: make every projecting part
read as truly three-dimensional. Do NOT change the design.

ADD DEPTH:
- TOP CAP: a heavy stone cap that clearly OVERHANGS the shaft on both
  visible faces; its top surface is visible from slightly above, and
  a dark shadow falls on the shaft just below its overhang.
- SPIKE-RING COLLAR: a thick stone ledge projecting well out from the
  shaft; lit top edge, deep shadow on its underside. The central blade
  and the two crescent horns stand OUT from the pillar into the air —
  clearly separated from the stone, with dark contact shadows at
  their roots.
- BRAZIER: the iron bowl projects forward from the front face; show
  the depth of the bowl — the oval rim, the inside of the bowl filled
  with fire, and its outer curved underside. It casts a soft shadow on
  the pillar below it. Its two crescent horns curve up in 3D, the near
  horn larger than the far one.
- MOULDING under the brazier: projecting ledge with a visible top
  face and a shadowed underside.
- STEPPED BASE: each tier noticeably WIDER than the one above it, with
  every tier's top surface visible from above and a shadow under each
  step.
- FACES: strong contrast between the two visible faces — the front
  face lit with warm orange light, the side face in deeper purple-
  brown shadow — with a crisp vertical corner edge. Ambient occlusion
  in every inner corner and mortar gap.

LOCK: same pillar, same height, same proportions, same stone blocks,
emblem and ornament designs, same camera angle. Complete pillar,
centred, nothing cropped, same flat solid chroma-green (#00B140)
background.
```

## 如果還是不夠

立體感最可靠的來源是**灰盒**（之前反覆驗證過）。在 3ds Max 把這根柱子做成簡單的灰盒：方柱、挑出的頂蓋、石托、階梯柱腳，配件用簡單幾何體代替就好。用同樣的 3/4 角度截圖，再跑上一則的「灰盒上材質」提示詞。形狀和深度由 3D 決定，AI 只負責畫材質，就不會每次都畫得太扁。

## 提示詞

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 第一版 3/4 結果
**設定：** Nano Banana 2、Enhance None、Style None、比例跟原圖一樣

```text
IMAGE 1 is a 3/4 view of a stone pillar game asset on a green
background. This is a GEOMETRY-ONLY edit: make the pillar DEEPER
front-to-back. Change nothing else.

- The right-hand side face of the pillar becomes about 40 percent
  WIDER, so the pillar reads as a thick, solid square block, not a
  thin slab. The cap, the spike-ring collar, the moulding under the
  brazier and the stepped base all extend deeper along that side face
  to match.
- The stone blocks on the side face continue in the same pattern,
  receding in the same 3/4 perspective.

LOCK — keep EXACTLY as in IMAGE 1:
- the lighting, light direction, colours, brightness, contrast and
  exposure — do NOT relight, do NOT brighten or warm up anything;
- the front face, its stone blocks and the carved emblem on the lower
  shaft;
- the spike blade, crescent horns, brazier, fire and their positions;
- the camera angle, pillar height and the flat solid chroma-green
  (#00B140) background. Nothing cropped.
```

## 更穩的做法：PS 直接拉寬側面（約 3 分鐘）

這只是幾何上的拉伸，PS 做得比 AI 準，光線也完全不會變：

1. 用**多邊形套索**選取柱子**右側面**，從頂蓋到柱腳整條，沿著轉角線選。
2. **Ctrl+T**，往右水平拉寬約 **130–150%**，左邊緣貼齊轉角不要動。
3. 頂蓋、石托、柱腳的右端會跟著變寬，這正是要的效果。
4. 右側的月牙角如果被拉變形，用原圖那支角另外貼回去。
5. 拉伸後露出的綠色空隙用綠色補滿，邊緣羽化 1px。

我比較推薦 PS：形狀改變很單純，用 AI 每跑一次都可能動到光線和細節。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 08:22 UTC

光線這次保住了，側面也確實變寬了，**但拉過頭了**。

## 檢查

| 項目 | 結果 |
|---|---|
| 光線、顏色、曝光 | ✅ 跟原圖一樣，沒有被改 |
| 正面、紋章、火盆、尖刺 | ✅ 都在原位 |
| 頂蓋、石托、柱腳跟著延伸 | ✅ 有做到 |
| 側面寬度 | ⚠️ **比正面還寬**，柱子變成前後很長的**長方石牆**，不是方柱了 |

方柱轉 35° 時，側面應該**比正面略窄**，大約是正面的 0.7–0.9 倍。現在側面大約是正面的兩倍。這也會跟正面圖、側面圖和走道灰盒的方柱對不上，之後當設定稿會出問題。

## 修法

**PS 壓窄（推薦，約 2 分鐘，光線不會變）**

1. 用多邊形套索選取**右側面**，從頂蓋到柱腳整條。
2. **Ctrl+T**，左邊緣貼齊轉角不動，往左水平壓窄到**約 55–60%**，讓側面寬度大約是正面的 0.8 倍。
3. 多出來的地方補綠色，邊緣羽化 1px。

這張的石塊、頂蓋、柱腳延伸都畫得很好，壓窄後就是比例正確的方柱，比上一版更有厚度。

**或用 AI 重跑**：以第一版當底，把提示詞第一點的 `about 40 percent WIDER` 改成：

```text
slightly wider — about 15 percent — so its width is about 0.8 times
the front face width; it must stay a SQUARE pillar, NOT a long
rectangular wall
```

不過 AI 對百分比的拿捏很不穩，就像這次說 40% 卻畫成兩倍，所以 PS 壓窄比較可靠。

好，這張可以用。光線、正面、紋章、火盆都沒動，只有側面加深了，這正是要的結果。

## 要注意：柱子現在不是正方柱了

這次加深得比原本說的 40% 多：側面大約是正面的 **2 倍寬**，所以柱子現在是**前後很深的長方柱**，像一面石牆垛，不是正方柱。

這樣也可以，比較厚重。但如果你接受這個比例，**側面圖也要照這個深度畫**。之前的側面提示詞寫的是「側面寬度 = 正面寬度」，現在要改成側面約為正面的 2 倍，不然三視圖會對不起來。

如果你要的其實是正方柱，就把這張的側面在 PS 水平壓窄大約一半。

## 側面圖要配合重跑

**上傳：** IMAGE 1 = 正面圖，IMAGE 2 = 這張加深後的 3/4 圖
**設定：** Nano Banana 2、Enhance None、Style None、比例 **9:16**（側面變寬了，可以設 864×1376 這類稍寬的比例）

```text
IMAGE 1 is the FRONT orthographic plate and IMAGE 2 is the 3/4 view of
the same stone pillar. IMAGE 2 shows its true depth: the pillar is a
deep rectangular block, about TWICE as deep front-to-back as it is
wide. Render the SAME pillar from its RIGHT SIDE, rotated exactly 90
degrees, so its front (with the brazier) faces the LEFT of the image.

SHAPE: in this side view the pillar is about twice as wide as in
IMAGE 1, matching the long side face seen in IMAGE 2 — same stone-block
pattern, same cap overhang, collar, moulding and stepped base. All
parts at the same heights as IMAGE 1.
ORNAMENTS IN PROFILE: the spike blade and the brazier bowl are on the
front face, so they stick out past the LEFT edge in profile; the
right-hand crescent horn rises at the front-left corner of the collar.
The side face itself has no ornaments and no emblem.

TRUE ORTHOGRAPHIC: no perspective, camera horizontal at mid-height,
perpendicular to the side face. Keep the same lighting, colours and
dark exposure as IMAGE 2's side face — do NOT relight or brighten.
Complete pillar, centred, nothing cropped, on one flat solid
chroma-green (#00B140) background.
```

## 之後

1. 側面跑好後，跟正面並排，用水平線檢查各部位的高度有沒有對齊。
2. 把**正面｜側面｜3/4（加深版）**拼成一張設定稿。
3. 拿設定稿當走道的 IMAGE 3。走道灰盒裡的柱子如果比較薄，也可以順便在 3ds Max 把柱子拉深，讓灰盒跟設定稿一致。灰盒跟參考圖一致，AI 就不會在兩者之間猶豫。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 08:26 UTC

這組 4 張可以用。提示詞的重點是讓 AI 搞清楚：**設定稿的哪一面，對應走道裡的哪一面**。

## 面的對應關係

| 設定稿 | 走道裡 |
|---|---|
| **窄的正面**（掛火盆、有刀刃和紋章） | 柱子**朝走道中間**那一面 |
| **深的側面**（大片石塊，無裝飾） | 柱子**朝鏡頭**那一面，也就是畫面上比較寬的那一面 |

這跟灰盒是一致的：灰盒的火盆碗掛在朝走道那一側，朝鏡頭那一面比較寬。把對應關係寫清楚，AI 就不會把火盆畫到朝鏡頭的那一面。

## 上傳順序

1. 灰盒
2. 氣氛圖
3. 柱子設定稿（正面 + 3/4 加深版）
4. 火盆定位圖

設定：Nano Banana 2、Prompt Enhance None、Style None、Dimensions 3840×2128

## 提示詞

```text
Four reference images.
IMAGE 1 = COMPOSITION AUTHORITY: a 3D greybox render of a long gothic
cathedral corridor in one-point perspective — vaulted ceiling, upper
balconies, arched windows, a tiled floor and two rows of dark stone
pillars. It defines the camera, the architecture, and each pillar's
position and height. The white archway at the far end is an EMPTY
OPENING to be filled.
IMAGE 2 = MOOD REFERENCE ONLY: hellish lava atmosphere, colour grade
and the volcano with a lavafall. IGNORE its pillars, chains and broken
roof.
IMAGE 3 = PILLAR MODEL SHEET: the exact final pillar, in front view
(left) and 3/4 view (right). It is a DEEP rectangular stone block — its
side face is about TWICE as wide as its narrow front face. Every pillar
in the corridor must look exactly like this model sheet, seen from its
own position. Its design OVERRIDES the rough ornament and base shapes
of the IMAGE 1 greybox.
IMAGE 4 = BRAZIER PLACEMENT MAP: IMAGE 1 with six orange boxes. The
orange lines must NOT appear in the output.

PILLAR ORIENTATION — map IMAGE 3 onto each pillar like this:
- The NARROW FRONT FACE of IMAGE 3 (with the spike blade, the brazier
  and the carved emblem) faces the CENTRE OF THE CORRIDOR.
- The DEEP SIDE FACE of IMAGE 3 (plain stacked stone blocks) faces the
  CAMERA — it is the wide face of each pillar seen in IMAGE 1.
- Keep both faces visible in perspective, stone blocks wrapping around
  the corner; the heavy cap, the spike-ring collar with its upright
  blade and two upward crescent horns, the moulding and the stepped
  base exactly as in IMAGE 3. Dark charcoal stone, never pale.

BRAZIERS — follow IMAGE 4: exactly SIX, one inside each orange box,
three per side, on every second pillar, on the narrow face toward the
corridor centre, seen in perspective. Each is IMAGE 3's brazier: a
wide iron bowl with two tall crescent horns rising above a burning
fire. No red gem. Every pillar without a box — including the two
large nearest pillars — is the same pillar with NO brazier and NO
fire.

THEME: blue stone becomes dark charcoal stone with chipped edges;
windows glow molten orange; railings become dark iron, stopping
against the side of each pillar; the floor is dark cracked stone with
only a few thin lava cracks near the far end. Low-key: mostly deep
shadow, lit by the far opening, the braziers and the windows.

FAR OPENING: a distant red smoky sky with a volcano and a glowing
lavafall like IMAGE 2 — the brightest point.

LOCK: keep IMAGE 1's exact camera, perspective, vanishing point,
architecture, and every pillar's position and height. Do not crop or
extend. No chains, rubble or broken roof. Painterly 3D game art, not
pixel art.
```

## 要注意

灰盒裡的柱子比設定稿**薄**，所以 AI 可能會被灰盒拉回去，畫出比較薄的柱子。提示詞裡已經寫了「設定稿優先於灰盒」，但如果跑出來柱子還是太薄，最根本的解法是在 3ds Max 把柱子**拉深**，跟設定稿一樣是 2 倍深，再 render 一次灰盒。灰盒跟設定稿一致，就不會互相打架。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:14 UTC

兩張都比之前進步很多：柱子變成厚實的深石塊，石塊會轉過轉角，柱腳也清楚了。兩張逐項比對如下：

| 項目 | 第 1 張 | 第 2 張 |
|---|---|---|
| 火盆數量 | ✅ **6 個**，位置對 | ❌ **8 個**：最近兩根大柱子也多了火盆 |
| 最近兩根柱子 | ✅ 沒有火盆 | ❌ 有火盆 |
| 柱子厚度、石塊 | ✅ | ✅ |
| 盡頭 | ✅ 火山 + 熔岩瀑布 | ⚠️ 只有火山，沒有熔岩瀑布 |
| 地面 | ⚠️ 中間多了幾灘熔岩 | ✅ 比較乾淨 |
| 柱頭尖刺環 | ⚠️ 近處大柱子還是一支大角往外伸，沒有中間直立的刀刃 | ⚠️ 同樣問題 |

**建議用第 1 張當定稿底圖。** 剩下兩個小地方：

1. **地面熔岩坑**：如果不要，用 PS 仿製印章從旁邊的地磚蓋掉，或者把第 2 張的乾淨地面用遮罩貼過來。兩張構圖一樣，對齊後就能直接換。
2. **近處兩根大柱子的柱頭**：灰盒那支大角還是蓋過了設定稿。這只影響近景兩根，用之前準備的局部重畫處理就好：用左右兩側的近景裁切圖，加上 `ref_spike_ring.png`，跑「ORNAMENT REPLACEMENT」那段提示詞。不過底圖要換成**這次第 1 張的結果**重新裁切。要的話，我幫你從這張重新裁好。

如果你覺得柱頭這樣也可以，那這張走道就算完成了。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:15 UTC

對，**這個 skill 沒有 GPT 生圖的專門章節**。我查了一下，裡面只有 `model-picker.md` 有幾行 GPT-4o 的簡介（擅長寫實、圖內文字、指令跟隨；弱點是局部編輯會動到其他區域），沒有提示詞寫法、參數或踩坑紀錄。

這次所有提示詞都是照 **Nano Banana** 的習慣寫的，例如「IMAGE 1 / IMAGE 2 角色標籤」、「LOCK 鎖句」、「這是重打光不是重畫」這類定性句。這些都是 NB 實測過有效的寫法，GPT 不一定吃。

## GPT 用這套提示詞容易不準的原因

| 原因 | 說明 |
|---|---|
| **畫面比例** | GPT 生圖的輸出尺寸只有固定幾種（以我知道的版本是方形、橫向 3:2、直向 2:3），你的圖是 16:9 或 1.9:1，它會**重新構圖**，透視和柱子位置就跑掉了。這可能是最大的原因 |
| **整張重畫** | GPT 比較傾向「看懂後重新畫一張」，不太會逐像素保留原圖，所以 GEOMETRY LOCK 這類鎖句效果比 NB 弱 |
| **多圖分工** | 「IMAGE 3 只取材質、IMAGE 4 只看位置」這種精細分工，GPT 比較容易混在一起理解 |
| **自帶風格** | 容易自動提亮、修得比較乾淨，跟我們要的低調暗色調相反 |

## 建議

1. **這類「鎖構圖、灰盒上材質」的工作，繼續用 Nano Banana**。這次走道的結果也證明它最穩。
2. GPT 比較適合**概念發想**，或者**不需要對位**的單張設計，例如畫一根新柱子的造型稿。

如果你想讓 GPT 也能用，我可以做兩件事：

- **A.** 把目前的走道提示詞改寫成 GPT 版：比例設成橫向、提示詞改短改口語，對位交給 PS 合成。
- **B.** 上網查 GPT 生圖目前的版本、尺寸限制和社群驗證過的寫法，在 skill 裡補一份 `references/gpt-image.md`，以後就有依據。

要做哪一個？

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:16 UTC

這張 GPT 的結果其實**很準**，跟 Nano Banana 那張差不多。我上一則說「GPT 用這套提示詞容易不準」說得太武斷了，這次實測推翻了那個判斷。

## 檢查結果

| 項目 | GPT 這張 | NB 第 1 張 |
|---|---|---|
| 構圖、透視 | ✅ 跟灰盒一致 | ✅ |
| 火盆 | ✅ 6 個，位置對，朝走道中間 | ✅ |
| 最近兩根柱子 | ✅ 沒有火盆 | ✅ |
| 柱子厚度、石塊 | ✅ 很厚實 | ✅ |
| 盡頭 | ✅ 火山 + 熔岩瀑布 | ✅ |
| 地面 | ⚠️ **濕亮反光**，像積水的石板 | ⚠️ 中間有幾灘熔岩 |
| 紋章位置 | ⚠️ 刻在**朝鏡頭那一面**（應該在朝走道那面） | — |
| 柱頭尖刺環 | ⚠️ 近處大柱子仍是一支大角，沒有刀刃 | ⚠️ 同樣問題 |
| 整體色調 | 更暗、對比更強，比較接近「暗中有亮點」 | 偏暖亮一些 |

**兩個模型都沒能修好柱頭尖刺環。** 這證實了問題出在**灰盒那支大角的形狀**，不是哪個模型的問題。要根治，只能在 3ds Max 改灰盒的柱頭，或用局部重畫。

## 選哪張

- 想要**更暗、更有氣氛** → GPT 這張
- 想要**地面乾燥、不反光** → NB 第 1 張

兩張構圖幾乎一樣，也可以在 PS 合成：用 GPT 這張當底，地面換成 NB 那張的。

地面反光如果不想要，下次在 THEME 段加一句：
`the floor is dry matte stone — no wet sheen, no reflections, no puddles`

## 關於 skill

這次的實測結果值得記下來：**GPT 用同一套 4 圖提示詞，構圖、配置、色調都能做到跟 NB 同級。** 如果你要，我可以把這次走道的成功配方，加上 NB 和 GPT 的對照結果，一起整理進案例紀錄，並在 skill 裡補上 GPT 生圖的章節。

### 🧑 使用者 · 2026-11-09 09:17 UTC

柱子結構完全不一樣

---

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:17 UTC

對，問題在這裡：**AI 照灰盒的柱子結構畫，設定稿只拿來當材質參考**。NB 和 GPT 兩個模型都一樣，所以這不是提示詞能修的。

## 為什麼

| | 設定稿的柱子 | 灰盒的柱子 |
|---|---|---|
| 分段 | 頂蓋 → 尖刺環 → 長柱身 → 火盆 → 線腳 → 紋章段 → 階梯柱腳 | 頂蓋 → 一支大角 → 柱身 → 中段大腰線 → 下段方塊 → 切角柱腳 |
| 比例 | 細長，頂蓋和柱腳約是柱身的 1.7 倍寬 | 較粗，分段位置也不一樣 |

提示詞裡有一個矛盾：一邊說「照設定稿」，一邊說「鎖住灰盒的柱子位置和高度」。AI 在透視圖裡，只能沿著灰盒的輪廓和明暗畫，很難把一根結構完全不同的柱子「轉角度」塞進去。所以**灰盒的形狀永遠會贏**。正面牆那次「柱子完全不像」也是同一個原因。

## 唯一穩定的解法：讓灰盒的柱子符合設定稿

在 3ds Max 用簡單的方塊，把柱子照設定稿重新拼一根，不需要細節。我從設定稿量出了比例：

**高度分段（從上往下，佔柱子總高的百分比）**

| 部位 | 位置 | 形狀 |
|---|---|---|
| 頂蓋 | 0–9% | 寬方塊，約柱身 1.7 倍寬 |
| 尖刺環 | 9–27% | 下緣是凸出的石托（22–27%），正面中間一片直立的刀刃，兩側各一支往上彎的角 |
| 上段柱身 | 27–56% | 方柱 |
| 火盆 | 56–67% | 掛在**窄面**（朝走道那面），碗兩側各一支高的月牙角 |
| 線腳 | 68–71% | 凸出的一圈 |
| 下段柱身 | 71–90% | 方柱，窄面有紋章 |
| 柱腳 | 90–100% | 階梯往外擴，約柱身 1.7 倍寬 |

**斷面：** 寬 1，深 2，也就是你定的那個深度。

做法：

1. 照上表用 Box 拼一根，角和刀刃用簡單的錐體代替就好。
2. 做兩個版本：有火盆、無火盆。
3. 取代灰盒裡所有的柱子，位置照舊。
4. 重新 render 灰盒，再做一張火盆定位圖。
5. 用同一段 4 張圖的提示詞跑。

灰盒的形狀跟設定稿一致後，AI 就沒有衝突，柱子結構自然會對。之後換任何角度都能直接用。

## 不想改模型的替代做法

只修**近處 4 根大柱子**：用「裁切 → 生成 → 貼回」，一次一根，把設定稿當參考重畫。中遠景的柱子太小，結構差異看不太出來，可以保留。缺點是每根都要單獨跑，之後換角度也要重做。

**我的建議是改灰盒。** 一根柱子大約 10–15 分鐘就能拼好，可以一次解決，以後也不用再修。拼好後截一張圖給我，我幫你對照設定稿檢查比例。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:18 UTC

我把灰盒和 GPT 結果的左側並排放在一起（上面送出的圖），可以清楚看到：**結果的柱子是照灰盒畫的，不是照設定稿畫的。**

| 部位 | 灰盒 | GPT 結果 | 設定稿 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 柱頭 | 一支往外伸的大角 | **一支大角**（照灰盒） | 中間刀刃 + 兩側月牙角 |
| 中段 | 約 2/3 高處有一圈大腰線 | **同一位置的腰線**（照灰盒） | 尖刺環在上段，火盆在中下段 |
| 下段 | 較寬的切角底座 | 方塊底座 + 紋章 | 細長柱身 + 紋章 + 階梯柱腳 |
| 比例 | 粗 | 粗（照灰盒） | 細長 |

連續兩個模型（NB、GPT）、好幾次重跑，都得到一樣的結果：**灰盒裡的形狀一定會贏過參考圖**。所以繼續改提示詞也不會變。

這也是為什麼之前正面牆那次，**直接把做好的柱子貼進灰盒**之後才成功：灰盒的形狀一對，AI 就照著畫對了。

## 兩條路

**A. 改 3ds Max 灰盒（推薦）**
照上一則的比例表，用方塊把柱子重拼一根，取代灰盒裡的柱子，重新 render。一次解決，之後換角度也都會對。

**B. 不動 3ds Max：在 PS 把設定稿的柱子「貼」進灰盒**
跟正面牆同樣的做法，只是要配合透視：
1. 用設定稿的 3/4 圖（加深版）去背。
2. 貼到灰盒每根柱子的位置，用 **Edit → Transform → Perspective / Distort** 把它扭成配合該柱子的透視角度。
3. 左側的柱子用水平翻轉的版本。
4. 只處理近處 4 根就好，遠處的太小，看不出差異。
5. 貼好後用同一段提示詞跑，這時灰盒裡就已經是正確的柱子了。

B 比較費工，而且透視不會完全準確。如果你會在 3ds Max 裡拼方塊，**A 會比 B 快而且準**。要做哪一個？

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:20 UTC

對，柱子結構對不上，是因為**灰盒裡的柱子跟設定稿是兩根不同的柱子**，AI 每次都照灰盒畫。既然暫時不改 3ds Max，我改用正面牆那次成功的做法：**直接把設定稿的柱子貼進灰盒**，讓輸入圖裡的柱子本來就是對的（上面送出的檔案）。

## 我做了什麼

1. 把設定稿裡的 3/4 柱子去背，另外做一個**無火盆版**。
2. 照灰盒 12 根柱子的位置和高度，逐根縮放貼上：
   - **右排**照原方向貼，窄面（火盆、刀刃）朝左，也就是朝走道中間。
   - **左排**水平鏡射，窄面朝右。
   - **火盆只放在橘框那 6 根**，最近的兩根大柱子沒有。
3. 由遠到近疊放，近的柱子蓋住遠的。

## 這張還不能直接用的地方（交給 AI 收尾）

- 設定稿是**略微俯視**的角度，所以每根柱子都看得到頂面。但走道的相機在柱子中段高度，近處大柱子的頂部應該看到**底面**，不是頂面。
- 最近兩根柱子後面還露出**灰盒的舊大角**。
- 牆面還是藍色。

所以下一步要跑一次**主題翻譯 + 融合**：柱子的結構鎖住，只修透視、光線，牆面換成熔岩風格。

## 上傳

1. IMAGE 1 = `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/corridor_pasted.png`
2. IMAGE 2 = 氣氛圖
3. IMAGE 3 = 柱子設定稿

設定：Nano Banana 2（或 GPT）、Enhance None、Style None、3840×2128

```text
Three reference images.
IMAGE 1 = SOURCE SCENE: a long gothic cathedral corridor in one-point
perspective. Its twelve stone pillars are FINISHED ASSETS pasted in
from IMAGE 3 — their structure is FINAL: heavy cap, spike-ring collar
with an upright blade and two crescent horns, long block shaft,
moulding, lower shaft with a carved emblem, stepped base, and a
horned brazier with fire on exactly six of them. The rest of the
scene (blue walls, ceiling, balconies, windows, floor) is a greybox
to be restyled. The white archway at the far end is an EMPTY OPENING.
IMAGE 2 = MOOD REFERENCE ONLY: hellish lava atmosphere, colour grade
and a volcano with a lavafall. IGNORE its pillars, chains and broken
roof.
IMAGE 3 = PILLAR MODEL SHEET, for detail reference.

PILLARS — keep each pillar's EXACT structure, segments, ornaments,
braziers, position and size from IMAGE 1. Only fix:
- PERSPECTIVE: the camera is at mid-height of the pillars. Pillars
  whose tops are above the camera show the UNDERSIDE of their cap,
  not its top surface; adjust each pillar's cap and ledges to the
  corridor's one-point perspective.
- CLEAN-UP: remove any leftover grey greybox horns or grey pillar
  edges sticking out behind the pasted pillars.
- BLEND: match their colour and lighting to the scene — dark charcoal
  stone, warm firelight on the faces toward the corridor centre and
  the braziers, deep shadow elsewhere; each pillar grounded on the
  floor with a contact shadow.
Do NOT add braziers to pillars that have none.

THEME: blue stone becomes dark charcoal stone with chipped edges;
windows glow molten orange; railings become dark iron, stopping
against the side of each pillar; the floor is dry dark cracked stone
with only a few thin lava cracks near the far end, no wet sheen.
Low-key: mostly deep shadow, lit by the far opening, the six
braziers and the windows.

FAR OPENING: a distant red smoky sky with a volcano and a glowing
lavafall like IMAGE 2 — the brightest point.

LOCK: keep IMAGE 1's exact camera, perspective, architecture and
every pillar's position, size and structure. Do not crop or extend.
No chains, rubble or broken roof. Painterly 3D game art.
```

## 跑完以後

- 柱子結構會比之前接近設定稿很多，因為輸入圖裡的柱子本來就是那個結構。
- 如果 AI 把柱子又改回灰盒的樣子，用之前的老方法收尾：在 PS 把**這張拼貼稿裡的柱子**貼回結果上層，只把頂部透視和光線交給 AI 的版本。
- 這是「先拼貼、再融合」的做法，跟正面牆那次一樣。長期來說，還是改 3ds Max 灰盒最一勞永逸。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:25 UTC

這版主要改一件事：**明確告訴 AI，灰盒裡的柱子只是「位置標記」，形狀要完全捨棄，照設定稿重建**。前幾次失敗的原因，就是提示詞同時要求「照設定稿」和「鎖住灰盒柱子」，兩邊打架時灰盒都會贏。

另外為了讓 GPT 也能用，句子改得比較口語、直接，少用大寫標籤，並把柱子的分段用百分比寫清楚。GPT 比較吃這種具體描述。

## 上傳順序

1. 灰盒
2. 氣氛圖
3. 柱子設定稿（正面 + 3/4）
4. 火盆定位圖

**設定：**
- **Nano Banana**：Enhance None、Style None、3840×2128
- **GPT**：選**橫向（landscape）**，出圖後在 PS 裁成 16:9

## 提示詞

```text
Use four reference images.

Image 1 is a 3D greybox of a long gothic cathedral corridor in
one-point perspective. Use it for the camera, the perspective, the
architecture (vaulted ceiling, balconies, arched windows, railings,
tiled floor) and for WHERE each of the twelve pillars stands and how
tall it is. The grey pillars in Image 1 are only position markers:
ignore their shape completely — ignore their single large horn, their
wide waist band and their chamfered lower block. The white archway at
the far end is an empty opening to fill.

Image 2 is the mood reference: hellish lava atmosphere, colour grade,
and a volcano with a lavafall in the distance. Do not copy its pillars,
chains or broken roof.

Image 3 is the pillar design sheet: the exact pillar to build, shown
in front view (left) and 3/4 view (right). Rebuild every one of the
twelve pillars to look exactly like this pillar, seen from its own
position in the corridor.

Image 4 is Image 1 with six orange boxes marking where the braziers
go. Do not draw the orange boxes.

THE PILLAR (from Image 3), from top to bottom:
- top 10%: a heavy square stone cap, wider than the shaft
- 10–27%: a spike ring — a stone collar with one tall upright pointed
  blade in the middle of the narrow face and one upward-curving
  crescent horn on each side
- 27–56%: a plain shaft of large stacked stone blocks
- 56–67%: the brazier zone (brazier only on the six marked pillars)
- 67–71%: a projecting stone moulding
- 71–90%: lower shaft with a small carved emblem on the narrow face
- bottom 10%: a stepped base that widens toward the floor
The pillar is a deep rectangular block: its long side is about twice
as wide as its narrow face. Dark charcoal stone with chipped edges.

ORIENTATION: on every pillar, the narrow face (blade, brazier, emblem)
faces the centre of the corridor. The long plain side faces the
camera. Both faces are visible, with the stone blocks wrapping around
the corner.

BRAZIERS: exactly six, one in each orange box of Image 4 — three on
each side, on every second pillar, hanging on the narrow face toward
the corridor centre. Each is a wide iron bowl with two tall crescent
horns rising above a burning fire, like Image 3. The two big nearest
pillars and all other pillars have no brazier and no fire.

SCENE STYLE: blue stone becomes dark charcoal stone; the windows glow
molten orange; the railings become dark iron and stop against the
side of each pillar; the floor is dry, matte dark stone with only a
few thin lava cracks near the far end — no wet shine. Keep it dark
and moody: light comes only from the far opening, the six braziers
and the windows. The far opening shows a red smoky sky with a volcano
and a glowing lavafall — the brightest part of the image.

Keep the exact camera, perspective and architecture of Image 1, and
every pillar's position and height. Do not crop or zoom. No chains,
no rubble, no broken roof. Painterly 3D game-art style.
```

## 如果柱子還是對不上

用我上一則做的拼貼稿 `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/corridor_pasted.png` **取代 IMAGE 1**。那張的柱子本來就是設定稿的結構，AI 只需要修透視和光線，成功率高很多。提示詞照用，把 Image 1 那段裡的 `The grey pillars in Image 1 are only position markers...` 改成：

```text
The pillars in Image 1 are already the correct design pasted in —
keep their structure, only fix their perspective and lighting.
```

再不行，就只剩在 3ds Max 把灰盒柱子改成設定稿的分段，這是最根本的解法。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:29 UTC

GPT 生圖的章節已經補進 skill，並推到 `claude/fervent-galileo-x97qm3` 分支。新檔案是 `.claude/skills/ai-media-generator/references/gpt-image.md`，`SKILL.md` 的圖片平台清單和 `model-picker.md` 都加了連結。

有一點要先說：這個雲端環境連不到 OpenAI 官方文件和 Wikipedia，原文一頁都沒讀到。所以版本和尺寸的資料主要來自網路搜尋摘要和第三方部落格，各家說法有出入，檔案裡都標成「未經官方確認」。可以確定的只有你截圖裡 Leonardo 的模型說明，以及我們這次走道的實測結果。

**目前的版本**
- **GPT Image 2.6 Flare**：適合快速迭代、試配置，有網路資料說是 2026-11-04 發布。
- **GPT Image 2.6 Sunburst**：適合細節多的定稿。
- 上一代是 GPT Image 2.5（2026-09）和 GPT Image 2（2026-04）。
- 網路上寫的 API ID 是 `gpt-image-2.6-flare` 和 `gpt-image-2.6-sunburst`。

**尺寸限制**（網路資料，以 GPT Image 2 / 2.5 為主，2.6 可能不同）
- 長寬都要是 **16 的倍數**，比例 **1:3 到 3:1**，最大 **3840×2160**。
- 超過 2560×1440 被標為實驗性。
- 平台上如果選不到跟輸入圖一樣的比例，就選最接近的，出圖後在 PS 裁切。

**參考圖上限**：說法互相衝突，有的說約 4 張，有的說最多 16 張。我們實測 2.6 Flare 上傳 4 張是正常的。

**社群和官方建議的寫法**
1. 參考圖要編號，每張只交代一個用途。沒交代用途的圖，內容會被亂混進結果。
2. 寫清楚哪個元素從哪張圖來、放在哪裡，用左、右、前景、背景這類空間詞。
3. 局部修改用這句：`Change only X. Keep everything else exactly the same.`
4. 一次只改一件事，看完結果再改下一件。
5. 一張圖不要同時追兩個目標。

**我們走道案的實測結論也寫進去了**
- 同一段 4 張圖的提示詞，GPT 2.6 Flare 的構圖和火盆位置跟 Nano Banana 一樣準。之前我說「GPT 不吃這種寫法」是錯的，已經在檔案裡更正。
- 兩個模型的柱子都照灰盒的形狀畫，所以柱子對不上是灰盒的問題，換模型解決不了。
- GPT 會預設把地面畫得濕亮。要乾的地面，就得明寫 `dry, matte floor — no wet shine`。

如果要把官方文件的內容核對進去，需要在環境設定裡允許連 `developers.openai.com`。開放後我可以再查一次，把資料改成有官方出處的版本。

Sources:
- [GPT Image (Wikipedia)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GPT_Image)
- [GPT Image 2.6 overview (OpenArt)](https://openart.ai/blog/gpt-image-2-6-overview/)
- [GPT Image 2.6 Prompt Guide (OpenArt)](https://openart.ai/blog/gpt-image-2-6-prompt-guide/)
- [OpenAI Cookbook: Image generation prompting guide](https://developers.openai.com/cookbook/examples/multimodal/image-gen-models-prompting-guide)
- [OpenAI Academy: Creating images with ChatGPT](https://openai.com/academy/image-generation/)
- [OpenAI image API models (aifreeapi)](https://www.aifreeapi.com/en/posts/openai-image-generation-api-models)
- [GPT Image 2.5 Prompt Guide (fuser)](https://fuser.studio/articles/gpt-image-prompt-guide)
- [ChatGPT image generation in 2026 (datastudios)](https://www.datastudios.org/post/chatgpt-image-generation-capabilities-styles-dimensions-editing-and-api-access-with-gpt-4o)

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:31 UTC

這版照剛補進 skill 的 GPT 寫法整理：每張圖只交代一個用途、全部寫成口語完整句、結構用百分比寫死，並明寫乾燥地面。

**上傳順序：** Image 1 灰盒、Image 2 氣氛圖、Image 3 柱子設定稿、Image 4 火盆定位圖

**設定：**
- 模型選 **GPT Image 2.6 Sunburst**，細節比較好。只是想試配置，可以用 Flare，比較快。
- 比例選**橫向**，最接近 16:9 的那個；出圖後在 PS 裁成 16:9。

```text
I am giving you four images. Each one has a single job.

Image 1 is the layout. It is a 3D greybox of a long gothic cathedral
corridor seen straight down its length. Copy its camera, its
perspective, its vaulted ceiling, balconies, arched windows, railings
and tiled floor exactly. It also shows where the twelve pillars stand
(six on the left, six on the right) and how tall each one is. Use the
grey pillars only for position and height — do not copy their shape,
their big single horn or their chunky lower block. The white arch at
the far end is an empty opening.

Image 2 is the mood. Take only its lava atmosphere, its dark red and
orange colours, and the volcano with a glowing lavafall in the
distance. Do not copy its pillars, chains, rubble or broken roof.

Image 3 is the pillar design. Every one of the twelve pillars must be
this exact pillar, seen in the corridor's perspective. From top to
bottom it has: a heavy square stone cap (top 10%); a stone collar with
one tall upright pointed blade in the middle and one upward-curving
crescent horn on each side (10–27%); a plain shaft of large stacked
stone blocks (27–56%); a brazier zone (56–67%); a projecting stone
ledge (67–71%); a lower shaft with a small carved emblem (71–90%); and
a stepped base that widens at the floor (bottom 10%). The pillar is
deep: its long side is about twice as wide as its narrow face.

Image 4 shows where the braziers go: six orange boxes. Do not draw the
boxes.

How to place the pillars: on every pillar, the narrow face with the
blade and the emblem points toward the middle of the corridor, and
the long plain side faces the viewer. Show both faces, with the stone
blocks wrapping around the corner.

Braziers: draw exactly six, one in each orange box — three on the
left and three on the right, on every second pillar, hanging on the
narrow face toward the middle of the corridor. Each brazier is a wide
iron bowl with two tall crescent horns rising above a burning fire,
like the one in Image 3. The two big pillars closest to the viewer,
and every pillar without a box, have no brazier and no fire.

The rest of the scene: turn the blue stone into dark charcoal stone
with chipped edges. The windows glow molten orange. The railings are
dark iron and stop at the side of each pillar. The floor is dry,
matte dark stone with only a few thin lava cracks near the far end —
no wet shine, no reflections, no puddles. Keep the whole image dark
and moody; light comes only from the far opening, the six braziers
and the windows. Through the far opening, show a red smoky sky with a
volcano and a glowing lavafall; it is the brightest part of the
image.

Do not change the camera, crop or zoom. No chains, no rubble, no
broken roof. Painterly 3D game-art style.
```

**跑完的檢查重點：**
- 柱子有沒有中間的**直立刀刃**，以及兩側往上彎的角
- 刀刃、紋章和火盆有沒有在**朝走道中間的窄面**
- 火盆是不是剛好 6 個，最近兩根大柱子沒有火盆
- 地面是不是乾的、不反光

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:35 UTC

我先承認：這幾輪我一直在**用文字修一個輸入圖的問題**，方向錯了。

## 這次哪裡不對

| 項目 | 要的 | 結果 |
|---|---|---|
| 柱子朝向 | 刀刃、紋章、火盆在**朝走道中間的窄面** | 刀刃和紋章全部**朝向鏡頭** |
| 柱子深度 | 前後很深（2 倍） | 變成一般方柱，正面朝鏡頭 |
| 尖刺環 | 中間一片刀刃 + 兩側彎角 | 變成兩、三片刀刃加彎角，每根都不一樣 |
| 柱頭、柱腳 | 照設定稿分段 | 分段比例自己編的 |
| 火盆 | 6 個 | ✅ 對（這是唯一有輸入圖支撐的部分） |

## 為什麼會這樣

1. **AI 做不到「看著一張設定稿，在腦中把它轉 90° 再放進透視」。** 我寫的「窄面朝走道、長邊朝鏡頭」是 3D 旋轉推理，對生圖模型來說太抽象。它只會照設定稿的樣子，把裝飾面朝向鏡頭畫。
2. **我叫它「不要照灰盒的柱子形狀」，等於拿掉了唯一的幾何依據。** 前幾版柱子至少照灰盒畫，形狀穩定；這版放開之後，模型只能自己編，所以每根都不一樣、比例也亂了。
3. **只有火盆是對的，正好證明了原則。** 火盆有定位圖（輸入圖）指定位置，所以準確；柱子結構只有文字描述，所以一直錯。這個 repo 的 B2、SOP 早就記錄過同一件事：**AI 照輸入圖的幾何畫，文字改不了幾何。** 我應該更早停下來，不該一直改提示詞讓你重跑。

## 接下來：停止改提示詞，改輸入圖

只剩兩條路，都是讓**輸入圖本身就帶著正確的柱子**：

**A. 改 3ds Max 灰盒（最根本，推薦）**
照之前給的分段表，用 Box 拼一根柱子：頂蓋、尖刺環（中間錐體刀刃 + 兩側彎角）、柱身、火盆碗、線腳、紋章段、階梯柱腳，斷面寬 1、深 2，**火盆和刀刃放在朝走道的那一面**。取代 12 根柱子，重新 render。之後用最早那段成功的 4 張圖提示詞跑就好，不用再寫朝向。這也是之後做其他角度的唯一一勞永逸解法。

**B. 用拼貼稿當 Image 1（不改模型，馬上能試）**
`/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/corridor_pasted.png` 裡，設定稿的柱子已經照正確朝向貼到 12 個位置（窄面朝走道、火盆只在 6 根上）。拿它當 Image 1，提示詞只要求**保留柱子、修透視和光線、換牆面風格**，不再讓 AI 推算朝向。缺點是近處柱子頂部的透視不對（看到頂面），要靠 AI 修，修不好就得 PS 收尾。

**我的建議：** 先試 B，因為素材已經準備好、成本最低；如果近景柱子的透視還是不行，就做 A。不論哪條，**都不要再讓 AI 從設定稿推柱子的 3D 朝向**。

要走 B 的話，我把精簡版提示詞（GPT / NB 通用）給你。這次的失敗也值得記進案例，等走道完成，我把「文字無法讓模型旋轉設定稿」這條教訓一起寫進 B14。

### 🧑 使用者 · 2026-11-09 09:38 UTC

為什麼我已經給柱子的3/4圖 還是會算錯

---

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 09:38 UTC

簡單說：**生圖模型不會「建 3D 模型」，它只會「照著圖案畫」。** 你給的 3/4 圖對它來說是一張「柱子長這樣」的圖片，不是一個可以轉角度的 3D 物件。

## 具體卡在哪

**1. 角度不一樣，每根柱子都要重新算**
設定稿只有**一個**角度：稍微俯視、轉 35°。但走道裡 12 根柱子的角度**每根都不同**：近處大柱子幾乎從正側面、而且從下往上看，遠處小柱子則幾乎是正面。要把一張 35° 的圖轉成 12 個不同角度，需要在腦中旋轉 3D 物件，模型做不到。

**2. 相機高度相反**
設定稿是**從上往下看**，所以看得到頂蓋的上面。走道相機在柱子中段，近處柱子的頂蓋應該看到**底面**。設定稿裡根本沒有這個資訊，模型只能猜，或乾脆照抄成看得到頂面。

**3. 左排要鏡射**
設定稿的窄面在左邊，剛好接近右排的朝向；但左排要左右翻過來。模型不會自己可靠地做鏡射。

**4. 「正面圖」把它教錯了**
設定稿左半邊的正面圖，刀刃、紋章、火盆都**朝向觀看者**。模型學到的是「裝飾朝向鏡頭」，所以每根都把裝飾轉向鏡頭（就是這次的結果）。

**5. 幾何只會跟最強的空間訊號走**
四張圖裡，真正有走道透視的只有**灰盒**。參考圖對模型來說主要提供「長相、材質、配件」。兩邊衝突時，形狀照灰盒，外觀照參考圖；叫它不要照灰盒時，它就沒有依據，只能自己編。

## 打個比方

這就像給畫家一張柱子的照片，請他畫出這根柱子站在走廊裡、從另一個角度看的樣子。人類畫家會在腦中轉動柱子再畫；生圖模型比較像拼貼高手，很會把看過的元素畫得自然，但**不會在腦中轉動物件**。

## 所以什麼才有效

**目標角度的形狀要直接出現在輸入圖裡**，模型只負責上材質和光線：

- **3ds Max 灰盒照設定稿改柱子**：每根柱子在正確角度下的形狀由 3D 算好，最準。
- **拼貼稿**：把柱子貼到每個位置，形狀至少大致在正確位置和朝向。

設定稿的用處是告訴模型**材質、配件長什麼樣子**，它沒辦法替模型決定**每根柱子在畫面裡的形狀**。這也是為什麼火盆（有定位圖）一直對，柱子結構（只有參考圖）一直錯。

## 選底圖

已有的結果裡，最接近完成的是這兩張：

| | 優點 | 要修的 |
|---|---|---|
| **NB 第 1 張**（地面有幾灘熔岩那張） | 火盆 6 個全對、柱子厚實、盡頭火山加熔岩瀑布 | 地面熔岩坑 |
| **GPT 2.6 Flare**（暗色調那張） | 火盆全對、氣氛最暗最有張力 | 地面濕亮反光、紋章刻在朝鏡頭那面 |

**建議用 GPT 那張當底**，氣氛比較好；地面問題用 NB 那張補。兩張構圖一樣，可以直接疊。

## 收尾步驟

**1. 地面（PS）**
- 兩張疊在一起，用 Auto-Align 對齊。
- 用遮罩把 NB 那張的地面換到 GPT 這張，熔岩坑用仿製印章蓋掉。
- 不想合成的話，對 GPT 那張跑一次局部提示詞（見下方）。

**2. 調色（PS）**
- 換過的地面用 Curves、Color Balance 對齊 GPT 的暗色調。
- 整體不夠暗：Curves 壓中間調，盡頭、火盆、窗戶用遮罩保留亮度。

**3. 裁切**
- GPT 輸出是橫向 3:2，裁成 **16:9**，上下各裁一點，盡頭拱門保持在中間。

**4. 放大**
- 照 B1 配方：Leonardo Universal Upscaler 2×，creativity 最低。

## 地面局部提示詞（不想手動合成時用）

上傳 1 張：GPT 結果。

```text
Change only the floor. Make it dry, matte dark stone tiles — no wet
shine, no reflections, no puddles. Keep a few thin glowing lava
cracks only near the far end. Keep everything else exactly the same:
the pillars, braziers, fire, walls, windows, ceiling, the far opening
and the colours.
```

## 可以晚點再處理的

- **紋章朝向**：只有近處 4 根看得到，PS 用仿製印章蓋掉，或保留也不影響。
- **近處柱頭**：之後若要修，用之前的局部重畫，配 `ref_spike_ring.png` 當參考。

做完地面和調色後，把結果給我，我幫你做最後驗收。

## 提示詞（GPT / NB 通用）

```text
I am giving you three images. Each one has a single job.

Image 1 is the finished scene: a dark lava-fortress corridor. Keep it
exactly as it is, except for the change below.

Image 2 shows the lamp design: a narrow vertical slot cut into the
stone pillar, with a pointed arch at its top, glowing bright molten
orange-yellow from inside, framed by a thin carved stone border.

Image 3 is Image 1 with six cyan boxes. They mark where the lamps go.
Do not draw the boxes.

Change only this: add one lamp like Image 2 inside each cyan box —
exactly six lamps, on the six pillars that have no brazier. Cut each
lamp into the face of the pillar that faces the middle of the
corridor, following that face's perspective; nearer pillars get
larger lamps, farther pillars smaller ones. Each lamp casts a soft
warm orange glow on the stone right around it, fading quickly.

Keep everything else exactly the same: the pillars' shape, the
braziers and their fire, the walls, windows, ceiling, floor, the far
opening and the overall dark colours. Do not add lamps anywhere
else. Do not crop or zoom.
```

## 跑完以後

- 用 PS 把結果疊在原圖上，**只用遮罩取 6 個燈和周圍的光暈**，其他地方保留原圖。AI 會重畫整張，石材細節可能會稍微跑掉。
- 燈如果太亮、把氣氛搶掉，在 PS 把光暈圖層的不透明度降到 60–70%。
- 某根柱子的燈位置或角度不對，用 PS 從另一根複製燈過來，再用 **Ctrl+T 的透視變形**貼合柱面，比重跑快。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 10:20 UTC

好，用 PS 合成。加燈本來就是確定性的小改動，手動做最快也最準。下面是做法，大約 10 分鐘。

**1. 做一個燈素材**
- 從燈參考圖裁下發光縫，連同石框一起，背景去掉。
- 另外複製一層，加 **Outer Glow**：顏色約 #FF8A30，混合模式 Screen，大小 20–40px，這就是光暈。

**2. 貼到 6 根柱子**
- 每根柱子貼一份，**Ctrl+T → 右鍵 Perspective / Distort**，四個角貼合柱子朝走道的那一面。
- 近的大、遠的小，高度跟同側的火盆對齊。
- 遠處 2 根很小，用一條細亮線加光暈也可以，不需要細節。

**3. 照亮周圍石頭**
- 新增一層，混合模式 **Linear Dodge (Add)** 或 Color Dodge。
- 柔邊筆刷、橘色、低流量，在每個燈周圍的石頭邊緣輕刷一圈，往外漸淡。

**4. 統一**
- 燈太搶眼：降低光暈層的不透明度到 60–70%。
- 把燈的所有圖層群組起來，最後加一層 Curves 微調，讓燈的亮度跟火盆差不多或稍暗，**火盆維持主光源**。

做好後給我看，我幫你做最後驗收。走道完成後，要不要我把正面牆和走道的完整流程整理成案例 B14？

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09 10:21 UTC

燈現在是平貼在柱面上的，外框沒有厚度，發光縫也沒有凹進去的深度。

**只有近處左右兩個大燈看得出厚度**，遠處 4 個太小，加了也看不到，不用處理。所以這次只局部重畫兩個近景燈，不重跑整張。上次整張加燈會失敗，原因之一就是一次要改的地方太多、太小。

我已經把兩個近景燈裁下來並放大 3 倍（上面送出的檔案）。

## 做法 A：AI 局部重畫（左右各跑一次）

**上傳：** Image 1 = `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-01/85c0f990-fe16-5781-afd8-cddc1694aa64/scratchpad/lamp_crop_left.png`（第二次換 `lamp_crop_right.png`）
**設定：** 比例 1:1

```text
This image is a close-up of a dark stone pillar with a glowing lamp
slot on it, next to a lava-lit gothic window. Change only the lamp.

Give the lamp real depth:
- The glowing slot is RECESSED into the stone: you can see its inner
  side walls going back into the pillar, the far inner wall lit bright
  orange, the near inner wall in shadow.
- Around it is a thick carved stone frame with a pointed-arch top that
  PROJECTS out from the pillar face: its front edges catch warm orange
  light from the slot, its outer sides cast a small shadow onto the
  pillar.
- A small stone sill sticks out under the slot, with its top surface
  lit and its underside dark.
- Follow the pillar face's perspective. Keep the same size and
  position, the same molten orange-yellow glow.

Keep everything else exactly the same: the pillar's stone blocks, the
window, the colours and lighting. Do not crop or zoom.
```

**貼回：**
1. 結果縮回 240×240。
2. 左邊貼到 **x=60、y=340**，右邊貼到 **x=1620、y=340**。
3. 遮罩只取燈和燈框，羽化 2px。

## 做法 B：PS 圖層樣式（約 3 分鐘，最穩）

1. 燈框圖層加 **Bevel & Emboss**：Style 選 Outer Bevel、Depth 200%、Size 4–6px、光源角度朝走道盡頭（約 150°），Highlight 用暖橘色。
2. 發光縫圖層加 **Inner Shadow**：Distance 3–5px、Size 4px、顏色深紫褐，模擬凹進去的內壁。
3. 燈框外側加一點 **Drop Shadow**：Distance 2–3px、不透明度約 40%，讓燈框看起來凸出柱面。
4. 調好後，右鍵 **Copy Layer Style**，貼到其他 5 個燈。

**建議：** 先用 B，幾分鐘就能讓 6 個燈都有立體感，而且風格一致。如果近景兩個燈還想要更多細節，例如凹槽內壁、石框的雕刻，再用 A 只補這兩個。

## 定稿後建議

1. **放大**：Leonardo Universal Upscaler 2×，creativity 最低（B1 配方）。
2. **分層輸出**（要進引擎的話）：火盆的火、柱燈光暈、盡頭熔岩瀑布各拆一層。之後在引擎可以做閃爍、bloom，也能讓熔岩流動。
3. **PSD 留檔**：燈和火的圖層不要合併，之後要改顏色、亮度都方便。

## 兩張都完成了

正面牆和直向走道都做完了。這次累積了不少可以重複用的做法和踩坑：

- 灰盒前處理：壓暗柱子、保留明暗、加落影
- 定位圖：用色框指定火盆、裝飾和燈的位置
- 柱子拆解和三視圖，含加深的那一步
- 裁切 → 生成 → 貼回（解決小物件畫不清楚）
- **灰盒幾何會壓過參考設定稿**，以及 AI 沒辦法自己旋轉設定稿
- NB 和 GPT 的實測對照
- 哪些步驟 PS 比 AI 快又準（局部補光、加燈、厚度）

這次有定位圖，又有兩張特寫參考，配件問題用這套最有機會修好。

## 上傳順序

1. Image 1 = 走道定稿（沒有框的那張）
2. Image 2 = 畫框的定位圖
3. Image 3 = 尖角特寫（綠底那張）
4. Image 4 = 柱子結構特寫

設定：GPT 2.6 Sunburst 或 NB 都可以，比例跟原圖一樣（1920×1064）。

## 提示詞（GPT / NB 通用）

```text
I am giving you four images. Each one has a single job.

Image 1 is the finished scene: a dark lava-fortress corridor. Keep it
exactly as it is, except for the two changes below.

Image 2 is Image 1 with red and cyan boxes. It only shows WHERE to
change things. Do not draw any boxes.

Image 3 is the design for the RED boxes: a spike ornament — one tall
upright pointed stone blade in the middle, with one crescent horn
curving UPWARD on each side, all rising from a projecting stone
collar ledge.

Image 4 is the design for the CYAN boxes: a heavy stone block that
projects out from the pillar, like a thick stepped bracket or collar,
with chipped edges, a lit top surface and a shadowed underside.

Change 1 — inside every red box: replace the single horn that sticks
out sideways with the spike ornament of Image 3. Each one follows the
perspective of its own pillar; near ones large, far ones small.

Change 2 — inside every cyan box: add the projecting stone block of
Image 4 onto the pillar, following that pillar's perspective, so the
pillar has a thicker, layered structure at that height.

Match the scene's lighting: warm orange light from the nearby
braziers, lamps and windows on the edges facing them, deep
purple-brown shadow elsewhere; dark charcoal stone like the rest of
the pillars.

Keep everything else exactly the same: the braziers and their fire,
the wall lamps, windows, ceiling, floor, the far opening and the
overall colours. Do not add anything outside the boxes. Do not crop
or zoom.
```

## 跑完以後

- 照之前的做法，**PS 用遮罩只取紅框、青框裡的部分**貼回定稿，其他地方保留原圖。
- 近處 4 個紅框（左右各 2 個大的）最重要。遠處的小框如果畫得糊，就從近處的尖角複製過去，用 Ctrl+T 縮小、變形貼上，比重跑快。
- 如果整張跑出來配件還是不對，就改成**只裁近景左右兩側**來局部重畫（像之前加燈那樣放大 3 倍再跑），像素多，配件會清楚很多。要的話我幫你裁。

- **青框的凸出石塊**：近處左右兩根大柱子下段長出的那塊厚石托，結構和光影都還不錯，可以用遮罩直接取下貼回定稿。

  4. 遠處的小框直接複製近處做好的尖角，縮小貼上。
- 原本那支往外伸的大角，用仿製印章取旁邊的牆面蓋掉。

```text
This image is a hand-painted stone pillar game asset. Change only the
glowing lamp slot in the middle of the shaft — it looks pasted on, too
sharp and too smooth compared with the rest of the pillar.

Repaint the lamp so it matches the pillar's painting exactly:
- Same painterly brushwork, same level of detail and the same texture
  density as the surrounding stone blocks — no crisp vector-like
  edges, no smooth airbrushed gradient.
- The pointed-arch frame is carved from the SAME charcoal stone as the
  pillar, with chipped edges, small cracks and hand-painted edge
  highlights, set into the stone blocks — the block joints run up to
  and around the frame.
- The slot is recessed into the stone, with a dark inner edge.
- The glow inside is molten orange-yellow, painted with visible
  strokes and slight colour variation, brightest in the centre.
- A soft warm glow spills onto the stone right around the frame,
  fading quickly.

Keep everything else exactly the same: the pillar's shape, stone
blocks, cap, spike ornament, moulding, carved emblem, base, colours,
lighting and the dark brown background. Same size and position of the
lamp. Do not crop or zoom.
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-11 09:29 UTC

這兩段是之前那根柱子用的流程：先轉 3/4，再加深。流程本身可以沿用，但**文字不能直接用**，原因有兩個：

1. **第 1 段需要「側面圖」**，新柱子目前只有正面圖。
2. **兩段都寫了舊柱子的配件**：刀刃、月牙角、尖刺環。新柱子沒有這些，它的是菱形雕花、高位火盆、火盆下的石台。照舊文字跑，AI 可能會把尖角加回去。

下面是改好的兩段，照順序跑。

## 第 1 步：正面轉 3/4

**上傳：** Image 1 = 新柱子正面圖（沒有側面圖就只傳這張）

```text
IMAGE 1 is the FRONT plate of a stone pillar. Render the SAME pillar —
identical geometry, proportions and details — as a single 3/4 view:
rotated about 35 degrees to show the front face and the right side
face, seen slightly from above so the top of the cap and the brazier
rim are visible. Square shaft, stone blocks wrapping around the
corner. The carved diamond panels, the horned brazier with its fire
and the carved emblem stay on the front face; the side face is plain
stone blocks. The stone shelf under the brazier, the moulding ring,
the cap and the stepped base wrap around both faces. Near-
orthographic, no wide-angle distortion. Same lighting and dark
exposure as IMAGE 1. Complete pillar, centred, nothing cropped, on one
flat solid chroma-green (#00B140) background.
```

## 第 2 步：加深

**上傳：** Image 1 = 第 1 步的結果

```text
IMAGE 1 is a 3/4 view of a stone pillar game asset on a green
background. This is a GEOMETRY-ONLY edit: make the pillar DEEPER
front-to-back. Change nothing else.

- The right-hand side face of the pillar becomes about TWICE as wide
  as the front face, so the pillar reads as a deep, solid block, not a
  thin slab. The cap, the stone shelf under the brazier, the moulding
  ring and the stepped base all extend deeper along that side face to
  match.
- The stone blocks on the side face continue in the same pattern,
  receding in the same 3/4 perspective.

LOCK — keep EXACTLY as in IMAGE 1:
- the lighting, light direction, colours, brightness, contrast and
  exposure — do NOT relight, do NOT brighten or warm up anything;
- the front face, its carved diamond panels, stone blocks and the
  carved emblem;
- the brazier, its horns, the fire and their positions;
- the camera angle, pillar height and the flat solid chroma-green
  (#00B140) background. Nothing cropped.
```

加深幅度改成「**側面約為正面 2 倍**」，跟上一根定案的深度一致。原文寫 40%，結果上次實際加深到大約 2 倍。這樣兩根柱子放在走道裡，比例才會一樣。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-11 09:38 UTC

可以。我理解你要的是**正方柱**：側面跟正面一樣寬，不要加深。這張的側面大約是正面的 2 倍，所以要把側面收窄一半。如果你指的是別的寬度（例如整根柱子太粗），跟我說。

## 做法 A：PS 直接壓窄（最穩，約 2 分鐘）

1. 用多邊形套索選取**右側面**，從頂蓋到柱腳整條，沿轉角線選。
2. Ctrl+T，左邊貼齊轉角不動，往左水平壓到 **約 50%**，讓側面寬度跟正面差不多。
3. 頂蓋、石台、線腳、柱腳的右端會跟著縮，比例就對了。
4. 右邊多出來的空位填回綠色。

光線、細節完全不會變，AI 也不會亂動火盆。

## 做法 B：AI 修改

**上傳：** Image 1 = 這張 3/4 結果


**設定：** 比例 9:16（768×1376）

```text
IMAGE 1 is the FRONT plate of a stone pillar — this is the design to
draw. IMAGE 2 shows an OLDER version of a similar pillar; use it ONLY
as a guide for the camera angle and the depth. Where the two differ,
follow IMAGE 1. Ignore IMAGE 2's green background.

Render the pillar of IMAGE 1 — identical design, heights and details —
as a single 3/4 view like IMAGE 2: rotated about 35 degrees to show

the top of the cap and the brazier rim are visible.

around the corner and run the full depth of the side face. The side

- the horned brazier bowl with its fire, at the same height as in
```

```text
as a guide for the camera angle and the depth. Where the two differ,
follow IMAGE 1. Ignore IMAGE 2's brazier and its green background.

around the corner and run the full depth of the side face. The side
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 05:47 UTC

━━━━━━ 20261112 ━━━━━━

我照 A5 石像鬼和火把的 de-lit 模板改寫，針對這組柱子加了三點：

1. **火盆裡的火要拿掉**（跟 A5 火把一樣寫 `NO flame`），image-to-3D 只建空碗，火之後在引擎用特效做。
2. **燈槽的發光要關掉**，畫成空的凹槽。發光屬於自發光（emissive），不是材質本色，留著的話 3D 模型會把它當成橘色的石頭。
3. **石頭上被火照出來的暖橘色也要洗掉**。這是最容易殘留的地方，火盆上方那幾塊石頭現在都是橘色。

## 使用前

這張是 8 個視圖拼在一起，請先**裁成 8 張單獨的圖**，一次跑一張。四根柱子的正面、3/4 各跑一次。

**設定：** 比例跟每張裁出來的圖一樣（直式 9:16），背景 `#808080`。

## 提示詞（換掉 `[VIEW]` 和 `[ORNAMENT]` 兩處）

```text
From the attached stone pillar, re-render it as one complete, isolated
object, centered and fully visible, in the SAME [VIEW] as the input,
on a flat neutral grey (#808080) seamless background.

KEEP the real material — the true dark charcoal stone colour, the
dark iron of the brazier and horns, every stone block, mortar gap,
chip, crack and carving (do NOT turn it into flat grey clay, do NOT
remove the stone texture). DE-LIT it: remove all the baked-in
lighting — no warm firelight, no orange glow on the stone, no
dramatic highlights, no bright side or dark side. Light it ONLY with
perfectly even, uniform, flat diffuse studio lighting so the whole
surface reads at even exposure. NO cast shadows, NO rim light, NO
light beams, NO scene elements.

[ORNAMENT]

Keep the exact shape, proportions, depth and every structural part:
the cap, spike ring, shelves, collars, hanging bracket, moulding ring,
carved panels, emblem and stepped base. High-resolution clean de-lit
albedo reference plate for image-to-3D.
```

**`[VIEW]` 換成：**
- 正面圖 → `true head-on FRONT orthographic view`
- 3/4 圖 → `3/4 view (same camera angle and depth)`

**`[ORNAMENT]` 換成：**
- 火盆柱 → `The brazier bowl is EMPTY: remove the flame, embers and sparks completely; show the inside of the bowl as plain dark iron.`
- 燈柱 → `The lamp slot is switched OFF: no glow, no orange light inside; show it as an empty recessed stone slot with a dark inner back wall, inside its carved stone frame.`

## 驗收（A5 的教訓）

- **變成灰黏土**：表示材質也被洗掉了。在 KEEP 那句前面加 `This is a lighting change only.`，再重跑。
- **火盆上方的石頭還是橘的**：補一句 `the stones above the brazier are the same charcoal colour as the rest of the pillar`。
- **有雜訊或殘留光斑**：A5 的經驗是**分段再跑一次**，拿結果當輸入，提示詞只寫「去除殘留暖色和亮斑，其他不變」。一次洗不乾淨很正常。
- 把正面和 3/4 的去光版**並排檢查**，兩張的明暗要一樣平均。如果一張偏亮、一張偏暗，image-to-3D 會把它們當成兩種不同的材質。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 05:50 UTC

可以，整張一起跑，8 個視圖的光線和色調會一致。提示詞改成針對**整張拼圖**，並要求每根柱子的位置不變，之後才能照原位置裁開。

**上傳：** 這張 8 格拼圖（整張，不用裁）
**設定：** 比例 **1:1**（跟原圖 2000×2000 一樣）

```text
The attached image is a model sheet of FOUR stone pillars in two rows:
each pillar is shown twice, as a flat front view and a 3/4 view —
eight views in total. Re-render the WHOLE sheet as a de-lit reference
sheet for image-to-3D.

LAYOUT LOCK: keep all eight views exactly where they are — same
positions, same sizes, same camera angles, same order. Do not add,
remove, merge, crop or rearrange any view. The whole canvas, including
the gap between the two rows, becomes one flat neutral grey (#808080)
seamless background.

KEEP the real material on every pillar — the true dark charcoal stone
colour, the dark iron of the braziers and horns, every stone block,
mortar gap, chip, crack and carving (do NOT turn them into flat grey
clay, do NOT remove the stone texture). DE-LIT everything: remove all
the baked-in lighting — no warm firelight, no orange glow on the
stone, no dramatic highlights, no bright side or dark side. Light all
eight views with the SAME perfectly even, uniform, flat diffuse studio
lighting, so every view reads at the same even exposure. NO cast
shadows, NO rim light, NO light beams.

BRAZIERS: every brazier bowl is EMPTY — remove all flames, embers and
sparks; show the inside of the bowl as plain dark iron.
LAMPS: every lamp slot is switched OFF — no glow, no orange light;
show each as an empty recessed stone slot with a dark inner back wall,
inside its carved stone frame.

Keep each pillar's exact shape, proportions, depth and every
structural part: caps, spike rings, shelves, collars, hanging
brackets, moulding rings, carved panels, emblems and stepped bases.
The front view and the 3/4 view of the same pillar must match each
other. High-resolution clean de-lit albedo reference sheet for
image-to-3D.
```

**要注意的取捨：** 一張圖裡有 8 根柱子，每根分到的像素比單張跑少很多。石塊、雕花這些細節可能會變糊，或被簡化。

**跑完檢查：**
- 8 個視圖的位置有沒有移動（跟原圖 50% 疊圖比對）
- 有沒有哪根的火或燈沒關掉
- 細節夠不夠用。不夠的話，放大時用 **Universal Upscaler 2×、creativity 最低**，只放大不重畫，整張一起放大，一致性也能保住。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 06:46 UTC

好，火盆是金屬，提示詞要多寫一段金屬的處理方式。照 A5 火把那次的經驗，金屬去光有兩個重點：

- **保留金屬本身**：顏色、質感、鍛打紋路都要留著，不能被洗成石頭或灰黏土。
- **去掉反光**：火光照出的高光、碗內壁映到的橘光都要拿掉。這些是光，不是材質。

下面是整張 8 格一起跑的完整提示詞，已經加入金屬的處理。

**上傳：** 這張 8 格拼圖（整張）
**設定：** 比例 **1:1**（2000×2000）

```text
The attached image is a model sheet of FOUR stone pillars in two rows:
each pillar is shown twice, as a flat front view and a 3/4 view —
eight views in total. Re-render the WHOLE sheet as a de-lit reference
sheet for image-to-3D.

LAYOUT LOCK: keep all eight views exactly where they are — same
positions, same sizes, same camera angles, same order. Do not add,
remove, merge, crop or rearrange any view. The whole canvas, including
the gap between the two rows, becomes one flat neutral grey (#808080)
seamless background.

KEEP the real materials (albedo) on every pillar — do NOT turn
anything into flat grey clay:
- STONE: the true dark charcoal stone colour, every stone block,
  mortar gap, chip, crack and carving.
- METAL: the brazier bowls and their crescent horns are dark forged
  METAL. Keep them clearly metallic — their true dark iron colour,
  hammered surface, rim and band details — and keep them visibly
  different from the stone. Do NOT turn them into stone or clay.

DE-LIT everything: remove all the baked-in lighting — no warm
firelight, no orange glow on the stone, no fire reflections or orange
highlights on the metal, no shiny specular hot-spots, no bright side
or dark side. Light all eight views with the SAME perfectly even,
uniform, flat diffuse studio lighting, so every view reads at the same
even exposure. NO cast shadows, NO rim light, NO light beams.

BRAZIERS: every brazier bowl is EMPTY — remove all flames, embers and
sparks; show the inside of the bowl as plain dark metal.
LAMPS: every lamp slot is switched OFF — no glow, no orange light;
show each as an empty recessed stone slot with a dark inner back wall,
inside its carved stone frame.

Keep each pillar's exact shape, proportions, depth and every
structural part: caps, spike rings, shelves, collars, hanging
brackets, moulding rings, carved panels, emblems and stepped bases.
The front view and the 3/4 view of the same pillar must match each
other. High-resolution clean de-lit albedo reference sheet for
image-to-3D.
```

**跑完檢查（金屬部分）：**
- 火盆跟石頭看得出是**兩種材質**：金屬偏冷、表面較平滑，石頭偏暖、粗糙。如果兩者變得一樣，補一句 `the metal bowls are slightly cooler and smoother than the stone`。
- 碗內壁沒有殘留橘色。
- 金屬上沒有白色亮點（去光後不應該有反光）。

**提醒：** image-to-3D 通常只拿 de-lit 圖當顏色參考，**金屬感不會自動變成金屬材質**。之後要在 Blender 或引擎把火盆那部分的 Metallic 設成 1，石頭維持 0（SOP AI PBR 裡有寫到 Metallic 的設定方式）。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 06:52 UTC

整體的 de-lit 是成功的：

- 8 個視圖位置沒動
- 石材保留了本色和紋理，沒有變成灰黏土
- 光線平均，燈槽也關掉了

剩下的問題只有火盆。

## 火盆為什麼會扭曲

原圖裡**碗口和碗內壁大多被火焰擋住**。要求「拿掉火」時，AI 得自己畫出被擋住的部分，就開始重新設計：碗變扁，碗口角度亂掉，3/4 視圖的碗看起來像飄在柱子外面。再加上 8 張擠在一張圖裡，每個火盆只有幾十個像素，形狀更容易跑掉。

所以這不是去光造成的，是**「去光」和「拿掉火」同時做**造成的。

## 修法：分兩步，先拿掉火，再去光

**第 1 步：只拿掉火（光線不動）**

上傳原本那張有火的 8 格圖：

```text
The attached image is a model sheet of four stone pillars, eight views
in total. Change only the braziers: remove the flames, embers, sparks
and the fire glow inside the four brazier bowls, so each bowl is
EMPTY.

BRAZIER SHAPE LOCK: keep every brazier exactly the same shape, size,
position and angle as now — the same round bowl, the same rim line,
the same two crescent horns on its sides, the same band and the same
point where it sits on its stone shelf. Only paint the inside of each
bowl, which was hidden by the fire, as plain dark forged metal
following the same rim. Do not redesign, flatten, tilt or move any
bowl.

Keep everything else exactly the same: the pillars, the lamps, the
lighting, the colours, the dark brown background and the layout of
all eight views. Do not crop or zoom.
```

**第 2 步：去光**

拿第 1 步的結果，跑上一則那段 de-lit 提示詞。BRAZIERS 那段改成下面這樣，因為火已經沒了，不需要再叫 AI 移除：

```text
BRAZIERS: the brazier bowls are already empty — keep their exact
shape, rim, horns and position; only remove the lighting on them.
```

這樣第 2 步只動光線，不用再重畫被火擋住的部分，形狀就不會再扭曲。

## 最穩的備案（PS）

如果第 1 步的火盆還是會變形，就**從現在這張 de-lit 結果裡挑一個形狀最好的火盆**，看起來左上那個正面火盆最接近。把它複製到其他 3 個位置：正面視圖直接貼，3/4 視圖用 Ctrl+T 的 Distort 貼合角度。四根柱子用的是同一個火盆，這樣反而保證一致。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 06:54 UTC

好，用原圖一次跑完。我把去光、金屬、拿掉火三件事合成一段。修正扭曲的關鍵是**火盆形狀鎖**：把每個碗的形狀、位置、角度都寫死，拿掉火時只准畫碗內，不准重新設計碗。

**上傳：** 這張原圖（8 格、有火）
**設定：** 比例 **1:1**（2000×2000）

```text
The attached image is a model sheet of FOUR stone pillars in two rows:
each pillar is shown twice, as a flat front view and a 3/4 view —
eight views in total. Re-render the WHOLE sheet as a de-lit reference
sheet for image-to-3D. This is a LIGHTING change plus fire removal —
NOT a redesign. Every shape stays exactly as it is.

LAYOUT LOCK: keep all eight views exactly where they are — same
positions, same sizes, same camera angles, same order. Do not add,
remove, merge, crop or rearrange any view. The whole canvas, including
the white gap between the two rows, becomes one flat neutral grey
(#808080) seamless background.

BRAZIER SHAPE LOCK (most important): there are four braziers — on the
two front views and the two 3/4 views of the first and third pillars.
Each brazier keeps EXACTLY its current outline, size, position and
angle: the same round metal bowl, the same rim line, the same two
crescent horns curving up on its sides, the same band, the same small
point on its front, and the same place where it sits on its stone
shelf or collar. In the front views the rim stays a straight
horizontal line; in the 3/4 views the rim stays the same oval seen at
the same angle, and the bowl stays attached to the pillar. Do NOT
flatten, enlarge, shrink, tilt, float or redesign any bowl or horn.

FIRE REMOVAL: remove all flames, embers, sparks and fire glow. Each
bowl becomes EMPTY: paint only its inner wall, which was hidden by
the fire, as plain dark metal that follows the existing rim exactly.
LAMPS: every lamp slot is switched OFF — no glow; show each as an
empty recessed stone slot with a dark inner back wall, inside its
carved stone frame, same shape and size.

KEEP the real materials (albedo) — do NOT turn anything into grey
clay:
- STONE: the true dark charcoal stone colour, every block, mortar
  gap, chip, crack and carving.
- METAL: the brazier bowls and their crescent horns are dark forged
  METAL — keep their true dark iron colour and hammered surface,
  clearly different from the stone.

DE-LIT everything: remove all baked-in lighting — no warm firelight,
no orange glow on the stone, no fire reflections or orange highlights
on the metal, no shiny hot-spots, no bright side or dark side. Light
all eight views with the SAME perfectly even, uniform, flat diffuse
studio lighting at the same exposure. NO cast shadows, NO rim light,
NO light beams.

Keep each pillar's exact shape, proportions, depth and every
structural part: caps, spike rings, shelves, collars, hanging
brackets, moulding rings, carved panels, emblems and stepped bases.
The front view and the 3/4 view of the same pillar must match. High-
resolution clean de-lit albedo reference sheet for image-to-3D.
```

**如果火盆還是扭曲：** 一次跑同時要做「去光 + 拿火」，模型仍可能重畫碗。這時改用上一則的分兩步（先拿火、再去光），或用 PS 把形狀最好的火盆複製到其他 3 個位置。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 07:01 UTC

這次的結果大致可以用，火盆的扭曲也比上一版好很多。

## 檢查結果

| 項目 | 結果 |
|---|---|
| 8 個視圖位置 | ✅ 沒有移動 |
| 光線 | ✅ 平均，沒有明暗面和陰影 |
| 火 | ✅ 都拿掉了 |
| 燈槽 | ✅ 關掉了，變成空的凹槽 |
| 火盆（3/4 視圖） | ✅ 碗口是橢圓、內部是空的、有貼著柱子，形狀合理 |
| 火盆（正面視圖） | ⚠️ 碗被畫成一整塊黑色，看不到碗口和金屬質感，但輪廓沒有變形 |
| 石材顏色 | ⚠️ 偏**紫紅褐**，原圖是炭灰偏暖，有一點漂色，局部還有偏綠的斑點 |
| 背景 | ⚠️ 兩排中間和最下面的**白色帶**沒有變成灰色 |
| 紋章 | ⚠️ 下段的紋章變淡，有點糊 |

## 建議用 PS 收尾，不要重跑

這張整體一致性很好，重跑反而可能又把火盆弄歪。剩下的都是 PS 幾分鐘就能修的：

1. **白色帶**：用 Magic Wand 選取白色區域，填入 `#808080`。
2. **石材偏紫紅**：加一層 Hue/Saturation，Reds 和 Magentas 的飽和度降 20–30。或把 Color Balance 的中間調稍微往青、綠推，拉回炭灰。用原圖的石頭當比對標準。
3. **偏綠斑點**：Hue/Saturation 選 Greens，降飽和。
4. **正面火盆太黑**：用 Curves 稍微提亮碗身，讓碗口的邊緣線看得出來，或從 3/4 視圖的碗吸色當參考。
5. **紋章**：要很清楚的話，從原圖把紋章那塊用 Luminosity 混合模式疊上去，只借明暗細節。

修完後**照原位置裁成 8 張**，正面和 3/4 成對餵給 image-to-3D。之前提過，火盆的 Metallic 要在 Blender 另外設定。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 07:04 UTC

三張裡**第 3 張最好**，建議直接用它。

| 項目 | 第 1 張 | 第 2 張 | 第 3 張 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 背景 | ⚠️ 最下面有一條淺灰帶 | ❌ 中間和下面都是白色帶 | ✅ 整張都是灰底 |
| 石材顏色 | ⚠️ 偏紫紅 | ⚠️ 偏紫紅 | ✅ 最接近炭灰偏暖 |
| 正面火盆 | ⚠️ 一塊黑色，看不到碗口 | ⚠️ 同左 | ✅ 看得到碗口和金屬箍，是金屬質感 |
| 3/4 火盆 | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ 碗口橢圓、貼著柱子 |
| 燈槽 | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |
| 光線一致 | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |

第 3 張剩下的小問題：

- 下段的**紋章變淡**了，要很清楚的話，從原圖用 Luminosity 混合模式把紋章那塊疊回來。
- 頂部的尖角偏紅褐色，看起來像石頭。如果尖角也是金屬，用 Hue/Saturation 降一點紅色飽和度，拉成跟火盆接近的深鐵色。

## 下一步

1. 照原位置把第 3 張**裁成 8 張**（每格約 500×1000）。
2. 每根柱子的**正面 + 3/4** 成對餵給 image-to-3D。
3. 模型出來後，在 Blender 把火盆（和尖角，如果是金屬）的 **Metallic 設成 1**，石頭維持 0。

這次也證明了一點：**整張一起跑，加上「拿掉火」的同時寫明形狀鎖**，比分開跑更能保持一致。這個做法之後可以寫進 B14 紀錄。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 07:47 UTC

我看了兩排的寶石：

| | 第 1 排（目標形狀） | 第 2 排（現在） |
|---|---|---|
| 正面火盆 | 細長的直立菱形，有深色邊框 | 比較短、偏橘的菱形 |
| 3/4 火盆 | 碗正面一塊直立的長方形寶石，看得到厚度 | 只剩碗緣一小片紅色 |

兩排用的是同一款火盆，寶石**直接從第 1 排複製過去**最準，形狀保證一樣。

## 做法 A：PS 複製（推薦，約 3 分鐘）

1. 用套索選取第 1 排**正面火盆的寶石**（連同深色邊框），Ctrl+J 複製成新圖層。
2. 移到第 2 排正面火盆的同樣位置，蓋掉原本的寶石，中心對齊碗的正中間。
3. 第 1 排 **3/4 火盆的寶石**用同樣方法，複製到第 2 排 3/4 火盆。兩個火盆的角度一樣，不需要變形。
4. 原本寶石如果有露出來的部分，用仿製印章取碗的金屬蓋掉。

## 做法 B：AI 修改

**上傳：** 這張圖

```text
The attached image is a de-lit model sheet of four stone pillars,
eight views. Change only the small red gems on the brazier bowls in
the BOTTOM row.

Make the gem on the bottom-row front-view brazier exactly the same as
the gem on the top-row front-view brazier: a tall, narrow upright
diamond with a dark metal outline, same size, centred on the bowl.
Make the gem on the bottom-row 3/4-view brazier exactly the same as
the gem on the top-row 3/4-view brazier: an upright rectangular gem
set into the front of the bowl, with visible thickness, same size and
angle.

Keep everything else exactly the same: the top-row gems, the bowls,
horns, pillars, the even flat lighting, the colours, the grey
background and the layout of all eight views. Do not crop or zoom.
```

另外，**最下面還有一條淺灰帶**，跟 #808080 不一樣，記得一起用 PS 填成 `#808080`。
