### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 07:54 UTC

我看了現在的寶石：

- **正面兩個**：形狀差不多，都是小菱形，但浮在碗的表面，沒有鑲嵌座。
- **3/4 兩個**：位置在**碗口上緣**，像一塊立起來的牌子凸出碗外，不像是碗身的一部分。第 1 排是菱形，第 2 排是長方形，兩個不一樣。

所以提示詞要把寶石的**位置、鑲法、角度**都寫清楚，四個火盆統一。

**上傳：** 這張圖
**設定：** 比例 1:1（2000×2000）

```text
The attached image is a de-lit model sheet of four stone pillars,
eight views. Change ONLY the small red gems on the four brazier bowls.
Everything else stays exactly the same.

Make all four gems identical and structurally correct:
- POSITION: on the front centre of the bowl's OUTER wall, just BELOW
  the rim — not on top of the rim, not sticking up above it.
- SHAPE: one small upright diamond-shaped red-orange gem, set in a
  thin dark metal bezel that is part of the bowl, flush with the
  bowl's curved surface — not a flat plate or slab standing on the
  rim.
- FRONT VIEWS (top-left and bottom-left): the gem is centred on the
  bowl, facing the viewer straight on.
- 3/4 VIEWS (second column, top and bottom): the gem sits at the same
  spot on the front of the bowl, turned with the bowl and foreshortened
  to follow its curve — the same gem as in the front views, seen at an
  angle. Both 3/4 gems must match each other.
- Same flat de-lit look as the rest: the gem keeps its red-orange
  colour but has NO glow, no highlights, no light on the metal around
  it.

Keep everything else exactly the same: the bowls, their rims and
horns, the pillars, the even flat lighting, the colours, the grey
background and the layout of all eight views. Do not crop or zoom.
```

**提醒：**
1. 寶石很小，AI 跑完如果還是不統一，就挑**一個最好的**，用 PS 複製到其他三個位置。3/4 的用 Ctrl+T 的 Distort 貼合碗的弧度。
2. 最下面那條**淺灰帶**還在，記得填成 `#808080`。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 08:22 UTC

圖 1 是**有火、有光的原版**，所以寶石換成圖 2 的樣式時，要**照圖 1 的光線來畫**：寶石要受火光照亮，不能是 de-lit 那種平光。

**上傳：** Image 1 = 原版（有火、咖啡底），Image 2 = de-lit 版（寶石設計），Image 3 = 紅框範圍圖
**設定：** 比例 1:1（2000×2000）

```text
Three images.
Image 1 is the BASE: a lit model sheet of four stone pillars, eight
views, with burning braziers on a dark brown background. Keep it
exactly as it is, except for the four brazier gems.
Image 2 is a de-lit version of the same sheet. Use it ONLY for the
DESIGN of the gems on the brazier bowls — not for its lighting, grey
background or colours.
Image 3 is Image 2 with four red boxes marking the four gems. Use it
ONLY to see which gems to change and where they sit. Do not draw any
boxes.

Change only this: replace the small red gem on each of the four
brazier bowls in Image 1 with the gem design from Image 2 — a
diamond-shaped red-orange gem set in a dark metal bezel on the front
centre of the bowl's outer wall, just below the rim, the same size and
position as in Image 2. In the two front views it faces the viewer
straight on; in the two 3/4 views it is turned with the bowl, as in
Image 2. All four gems match.

Paint the new gems in Image 1's lighting: the gem glows a little with
a warm inner light, catches the firelight from the flames above it,
and has a small bright highlight; the metal bezel picks up warm orange
reflections like the rest of the bowl.

Keep everything else in Image 1 exactly the same: the flames, the
bowls, horns, lamps, pillars, the lighting and colours, the dark
brown background and the layout of all eight views. Do not crop or
zoom.
```

**提醒：** 3/4 視圖的寶石有一部分會被火焰擋住。如果 AI 為了露出寶石而把火改小，就在 PS 用遮罩只取寶石那一小塊，貼回圖 1。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 08:27 UTC

寶石的樣式和打光都對了：有金屬鑲座，也有吃到火光。只是尺寸太大，現在寶石幾乎跟碗一樣高，還凸出碗口。

**要注意的是**：不能只叫 AI「小一點」。相對詞會讓尺寸來回擺盪，這是 B8 的教訓。要用**跟碗的比例**把尺寸寫死。

## 做法 A：PS 縮小（最快，約 2 分鐘，推薦）

樣式已經對了，只差大小：

1. 選取寶石連同金屬座，Ctrl+J 複製。
2. Ctrl+T 等比縮到 **約 50–55%**，讓寶石整個落在**碗口下方**，高度約為碗身的三分之一。
3. 縮完露出原本的大寶石邊緣，用仿製印章取旁邊的碗身金屬蓋掉。
4. 四個寶石用同樣比例縮，保持一致。

另外這張的**火變小了**。如果你比較想要原本的火，就把縮好的寶石貼回原圖（有大火那張），不要用這張當底。

## 做法 B：AI 修改（用這張結果當輸入）

```text
The attached image is a lit model sheet of four stone pillars, eight
views. Change ONLY the size of the four red gems on the brazier bowls.
Keep their design exactly: a diamond-shaped red-orange gem in a dark
metal bezel.

SIZE: each gem, including its metal bezel, must be only about ONE
THIRD of the height of the bowl it sits on, and narrower than one
third of the bowl's width. It sits fully BELOW the rim, centred on
the front of the bowl's outer wall — no part of it touches or rises
above the rim. In the 3/4 views, the same size, turned with the bowl.
All four gems the same size.

Fill the area the old larger gems covered with the same dark metal as
the rest of the bowl. Keep everything else exactly the same: the
flames, bowls, horns, lamps, pillars, lighting, colours, the dark
brown background and the layout. Do not crop or zoom.
```

如果還是偏大，把 `ONE THIRD` 改成 `ONE QUARTER`。

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-12 08:30 UTC

這次寶石的大小對了，現在要讓它**看起來是嵌在火盆裡**，而不是貼在表面。

**現在的問題：**
- **交界生硬**：金屬鑲座的邊緣太乾淨，跟碗身之間沒有過渡，也沒有接觸陰影。
- **光源不一致**：火在寶石正上方，但寶石的明暗是平均的。上緣沒有被火照亮，也沒有反光和內部透光，看起來像貼紙。

**上傳：** 這張結果
**設定：** 比例 1:1（2000×2000）

```text
The attached image is a lit model sheet of four stone pillars, eight
views, with burning braziers. Change ONLY the four red gems on the
brazier bowls and the metal right around them. Keep their size,
shape and position exactly as they are now.

INTEGRATION — make each gem truly set INTO the bowl:
- The dark metal bezel is forged as part of the bowl: it follows the
  bowl's curved surface, its outer edge blends into the bowl's metal
  with a slightly raised lip, and the bowl's texture and wear continue
  up to it — no clean pasted-on outline.
- A thin dark contact shadow where the bezel meets the bowl, deepest
  under its lower edge.
- The gem sits slightly recessed inside the bezel, with a small dark
  gap around it.

LIGHTING — the fire is directly above the gem:
- The gem's upper facets catch bright warm firelight; its lower
  facets are deeper red. Light passes through it: a soft glowing red-
  orange core and a tiny bright highlight near the top.
- The top edge of the bezel catches a warm orange rim of firelight;
  its lower edge is darker.
- A very faint red-orange glow from the gem tints the bowl metal just
  around it, fading quickly.
- In the 3/4 views the same lighting, following the bowl's angle.
All four gems match.

Keep everything else exactly the same: the flames, the bowls' shape,
horns, lamps, pillars, lighting, colours, the dark brown background
and the layout of all eight views. Do not crop or zoom.
```

**跑完：** 用 PS 遮罩只取四個寶石區域，貼回這張圖。寶石很小，AI 重畫整張時其他地方容易被微改，這樣最保險。
